# The Value Added Tax Law of 2000 - 95(I)/2000

Source: CyLaw (cylaw.org), the Cyprus legal database maintained by the Cyprus Bar Association. Original file: 95(I)-2000_Value-Added-Tax-Law_7cd4ab_EN.html

This is an English translation. Legislation of the Republic of Cyprus is enacted in Greek, and where the two differ the Greek text prevails. Reproduced here for reference only: it may not reflect later amendments, so the consolidated text in force must be checked against the official source before it is relied on. Do not quote a section as current law without that check.

## The Value Added Tax Law of 2000 (95(I)/2000)
For purposes of harmonization with Article 2 and Article 383 of the act of the European Community entitled -
Council Directive 2006/112/EC of 28her November 2006 on the common system of value added tax.
The House of Representatives votes as follows:

## Short title
1. This Law shall be referred to as the Value Added Tax Law of 2000.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Interpretation
2.—(1) In this Act, unless the text indicates otherwise—
"excise goods" means energy products, alcohol and alcoholic beverages and manufactured tobacco, as defined by applicable Community provisions, except for gas delivered through the natural gas system located in the territory of the Community or through any network connected to such a system;
"farmer" means a person who runs a business involving one or more specified activities, and has chosen to join the special status of farmers;
"immovable property" has the same meaning assigned to that term by each applicable Immovable Property (Possession, Registration and Valuation) Act;
"other Member State" means, subject to the provisions of Article 3B(1), any Member State other than the Republic;
"restructuring" means the direct or indirect disposition and transfer of immovable property, as well as the transfer of rights pursuant to a deed of sale filed with the Department of Land and Survey, between one or more borrowers and/or debtors and/or guarantors regarding the same credit facility or grant or debt and one or more lenders, which is carried out within eleven (11) years from the effective date of the Value Added Tax Value (Amendment) Law of 2015 and aims to reduce or repay credit facilities or grants or debts given to borrowers by one or more lenders:
Provided that, the disposal and transfer of immovable property, as well as the transfer of rights pursuant to a deed of sale filed with the Department of Cadastre and Land Surveying, may be carried out to a lender and/or to a non-"connected person", as this term is interpreted in accordance with the provisions of article 33 of the Income Tax Law:
It is further understood that, the disposition and transfer of real property, as well as the transfer of rights pursuant to a deed of sale filed with the Department of Cadastre and Land Surveying to a non-"connected person", excluding the lender, falls within this definition only in relation to the reduction or repayment of those credit facilities or loans or debts, arising in the context of a written agreement with the lender:
It is further provided that the above provisions apply in relation to non-performing loans (NPLs) on or before 31h December 2015, as this term is interpreted by the European Banking Authority;
"VAT representative." has the meaning assigned to this term by article 37;
"local authority" means a borough council, improvement board, zoning authority and community council;
"disabled person" means a person who has long-term physical, mental, intellectual or sensory disorders, which, in their interaction with various barriers, may prevent their full and effective participation in society on an equal basis with other persons and who is certified as a person with a severe or total disability by the Disability Assessment System or is approved as a recipient of public assistance as a disabled person based on the provisions of the Public Legal Aid and Services until certified as a person with severe or total long-term physical and/or mental and/or intellectual and/or sensory disability by the Disability Assessment System, if called for certification or a person certified by the Disability Assessment System as a person with a moderate mental disability;
"borrower" means a person who has contracted with the lender and includes a person whose credit facility was purchased in accordance with the provisions of the Sale of Credit Facilities and Related Matters Law and was either taken over by or transferred to a public body or company that is licensed and which holds or to which loans from a credit institution have been transferred, which are in arrears and/or have become overdue under the technical standards of the European Banking Authority, which define the criteria for classifying a grant as non-performing and/or regulated as established by the European Commission through Executive Regulation 2015/227:
Provided that any related person of the first borrower is also considered a borrower, as the term "related person" is interpreted in accordance with the provisions of article 33 of the Income Tax Law, provided that the disposal and transfer of immovable property, as well as the transfer of rights pursuant to a sale deed filed with the Department of Land and Surveying, is carried out for the benefit of the lender;
"lender" means a licensed credit institution, as defined in article 2 of the Law on Operations of Credit Institutions, and includes the credit and financial institutions operating in the Republic pursuant to the provisions of article 10A thereof, as well as the subsidiary companies of these institutions or a credit acquisition company, as defined in accordance with the provisions of article 2 of the Law on Buying and Selling Credit Facilities and for Related Law Matters, as well as its subsidiaries or a public body or company that is licensed and that holds or to which or to which have been transferred from a credit institution loans that are in arrears and/or have become overdue by virtue of the technical standards of the European Banking Authority, which determine the criteria for classifying a loan as non-performing and/or regulated as established by the European Commission through Executive Regulation 2015/227;
"Director" means the Director of the Department of Customs;
"special scheme for farmers" means the scheme provided for in Article 42;
"authorized person" means any person acting under the authority of the Commissioner;
"Commissioner" means the Commissioner of Value Added Tax, as defined in section 4(1);
"specified tax period" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 20(1);
"prescribed" means prescribed under the Regulations;
"normal value" means the total amount which a recipient, at the same stage of trading as the supply of the goods or the provision of the services, would have to pay, under conditions of free competition, to an independent supplier of a good or service provider in the Republic, in order to acquire that good or service in question at the time:
Provided that if no comparable delivery of goods or provision of services can be established, the "normal value" cannot be lower than the purchase price or similar goods or in the absence of a purchase price, the cost price, determined at the time of delivery or in the case of services, from the total cost entailed for the taxable person by the provision of the services.
"Regulations" means Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers pursuant to this Law;
"state authority" means a Ministry, Service or Department whose operating expenses are provided for in the budget of the Republic;
"Member State" means a Member State of the European Union;
"Community Customs Code" has the meaning assigned to that term by the Customs Code Law for the time being in force;
"officer" means an officer of the VAT Service. of the Customs Department;
"Register of the Special VAT Status of Farmers." has the meaning assigned to that term by subsection 4 of section 42;
"VAT Register." has the meaning assigned to that term by Article 6(3);
"body corporate" includes a company, partnership, association, club, association, association, organization or any other association of persons, whether or not incorporated under the provisions of any law or regulation;.
"Act of 1990" means the Value Added Tax Acts 1990 to (No. 2) of 2000;
"Directive (EU) 2020/285" means the act of the European Union entitled "Council Directive (EU) 2020/285 of 18 February 2020 amending Directive 2006/112/EC on the common system of value added tax as regards the special scheme for small enterprises and Regulation (EU) No 904/2010 as regards administrative cooperation and the exchange of information for the purpose of monitoring the correct implementation of the special regime for small businesses".
"prescribed activities" means the activities defined in the Schedule to the Value Added Tax (Special Status of Farmers) (Prescribed Activities) Regulations 2001;
"VAT credit balance." has the meaning assigned to that term by Article 20(3);
"supplier" means the person who delivers goods and/or provides services;
"transaction" has the meaning assigned to that term by Article 8;
"customs legislation" means the customs legislation as determined under the Customs Code Law for the time being in force and the Excise Law for the time being in force;
"invoice" means any document used as such;
"VAT invoice." has the meaning assigned to this term by Article 9(15);
"quarter" means a period of three months ending at the end of March, June, September or December;
"taxable person" means a person who is liable to tax by virtue of Article 6;
"VAT." means value added tax imposed in accordance with this Law or, where the text so requires, in accordance with the law of another Member State;
"taxable acquisition" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 12A(2);
"taxable transaction" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 7(2);
"tax warehouse" means such place in the Republic in the possession or under the control of the tax warehouse, not being an immovable or a place of retail trade, as such person notifies the Commissioner in writing and such place becomes a tax warehouse on the receipt by the Commissioner or an authorized person of such notice or on the date specified therein as the date from which it shall take effect, whichever is the later, and remains a tax warehouse so long as it is in possession or under the control of the tax warehouse or until he notifies the Commissioner or an authorized person in writing that he ceases to be a tax warehouse;
"tax" means value added tax;
"input tax" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 19;
"output tax" has the meaning assigned to that term by section 19; and
any reference to a specific Article, Part or
Annex is a reference to this Article, Part or Annex of this Law.
(2) Any reference in this Act to a registered person shall be construed in accordance with section 6(3).
(3) Subject to paragraph 2(2) of the Tenth Schedule in any provision contained in, or in force by virtue of, this Law "document", "copy" and "electronic computer" shall have the same meaning assigned to those terms by the Evidence Law.
(4) Whether, in relation to any provision of services, the provider or recipient of the service belongs to one country or another is determined in accordance with article 12.
(5) References in this Law to "the interior of the Republic" include the territorial waters of the Republic.
(6) Any reference in this Law to a VAT receipt. is a reference to legal proof specified in Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph 1A of the Tenth Schedule to the Law.
(7) Any reference in this Law to the Republic includes the Exclusive Economic Zone of the Republic.

## Business concept
3.—(1) In this Law "enterprise" means an economic activity carried on independently and in any place, regardless of the intended purpose or the results of such activity.
(2) Economic activities, in the sense of subsection (1) above, are all the activities of the producer, trader or service provider, including mining activities, agricultural activities, and the activities of the independent professions or those similar to them; the exploitation of tangible or intangible goods, for the purpose of generating income of a permanent nature, is also considered an economic activity.
(3) Employees and other natural persons, to the extent that they are connected to their employer by an employment contract or by any other legal relationship that creates ties of dependence regarding the working conditions, remuneration and liability of the employer, do not carry out economic activity "in an independent manner" within the meaning of subsection (1) above.
(4) Without affecting the generality of any provision of this Law, the following shall constitute carrying on a business:
(a) The provision by a club, association or other association of persons (in return for subscription or other consideration) of the facilities or benefits available to its members - and
(b) the entry of persons into any premises for consideration.
(5) If any person accepts any office in the course of or in furtherance of a trade, profession or profession, the services rendered by him as the holder of such office shall be deemed to be rendered in the course of or in furtherance of the trade, profession or profession.
(6) Anything done in connection with the winding up or intended winding up of a business shall be deemed to be done in the course of or in furtherance of that business.
(7) The disposal of the business as an operating economic unit or its assets or liabilities (regardless of whether it is done in connection with its reorganization or dissolution) is a transaction carried out within the framework of or for the promotion of the business.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Taxation under the laws of other member states, etc.
3A.-(1) Subject to the following provisions of this article –
(a) References in this Law in relation to another Member State, to the law of another Member State shall be interpreted to the extent that the law of that Member State applies for the purposes of any Community legislation relating to VAT.
(b) references in this Law to a person who is a taxable person in another Member State are references to that person who is taxable under the law of that Member State to the extent that it provides for purposes corresponding, in relation to that Member State, to the purposes of this Law, whether a person is a taxable person; and
(c) references in this Law to goods acquired by a person in another Member State are references to goods deemed to be so acquired under provisions of the law of that Member State which correspond, in relation to that Member State, to the provisions of this Law which provide that goods are deemed to be acquired in the Republic from another Member State.
(2) Without prejudice to subsection (4), Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the manner in which any of the following is proved or may be proved for any of the purposes of this Law, namely -
(a) the application of any provisions of the law of any other member state;
(b) that the provisions of any such law correspond or have a similar purpose, in relation to any Member State, to any provision of this Law or to its purpose.
(3) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide -
(a) for a person to be regarded for specified purposes of this Act as a taxable person in another Member State only when he has served such notice, and provided such other information, to the Commissioner as may be specified in the Regulations;
(b) of the form and manner in which any notice or information is to be served or provided under the Regulations and the particulars contained therein;
(c) for the proportion of any consideration for any act which is received for the purposes of this Law as representing a liability, under the law of another Member State, for VAT. to be proved by reference to such invoices or otherwise as may be specified in the Regulations.
(4) In any proceeding, whether civil or criminal, a certificate of the Commissioner -
(a) that person was or was not, at any date, a taxable person in another Member State; or
(b) that any VAT payable under the law of another Member State has or has not been paid,
is sufficient evidence of the fact which it certifies until the contrary is proved, and any document held to be a certificate under this subsection shall be deemed to be such a certificate until the contrary is proved.
(5) Without affecting the generality of any powers of the Commissioner under the provisions for the provision of relevant information, those powers, for the purposes of facilitating compliance with any Community obligations, are exercised in relation to matters relating to the imposition of VAT. by virtue of the law of another member state, as practiced in relation to matters related to any of the purposes of this Law.
(6) The reference in subsection (5) to provisions for relevant information is a reference to the provisions of article 49(6) and the Tenth Schedule concerning:
(a) Keeping accounts;
(b) submitting statements and other documents to the Registrar;
(c) the issuance, use and content of invoices;
(d) keeping and maintaining records;
(e) providing information and producing documents.

*(95(I)/2004)*

## Territories included in references to other member states, etc.
3B.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide whether the territory of the Community or the member states is considered for any of the purposes of this Law to include or not include such lands as may be specified in the Regulations.
(2) Without prejudice to the generality of the powers conferred under subsection (1) and section 13, Regulations made by the Cabinet may, for any of the purposes of this Act, provide for specified provisions of any Customs Acts to apply in relation to cases where any territory is deemed by virtue of subsection (1) not to be included in the territory of the Community, with such exceptions and adjustments as may be specified in the Regulations.

*(95(I)/2004)*

## Meaning of the term "new means of transport"
3C.-(1) In this Law "means of transport" in the term "new means of transport" has the meaning, subject to subsection (2), of any of the following, namely -
(a) any vessel exceeding 7.5 meters in length;
(b) any aircraft with a take-off weight greater than 1550 kilograms;
(c) any motor land vehicle which -
(i) has an engine displacement of more than 48 cubic centimeters; or
(ii) is constructed or adapted to be electrically driven with a power exceeding 7.2 kw.
(2) A ship, aircraft or motor land vehicle is not subject to subsection (1) unless it is intended for the carriage of persons or goods.
(3) For the purposes of this Law, a means of transport shall be deemed to be new in relation to any delivery or any acquisition from another Member State, at any time unless at that time -
(a) the period elapsed from the date of first placing in circulation is -
(i) in the case of a ship or aircraft, a period exceeding three months; and
(ii) in the case of a land vehicle, a period of more than six months; and
(b) it has, since the date of first placing in circulation, -
(i) in the case of a ship, travel for more than 100 hours;
(ii) in the case of an aircraft, travel in excess of 40 flight hours; and
(iii) in the case of a land vehicle, travels a distance of more than 6000 kilometres.
(4) The Council of Ministers may, by Decree published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, amend this article -
(a) by the addition or deletion of any ship, aircraft or vehicle of a description specified in the Ordinance from those for the time being specified in subsection (1);
(b) by amending, omitting or adding to the provisions of subsection (3) for determining whether a means of transport is new.
(5) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the determination of the circumstances in which a means of transport is considered for the purposes of this article to have been put into circulation for the first time.

*(95(I)/2004)*

## Definition of the term intra-Community distance sales of goods
3D. In this Law-
"intra-Community distance sales of goods" means deliveries of goods dispatched or transported by the supplier or by a person acting on his behalf, including where the supplier intervenes indirectly in the dispatch or transport of the goods, from a Member State other than that of the arrival of the shipment or transport to the acquiring customer, where the following conditions are met:
(a) The supply of goods is made to a taxable person or a non-taxable legal entity, whose intra-Community acquisitions of goods are not subject to VAT. under section 12A or for any other non-taxable person;
(b) the goods delivered are not new means of transport, nor are goods delivered after assembly or installation, with or without function testing, by or on behalf of the supplier.

*(181(I)/2021)*

## Meaning of the term distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries
3E. For the purposes of this Law and the relevant special regime created pursuant to it-
"distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries" means deliveries of goods sent or transported by the supplier or by a person acting on his behalf, including where the supplier intervenes indirectly in the dispatch or transport of the goods, from a third territory or third country, to a customer in a Member State when the following conditions are met:
(a) The supply of goods is carried out for a taxable person or a non-taxable legal entity, whose intra-Community acquisitions of goods are not subject to VAT. under section 12A or for any other non-taxable person;

*(181(I)/2021)*

## Commissioner of Value Added Tax, exercise of authority and performance of duties
4.—(1) The Council of Ministers appoints a civil servant who holds a permanent position in the Civil Service as Commissioner of Value Added Tax, who in this capacity is responsible for the faithful and effective implementation of the provisions of this Law.
(2) Any act or anything which the Superintendent is required or authorized to do by virtue of any law, may be performed by any officer or other person acting under the authority of the Superintendent; and any statement signed by the Superintendent certifying that a person is acting under his authority shall be admissible as evidence in any judicial proceeding.
(3) Any person, whether an official or not, who, based on the order or consent of the Commissioner, participates in the execution of any act or duty concerning a granted authority which by Law is required to be performed by an official, is considered to be a competent official for the execution of that act or duty.
(4) Any person deemed to be the competent officer by virtue of subsection (3) above has all the powers of an officer in relation to the act or duty performed or to be performed as referred to in that subsection.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Value added tax
5.—(1) Value added tax is imposed, in accordance with the provisions of this Law—
(a) On the delivery of goods and the provision of services within the Republic (including anything deemed to be such a transaction);
(b) on the acquisition of any goods within the Republic from other member states; and
(c) on the importation of goods from a place outside the Member States.
(2) The tax on any supply of goods or provision of services is an obligation of the person making the supply or supply and (subject to the provisions for account rendering and payment) becomes due at the time of the transaction.
(2A) When a taxable supply of goods or provision of services is made in the Republic, a taxable person who has a permanent establishment in the Republic, is considered as a non-established taxable person, when:
(a) makes a taxable supply of goods or services in the Republic;
(b) his establishment in the Republic does not interfere with the said delivery of goods or provision of services.
(3) The tax on any acquisition of goods from another member state is an obligation of the person who acquires the goods and subject to the provisions for rendering an account and payment, becomes due at the time of acquisition.
(4) The tax on the importation of goods from a place outside the Member States shall be levied and paid as if it were a customs duty.
(5) Regardless of the provisions of subsections (1) to (4), when goods are delivered based on the conditions of subsections (12) or (13) of article 10, the payment of the tax is an obligation of the taxable person established in the Republic.

## Taxable persons and registration
6.—(1) A taxable person is any person who is registered or required to be registered under this Act.
(2) For the purposes of registration, the provisions of the First Annex apply.
(3) Persons who are registered pursuant to the provisions of the First Annex, are registered in the VAT Register. kept by the Registrar for the purposes of this Law; and references in this Law to a registered person are references to a person registered under the provisions of the First Schedule.
(4) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the inclusion or correction of information and data contained in the VAT Register. in relation to the First Schedule under the provisions of which any person is registered.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Scope of VAT on taxable transactions
7.—(1) VAT is imposed on any delivery of goods or provision of services carried out within the Republic, when it is a taxable transaction carried out by a taxable person within the framework of or for the promotion of any business he carries out.
(2) A taxable transaction is any delivery of goods or provision of services that takes place within the Republic and is not exempt.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Transaction concept
8.—(1) The Second Schedule applies to determine what is, or what is deemed to be, a supply of goods or a supply of services.
(2) Subject to the provisions of the Second Schedule and any regulations issued under subsections (3) to (6) below-
(a) The term "transaction" in this Law includes all kinds of delivery or supply, but not anything that takes place without consideration;
(b) anything that is not a supply of goods, but is made for consideration (including the grant or assignment of any right made for consideration), is a supply of services.
(3) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide in relation to any act that—
(a) It is regarded as a delivery of goods, and not as a provision of services; or
(b) is considered a provision of services and not a delivery of goods; or
(c) is regarded as neither a supply of goods nor a supply of services; and without prejudice to the preceding provisions, these Regulations may provide that paragraph 4(4) of the Second Schedule shall not apply, in relation to goods of any description which are used or made available for use under specified circumstances, so as to make that act a supply of services under that paragraph and they may provide that paragraph 4A of the said Schedule shall not applies, in such circumstances as may be described in the Regulations, so that the transfer of the assets becomes a delivery of goods by virtue of the said paragraph.
(4) Without prejudice to subsection (3) above, Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide, in relation to services of any description specified in the Regulations, that-
(a) When a person carrying on a business carries out anything that is not a provision of services but would, if carried out for consideration, be a provision of services described in the Regulations; and
(b) when such other conditions, as may be specified in the Regulations, are satisfied,
such services are deemed for the purposes of this Law to be provided by him within the framework of or in furtherance of such business.
(5) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide that, subject to certain conditions specified in the Regulations, goods referred to in the Regulations which are possessed or produced by a person in the context of or in furtherance of a business carried on; and—
(a) Neither delivered to another person nor incorporated into other goods produced within or in furtherance of this business, but
(b) are used by him for the purposes of the business he carries out,
considered for the purposes of this Law to be delivered to that person for the purposes of his business and at the same time to be delivered by him within the framework of or to promote his business.
(6) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide, in relation to services described in the Regulations, that—
(a) When a person, within the framework of or for the promotion of the business he carries out, carries out anything for the purposes of that business which does not constitute a provision of services but would, if carried out for consideration, be a provision of services described in the Regulations; and
(b) where such other conditions, as may be specified in the Regulations, are satisfied, such services shall be deemed for the purposes of this Act to be supplied to that person for the purposes of that business and at the same time to be supplied by him in connection with or in furtherance thereof.
(7) For the purposes of this subsection, where goods are manufactured or produced from any other goods, those other goods are taken to be incorporated in the aforesaid goods.
(8) Regulations under subsection (4) or (6) above may provide for the method by which the value of any provision of services deemed to take place under the Regulations is calculated.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Time of the transaction
9.—(1) The provisions of this section shall apply subject to the provisions of sections 13B and 13C to determine the time at which a supply of goods or provision of services is deemed to take place for the purposes of charging VAT.
(2) Subject to the provisions of subsections (4) to (14) below, a supply of goods shall be deemed to take place—
(a) If the goods are to be transported, at the time of transportation;
(b) if the goods are not to be transported, at the time they are placed at the disposal of the person to whom they are delivered;
(c) if the goods (sent or received for approval, or "for the purpose of sale or return" or on other similar terms) are transferred before it is known whether delivery will take place, at the time it becomes certain that delivery has taken place or twelve months after transfer, whichever is the earlier.
(3) Subject to subsections (4) to (14) below, a supply of services is deemed to take place at the time the services are performed.
(4) If, before the time determined under subsection (2) or (3) above, the person carrying out the transaction issues a VAT invoice. in relation thereto or if, before the time specified under subsection 2(a) or (b) or (3) above, he receives payment in relation thereto, the transaction, to the extent covered by the invoice or payment, is deemed to take place at the time the invoice is issued or the payment is received.
(5) If, within fourteen days from the time determined under subsection (2) or (3) above, the person carrying out the transaction issues a VAT invoice. in relation thereto, then, unless he has notified the Registrar in writing that he chooses not to apply this subsection to his case, the transaction (to the extent that it is not deemed to take place during the time referred to in subsection (4) above) is deemed to take place at the time the invoice is issued.
(6) The Commissioner may, on the application of a taxable person, determine by order that subsection (5) above applies to supplies or supplies made by him (or to such supplies or supplies made by him as may be specified in the order) as if the period of fourteen days were replaced by such longer period as may be specified in the order.
(7) When any delivery of goods involves -
(a) the transport of the goods from the Republic; and
(b) the acquisition of said goods in another member state by the person who is liable for the payment of VAT. the acquisition in accordance with the corresponding provisions of Article 12A of the legislation of that Member State,
subsections (2), (4) to (6) and (10) to (12) do not apply and delivery for the purposes of this Law is deemed to take place on the earlier of the days specified in subsection (8).
(8) The days referred to in subsection (7) are -
(a) the fifteenth (15th) day of the month following that in which said transfer of goods takes place; and
(b) the day of issue, in relation to the delivery, of a VAT invoice. or invoice of such other description as may be specified in Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers.
(9) When a taxable person issues a document to himself, which –
(a) is referred to as a VAT invoice. in relation to the supply of goods or services to him by another taxable person; and
(b) is considered, in accordance with Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph 1 of the Tenth Schedule, as the VAT invoice. required by the Regulations to be issued by the supplier, subsections (5) and (6) above apply in relation to that transaction as if -
(i) the issuance of the document to himself was the issuance of a VAT invoice. by the supplier in connection with the transaction; and
(ii) any notice of election given or request made by it for the purposes of these provisions to have been given or made by the supplier;
(10) When a taxable person issues a document to himself which—
(a) It is carried as a VAT invoice. in relation to the supply of goods or provision of services to it by another taxable person; and
(b) is considered, in accordance with Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph 1 of the Tenth Schedule, as the VAT invoice. required by the Regulations to be issued by the supplier, subsections (5) and (6) above apply in relation to that transaction if—
(i) the issuance of the document to himself was the issuance of a VAT invoice. by the supplier in relation to the transaction- and
(ii) any notice of election given or request made by it for the purposes of these provisions had been given or made by the supplier.
(11) The Commissioner may, on the application of a taxable person, by order vary the time at which transactions carried on by him (or such transactions carried on by him as may be specified in the order) are deemed to take place, either—
(a) By providing that those transactions are deemed to take place—
(i) At a time or dates specified by, or with reference to, the occurrence of any event described in the order- or
(ii) at a time or dates determined by, or with reference to, the time when any event so described would in the ordinary course of events occur, so that in each case the resulting time or dates are earlier than those which would otherwise apply; or
(b) providing that, notwithstanding subsections (5) and (6) above, those transactions shall, to the extent that they are not deemed to take place at the time referred to in subsection (4) above, be deemed to take place—
(i) at the commencement of the relevant period of employment (as, where applicable, specified in the order and for the purposes thereof); or
(ii) at the end of the relevant employment period (as so determined).
(12) Where there is a delivery of goods by virtue of paragraph 4(1) of the Second Schedule only, delivery shall be deemed to take place when the goods are transferred or disposed of as referred to in that paragraph.
(13) Where there is a supply of services by virtue of paragraph 4(4) of the Second Schedule alone, the supply is deemed to take place when the goods are put to the use referred to in that paragraph.
(14) Regulations made by the Cabinet may provide that in relation to the time at which (notwithstanding subsections (2) to (8) and (11) to (13) above) a transaction is deemed to take place in cases where—
(a) Is a supply of goods or provision of services for consideration the whole or part of which is specified or payable periodically, or from year to year, or at the end of any period, or
(b) is a supply of goods for consideration the whole or part of which is determined at the time the goods are put to any purpose, or
(c) there is a delivery to which section 42A applies, or
(d) there is provision of services pursuant to paragraph 4(4) of the Second Schedule or Regulations issued pursuant to article 8(4); and for any such case referred to in this subsection the Regulations may provide that goods or services are deemed to be delivered or provided separately and consecutively at specified times or intervals.
(14A) The continuous deliveries of goods for a period of time longer than a calendar month, when their delivery entails:
(a) the transport of goods from the Republic, and
(b) the acquisition of said goods in another member state by the person who is liable for the payment of VAT. on the acquisition, in accordance with the corresponding provisions of Article 12A of the legislation of that Member State, or
(c) are transferred by a taxable person to another Member State for the needs of his business,
deemed to be completed at the end of each calendar month until delivery is completed.
(14B) The provision of services for which the tax is due by the recipient pursuant to Article 11 and which:
(a) are carried out continuously for a period of time longer than a year; and
(b) do not involve installment payments against an account or payments during this period,
they are considered to be completed at the end of each calendar year, as long as the provision of services continues.
(14C) When the delivery of goods entails:
(a) the transportation of goods from the Republic; and
(b) the acquisition of said goods in another member state by the person who is liable for the payment of VAT. on the acquisition, in accordance with the corresponding provisions of Article 12A of the legislation of that Member State; or
(c) the goods are transferred by a taxable person to another Member State for the needs of his business,
the transaction is deemed to take place on whichever of the following dates is the earliest:
(i) on the fifteenth day of the month following that in which the delivery of the goods takes place, or
(ii) the day the transaction is invoiced;
(14D) In a case where the delivery of goods concerns the distance sale of goods imported from third territories or third countries in accordance with the provisions of the special regime defined in articles 42F and 42H, the delivery is considered to take place at the time of acceptance of payment.
(15) In this Law "VAT invoice." means an invoice required under paragraph 1(1) of the Tenth Schedule, or would be so required if the person to whom the transaction is effected was the person to whom such an invoice should have been issued.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 141(I)/2007
 
 
 
 
 164(I)/2013
 
 
 
 
 181(I)/2021)*

## Place of delivery of goods
10.—(1) Subject to articles 12E and 13B, this article shall be applied to determine, for the purposes of this Law, whether goods are delivered or supplied within the Republic.
(2) Subject to the following provisions of this article, if the delivery of any goods does not entail their transport from or to the Republic, they are considered to be delivered within the Republic if they are in the Republic and otherwise they are considered to be delivered outside the Republic.
(3) Goods are deemed to be—
(a) That they are delivered within the Republic when their delivery also entails their installation or assembly in a place in the Republic to which they are transported; and
(b) that they are delivered outside the Republic when their delivery entails their installation or assembly in a place outside the Republic to which they are transported.
(4) Goods whose place of delivery is not determined by the provisions of this article and concern-
(a) intra-Community distance sales of goods, as their place of delivery is considered the place where the goods are located at the time of arrival of the shipment or transport to the acquiring customer.
(b) distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries into a Member State other than the Member State of arrival of the shipment or transport to the acquiring customer, the place of delivery shall be deemed to be the place where the goods are located at the time of arrival of the shipment or transport to the acquiring customer.
(c) distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries in the Member State of arrival of the shipment or the transfer of the goods to the acquiring customer, as the place of their delivery is considered the specific Member State, provided that the VAT. on the goods in question is declared under the special regime provided for in article 42H:
It is understood that, in the above cases, the deliveries subject to the profit margin plans are not included in accordance with the provisions of article 40.
(4A)(a) The provisions of paragraph (a) of subsection (4) do not apply in the event that the following conditions are met:
(i) The person delivering the goods is established or, in the absence of an establishment, has his place of residence or habitual residence in a single Member State;
(ii) goods are dispatched or transported to a Member State other than the Member State referred to in subparagraph (i); and
(iii) the sum of the total amount, without VAT, of the deliveries provided for in subparagraph (ii), together with the total amount, without VAT, of the services provided for in paragraph 18 of the Thirteenth Schedule, does not exceed, during the calendar year, ten thousand euros (€10,000) and does not exceed this amount during the previous calendar year.
(b) In the event that during a calendar year the limit provided for in subparagraph (iii) of paragraph (a) is exceeded, the provisions of paragraph (a) of subsection (4) shall apply from that point in time.
(c) In the event that the conditions of paragraph (a) are met and the goods at the time of departure of the shipment or transport are in the Republic, the person who delivers them has the right to choose the place of delivery of the goods, which in any case covers two (2) calendar years.
(5) Goods whose place of delivery is not determined by virtue of any of the previous provisions of this article and which do not constitute new means of transport are considered to be delivered outside the Republic when -
(a) the delivery involves the movement of the goods to another Member State by the supplier or pursuant to his instructions;
(b) the person making the supply is a taxable person in another Member State; and
(c) provisions of the law of that Member State corresponding, in relation to that Member State, to the provisions of subsection (4) make that person liable for the payment of VAT. on delivery;
but this subsection shall not apply in relation to any transaction where the obligation referred to in paragraph (c) is dependent on the exercise by any person of an option in the Republic of such option as is mentioned in paragraph 1(2) of Part II of the First Schedule unless that person has given notice, and not withdrawn, to the Commissioner that he wishes such transactions to be deemed to take place outside the Republic when they are transactions in respect of which the other requirements of this subsection are satisfied.
(6) Goods whose place of delivery is not determined by virtue of any of the previous provisions of this article, are considered to be delivered within the Republic when -
(a) the delivery of said goods implies that they are imported from a place outside the member states; and
(b) the supplier of the goods is the person by whom or under whose instructions the goods are so imported.
(7) Goods whose place of delivery is not determined by virtue of any of the previous provisions of this article but whose delivery entails their transport to or from the Republic are considered—
(a) That they are delivered within the Republic if their delivery entails their transfer from the Republic without also entailing their prior transfer to the Republic; and
(b) that they are delivered outside the Republic in any other case.
(8) For the purposes of the previous provisions of this article, when goods, within the framework of their movement from a place within the Republic to another place within the Republic, leave and re-enter the Republic, the movement is not considered as a transfer from or to the Republic.
(9) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide that notice for the purposes of subsection (5) shall not be served or withdrawn except in such circumstances, and in such form and in such manner as may be specified in the Regulations.
(10) [Deleted]
(11) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide, with respect to goods or services in general or with respect to specific goods specified in the Regulations, the differentiation of the provisions of this article which determine the place where a delivery of goods takes place.
(12) Irrespective of the previous provisions of this article, in the case of delivery to a taxable reseller of gas through the natural gas system or any other network connected to such a system, delivery of electricity or heat or cold through heating or cooling networks, the place of delivery shall be deemed to be the place where the taxable reseller has its seat of economic activity or its permanent establishment for which the goods are delivered or, in the absence of a seat or permanent establishment, the place where he has his permanent residence or habitual residence.
(13)(a) In the case of the delivery of gas through the natural gas system or through any other network connected to such a system or the delivery of electricity or heat or cold through heating or cooling networks, if such delivery is not covered by the provisions of subsection (12), the place of the transaction is the place of actual use and consumption of the goods by the customer.
(b) Where all or part of the gas or electricity or heat or cold is not actually consumed by the customer, such unconsumed goods shall be deemed to have been used and consumed at the place where the reseller, taxable person or permanent establishment for which the goods are delivered has its seat of economic activity and, in the absence of a seat of economic activity or fixed establishment, shall be deemed to have used and consumed the goods at the place where he has his permanent residence or habitual residence.
(14) For the purposes of subsections (12) and (13) of this article, "reseller taxable person" means a taxable person whose main activity in relation to gas, electricity, heat or cold purchases is the resale of the above products and whose self-consumption of these products is negligible.
(15)(a) Notwithstanding the provisions of subsections (1) to (14) of this article, where the goods are delivered successively and they are dispatched or transported from one Member State to another directly from the first supplier to the last recipient throughout the chain, the shipment or transport shall be attributed only to the delivery to the intermediate operator.
(b) Notwithstanding the provisions of paragraph (a), the shipment or transport is only attributable to the delivery of goods by the intermediary operator, when this intermediary operator has notified its supplier of the VAT registration number received from the Member State from which the goods are dispatched or transported.
(16) For the purposes of subsection (15), an “intermediate operator” means a supplier other than the first supplier in the chain who ships or transports the goods either himself or through a third party acting on his behalf.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
