(a) To what extent, when and to what extent is it considered that consideration has been written off in its accounting books as a bad debt;
(b) whether a payment is deemed to have been received as part of the consideration for a particular transaction;
(c) whether, and to what extent, a payment is taken to have been received as part of consideration that has been written off as a bad debt.
(8) The provisions which may be made in Regulations by virtue of subsection (6)(f) above may include rules governing particular cases, such as those relating to partial payments or mutual debts; and in particular such rules—may modify the manner in which the following amounts are calculated—
(a) The amount due referred to in subsection (2) above, and
(b) the amount of any refund where a refund has been allowed under this subsection.
(9) In order to determine the time when a transaction is considered to take place for the purposes of interpreting this article, the provisions of article 9 shall apply.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 38(I)/2008)*

## Exemption from VAT upon acquisition
27A.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for exemption from VAT. of the acquisition of any goods from another member state if, or to the extent that, the exemption from VAT would be granted under Regulations under Article 28 if the acquisition was an import from a place outside the Member States.
(2) Regulations pursuant to this article may provide for the exemption to be subject to such conditions as the Council of Ministers deems necessary or appropriate. They may -
(a) include conditions that prohibit or restrict the disposal or marketing of the goods in question;
(b) state the conditions under which, by virtue of any regulations in force under section 28 at the time of acquisition, relief would be granted under those regulations in the case of the importation of those goods.
(3) When exemption from VAT granted in accordance with regulations issued pursuant to this article subject to a condition which has been violated or not complied with, then the F.P.A. becomes payable at the time of the breach or, as the case may be, at the last time allowed for compliance.

*(95(I)/2004)*

## Exemption from VAT on the import of goods
28.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the granting of exemption from all or part of the VAT. of the levy on the importation of goods from outside the Member States, subject to any conditions (including conditions prohibiting or restricting the disposal or trading of the goods) that may be imposed on the basis of the Regulations, although to the extent that the Council of Ministers deems necessary or appropriate, taking into account any international agreement or agreements.
(2) In any case in which—
(a) It is intended that goods have been imported from a place outside the Member States by any person ("the original importer") exempt from VAT. by virtue of subsection (1) above, be transferred to another person ("the transferee"), and
(b) upon application by the transferee, the Commissioner decides that in this case this article will apply, this Law applies as if, on the date of the transfer of the goods (and at the place of transfer), the goods were exported by the original importer and imported by the transferee and, accordingly, where appropriate, anything provided by virtue of subsection (1) above applies in relation to VAT. the chargeable on the importation of the goods by the consignee.
(3) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the refund or exemption of all or part of the VAT. of the levy on the importation of any goods from a place outside the Member States for which the Commissioner is satisfied that they had previously been exported from the Republic or had been transported from any Member State.
(4) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the refund or exemption of all or part of the VAT. of the levy on the importation of any goods from a place outside the Member States if the Commissioner is satisfied that the goods have been re-exported or are to be re-exported or will otherwise be transferred from the Republic and considers it expedient to so act in all cases and having regard to-
(i) VAT enforceable on the delivery of similar goods within the Republic;
(ii) any VAT which may have become enforceable in another Member State in respect of the goods.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Import of goods by taxable persons
29. Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the delivery or transport of goods imported from a place outside the Member States by a taxable person within the framework of or for the promotion of any business carried on by him, subject to such conditions and restrictions as may be imposed by the Commissioner for the protection of public revenues, without the payment of VAT. of the chargeable on the import, and the rendering of the account for that VAT. together with VAT the obligee on the delivery of goods or the provision of services by that person or on the acquisition of goods by that person from other Member States.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Refund of VAT to those who conduct business abroad
30.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for a scheme for refunding, to persons to whom this article applies, VAT. on transactions to them within the Republic or on the importation of goods by them into the Republic which would be input tax if they were taxable persons from a place outside the member states.
(2) This section—
(a) Applies to persons carrying on business in another Member State, and
(b) also applies to persons carrying on business in other countries subject to reciprocity, but does not apply to persons carrying on business in the Republic.
(3) The refund referred to in subsection (1) shall be made in such cases only, and subject to such conditions, as the scheme specifies (being conditions specified in Regulations or imposed by the Commissioner either generally or in particular cases).
(a) [Deleted]
(b) [Deleted]
(c) [Deleted]

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 13(I)/2010)*

## Refunds in respect of new means of transport delivered to other Member States
30A.-(1) Subject to subsection (2), when a person who is not a taxable person makes a delivery of goods involving new means of transport as it involves the transfer of the goods to another member state, the Commissioner, following a relevant claim submitted in relation to the goods in question, returns to that person, as the case may be -
(a) the amount of any VAT on the delivery of that means of transport to that person; or
(b) the amount of any VAT paid by that person on the acquisition of this new means of transport from another Member State or on its import from a place outside the Member States.
(2) The amount of VAT refundable under this section does not exceed the amount that would have been payable on the supply comprising the transfer if it had been a taxable transaction by a taxable person and had not been subject to the zero rate.
(3) The Commissioner does not consider a VAT refund claim. under this section unless the claim -
(a) submitted at such time and in such form and in such manner;
(b) contains such information, and
(c) is accompanied by such documents, whether in evidence or otherwise,
as may be provided in Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers.

*(95(I)/2004)*

## Public Authorities
31.—(1) Subject to subsections (2) and (3) below, state authorities, local administration authorities and other public law organizations are not considered taxable persons for the acts or transactions they act in the exercise of public authority, even if they collect fees, contributions or other charges in relation to these acts or transactions.
(2) The Commissioner may consider any state authority, local authority or public law organization as a taxable person if the transactions of such authority or organization may create a risk of distortion of the conditions of competition.
(3) Notwithstanding any other provision of this Act or any other law, State authorities, local government authorities and other bodies governed by public law shall be deemed to be taxable persons in relation to the transactions described in the Ninth Schedule, unless they are negligible.
(4) The Council of Ministers may, by decree published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, amend the Ninth Annex by adding or removing from it any description or by amending any description specified in it from time to time.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Groups of companies
32.—(1) Where by virtue of the following provisions of this article any legal persons are deemed to be members of a group, any business carried on by a member of the group shall be deemed to be carried on by the representative member, and—
(a) Any delivery of goods or provision of services by one group member to another group member is disregarded; and
(b) any transaction which is a transaction to which paragraph (a) above does not apply and is a delivery of goods or a provision of services by or to a member of the group is deemed to be a transaction by or to the representing member- and
(c) any VAT paid or payable by a member of the group on the acquisition of goods from another Member State or on the importation of goods from a place outside the Member States shall be deemed to have been paid or payable by the representing member and the goods shall be deemed -
(i) in the case of goods acquired from another Member State, for the purposes of Article 49(6); and
(ii) in the case of goods imported from a place outside the Member States, for those purposes and for the purposes of Article 29;
that they were acquired or, as the case may be, imported by the representative member.
and all members of the complex are jointly and severally liable for the payment of any VAT. due from the representative member.
(2) Paragraph (a) of subsection (1) above shall not apply in relation to any supply of goods or provision of services from one member to another unless both the person making the transaction and the person to whom the transaction is made continue to be members of that group—
(a) In the case of delivery of goods which are to be transported in execution of the delivery, at any point in time subsequent to the transfer-
(b) in the case of any other delivery of goods, at any point after the time at which the goods were made available, in execution of the delivery, to the person to whom the delivery is made - or
(c) in the case of the provision of services, at any point in time after the performance of the services.
(3) Any regulations made under section 8(5) or (6) may provide that any goods or services which, although the members of the group were one person, would under that section be deemed to have been delivered or provided to and by that person shall be deemed to be delivered or provided to and by the representing member and they may provide for that purpose that a representing member shall be deemed to be a person of such description as may be determined under s. regulations and they may provide for that purpose that a representative member shall be deemed to be a person of such description as may be determined under the regulations.
(4)(a) Two or more legal entities may be considered group members if they are closely linked by financial, economic and organizational ties and each of them is established in the Republic.
(b) For the purposes of this Article -
(i) "financial links" exist when -
(k) one of the legal entities controls each of the others;
(l) one person, whether legal or natural, controls all of them;
(m) two or more natural persons carrying on business as a partnership control all of them.
(ii) "economic links" exist when the main economic activity of the members of the group is similar, or the economic activities of the members of the group are complementary or interdependent, or one member of the group carries out economic activities, which are wholly or substantially for the benefit of the other members; and
(iii) "organizational ties" exist when there is a common administrative structure of the complex, even partially.
(5) Where an application is made to the Registrar in respect of two or more legal persons capable of being regarded as members of a group, then, they are regarded as such from the commencement of a specified tax period, and one of them is the representative member, unless the Registrar rejects the application for the purposes of protecting the public revenue.
(6) When any legal persons are considered to be members of a complex and a relevant application is submitted to the Commissioner, then, from the beginning of a specified tax period—
(a) One more legal person candidate to be considered as such is included among the persons considered as members of the complex; or
(b) a legal entity ceases to be included among the persons considered as members of the complex; or
(c) the representative member is replaced by another member of the group; or
(d) legal entities are no longer considered members of a group, unless the Registrar rejects the application under subsection (7) below.
(7) If the Commissioner considers it necessary for the protection of public revenue, he may—
(a) Reject any application made for the purpose referred to in paragraph (a) or (c) of subsection (6) above; or
(b) refuse any application made for the purpose referred to in paragraph (b) or (d) of that subsection where he considers that it does not fall within subsection (8) below.
(8) Where a legal person is considered to be a member of a group controlled by another person and the Commissioner considers that it has ceased to be so controlled, he shall, by notice served on that person, terminate his inclusion in the group from the date he specifies in the notice.
(9) An application pursuant to this article in relation to any legal persons is submitted by one of those persons or by the person who controls them and is submitted at a time not less than 90 days before the effective date of its approval, or at such later time as the Registrar may allow.
(10) For the purposes of this article, a legal person is considered to control another legal person if it has legal authority to control the activities of that person or if it is the parent company of that person within the meaning of article 148 of the Companies Law; and a natural person or natural persons are considered to control a legal person if the natural person or natural persons in the event that it were a company, would be the parent company of the legal person within the meaning of the said Law.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 16(I)/2012)*

## A divisional business
33. (1) The registration pursuant to this Law of a legal entity that conducts business with separate departments may, if the legal entity so desires and the Registrar deems it appropriate, be in the name of those departments.
(2) The reference in this article to an enterprise includes references to any other activities in the context of or in furtherance of which any legal person acquires goods from another Member State.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Cooperatives
34.—(1) The registration pursuant to this Law of persons -
(a) carrying on business as a cooperative; or
(b) carrying out any other activities as a cooperative in the context of or in furtherance of which they acquire goods from another Member State,
may be made in the name of the house and any change in the partnership is not taken into account when determining for the purposes of this Law whether goods are delivered or services are provided to or by said persons or acquired by said persons from another member state.
(2) Subject to the provisions of article 38 of the Law on Uniform and Heterogeneous Companies and Trade Names (rights of persons doing business with a firm against persons appearing as members of the firm), until the date on which the Registrar is notified of a change in the partnership, a person who has ceased to be a member of the partnership is considered to continue to be a partner for the purposes of this Law and, in particular, for the purposes of any liability for the payment of VAT. upon delivery of goods or provision of services by the cooperative.
(3) Where a person ceases to be a member of a co-operative during a specified tax period (or is deemed to be so acting by virtue of subsection (2) above) any notice, whether tax return or otherwise, which is given to the co-operative and relates to that period, or to any matter arising out of that period or to any previous period during the whole or part of which he was a member of the co-operative is deemed to be given to it.
(4) Subject to the provisions of article 19 of the Law on Uniform and Heterogeneous Companies and Trade Names (notification to a partner who carries out operations of the cooperative constitutes notice to the house) any notification, whether a tax certificate or otherwise, addressed to a cooperative under the name under which it is registered pursuant to subsection (1) above and served in accordance with this Law deemed for the purposes of this Law to be served on the partnership and, accordingly, when subsection (3) above applies, also served on the former partner.
(5) Subsections (1) and (3) above do not affect the extent to which, by virtue of article 9 of the Law on Partnerships and Partnerships and Trade Names, a partner is responsible for paying VAT. of what is owed by the house; but when a person is a partner in a house during only part of the specified tax period, his liability for the payment of VAT on the supply of goods or services by the house during that tax period or on the acquisition during that period of any goods by the house from another member state is in such proportion of the house's liability as may be just.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Business carried on by associations of persons without legal personality, personal representatives, etc
35.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide by which persons anything required by or based on this Law to be carried out by a person carrying on a business is carried out when the business is carried on by a cooperative or by a club or by another association of persons without legal personality and the affairs of which are managed by the members or by a committee or committees of its members.
(2) The registration under this Law of any such club or other association of persons without legal personality may be in the name of the club or association of persons; and when determining whether goods are delivered or services rendered to or by such club or association of persons or whether goods are acquired by such club or association of persons from another Member State, no account shall be taken of any change in its members.
(3) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide that persons carrying on the business of a taxable person who has died or been declared bankrupt or whose property has been placed under escrow or who has been declared incapable of managing his property are considered for a limited time as taxable persons, and that the continuity of the application of this Law is ensured in cases where persons are considered as such.
(4) In relation to a company which is a taxable person, the reference in subsection (3) above to a taxable person who has been declared bankrupt or whose property has been placed in escrow or who has been declared insolvent, shall be construed as a reference to a company in respect of which a receiver or administrator has been appointed or which has been placed in liquidation.
(5) References in this article to an enterprise include references to any other activities in the context of or for the promotion of which any club or any association of persons or other person without legal personality acquires goods from another Member State.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Representatives, etc
36.—(1) When-
(a) goods are acquired from another Member State by a person who is not a taxable person and a taxable person acts in connection with the acquisition, and subsequently delivers the goods on behalf of the person from whom they were acquired; or
(b) goods are imported from a place outside the Member States by a taxable person who supplies them as an agent of a person who is not a taxable person,
then, if the taxable person acts in relation to the supply in his own name, the goods are deemed for the purposes of this Act to be acquired and delivered or, as the case may be, imported and delivered by the taxable person acting as agent.
(2) For the purposes of subsection (1) above, a person who does not reside in the Republic and whose registered office or principal place of business is outside the Republic may be considered not to be a taxable person if, as a result of subsection (1) above, he is not required to be registered in the VAT Register. pursuant to this Law.
(3) Where, in the case of any delivery of goods to which subsection (1) above does not apply, goods are delivered through an agent acting in his own name, the delivery shall be deemed to be both a delivery to the agent and a delivery by the agent.
(4) When services are provided through an agent acting in his own name the supply is considered both as a supply to the agent and as a supply by the agent.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## VAT representatives
37.—(1) Where any person—
(a) Is a taxable person for the purposes of this Law or, without being a taxable person, is a person who carries out taxable transactions or who acquires goods within the Republic from one or more other member states;
(b) does not have any business establishment or other permanent establishment in the Republic;
(ba) is established in a country or territory in respect of which the Commissioner considers that the conditions specified in subsection (1A) are satisfied; and
(c) in the case of a natural person, does not have his usual place of residence in the Republic,
then the Commissioner may instruct that person to appoint another person (referred to in this Act as a "VAT representative") to act on his behalf in relation to VAT.
(1A) The conditions referred to in subsection (1)(ba) are -
(a) the country or territory is not a Member State or part of a Member State; and
(b) there is no provision for mutual assistance between the Republic and the country or territory similar in scope to the assistance provided for between the Republic and each Member State by the mutual assistance provisions.
(1B) In subsection (1A) “mutual assistance provisions” means—
(a) the relevant provisions of the Mutual Assistance for the Collection of Claims relating to Duties and Taxes Act 2004, and
(b) the relevant provisions of Regulation (EC) no. 1798/2003 of the Council of 7 October 2003 on administrative cooperation in the field of value added tax and repealing Regulation (EEC) no. 218/92.
(2) With the agreement of the Commissioner, any person who has not been required to appoint a VAT representative. by virtue of subsection (1) above may appoint such an agent if he is a person in respect of whom the conditions specified in paragraphs (a), (b) and (c) of that subsection are satisfied.
(2A) In this Law "VAT representative" means a person appointed under subsection (1) or (2).
(3) When any person is appointed pursuant to this article as a VAT representative. of another ("the represented one"), then, subject to subsections (4) to (6) below, the VAT representative.—
(a) Is entitled to act on behalf of the representative for any of the purposes of this Law, any other subsequent law related to VAT. or of any secondary legislation enacted under this Law or any subsequent law;
(b) subject to such Regulations as the Council of Ministers may issue, ensure (acting on behalf of the represented when necessary) the compliance of the represented and the fulfillment of the obligations and responsibilities of the represented under this Law, any other such law and any secondary legislation; and
(c) is personally liable in respect of—
(i) any failure to ensure compliance or fulfillment of such obligations or responsibilities by the represented; and
(ii) anything carried out for purposes related to actions on the part of the represented, as if the obligations and responsibilities imposed on the represented were jointly and severally imposed on the VAT representative. and to the represented.
(4) VAT Representative has no responsibility under subsection (3) above to register himself under this Law, but Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may—
(a) To require the registration of the names of VAT representatives. against the names of those represented in any register kept for the purposes of this Law; and
(b) make it the duty of the VAT representative, for the purposes of registration, to notify the Registrar, within such period as may be specified, that his appointment has come into force or ceased to have effect.
(5) VAT Representative is not guilty of any offense under subsection (3) above unless and to the extent that—
(a) Has consented or acquiesced in the commission of the offense by the represented;
(b) the commission of the offense by the represented person is attributable to any negligence on the part of the VAT representative; or
(c) the offense consists of an infringement by the VAT representative. obligation which, by virtue of subsection (3), is imposed both on the VAT representative. as well as to the represented.
(6) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the manner and circumstances under which a person is appointed, or is deemed to have ceased to be, a VAT representative. of another person; and Regulations under this subsection may include provisions that the Council of Ministers deems appropriate for the purposes of subsection (4) above in relation to the entry or deletion of any information in any register.
(7) When a person fails to appoint a VAT representative. in accordance with any order under subsection (1) above, the Commissioner may require him to provide a guarantee, or further guarantee, which he deems appropriate for the payment of any VAT. which is or may be made payable by it.
(8) For the purposes of this Law, a person is not considered to have received an order to appoint a VAT representative, or to have been required to provide a guarantee, by virtue of subsection (7) above, unless the Commissioner-
(a) Has served notice of the order or demand on that person; or
(b) has taken all other steps it considers reasonable to bring the order or demand to its attention.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 141(I)/2007)*

## Transfer of an active financial unit
38.—(1) When a business carried on by a taxable person is transferred to another person as an active economic unit, then—
(a) For the purpose of determining whether the transferee is required to register under this Law, it is considered that it has carried on the business both before and after the transfer and transactions by the transferor are treated accordingly; and
(b) any records relating to the business which, by virtue of paragraph 5 of the Tenth Schedule, are required to be kept for any period after the transfer, shall be kept by the transferee instead of the transferor, unless the Commissioner, on application by the transferor, gives different orders.
(2) Without prejudice to subsection (1) above, Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the continuation of the application of this Law in cases where a business carried on by a taxable person is transferred to another person as an active economic unit and the transferee is registered in the VAT Registry. pursuant to this Law in place of the transferor.
(3) Regulations under subsection (2) above may, in particular, provide—
(a) That the obligations and duties under this Law (excluding Article 45) of the transferor become, to such an extent as the Regulations may determine, obligations and duties of the transferee; and
(b) that any right of each of them to a refund or deduction in respect of VAT. is satisfied by refunding the tax or granting the deduction to the other; but no such provision referred to in paragraph (a) or (b) of this subsection shall apply in relation to any transferor and transferee unless an application has been made by them under the Regulations.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Real property
39.—(1) With reference to transactions concerning immovable property, the provisions of the Eighth Schedule shall apply.
(2) The Council of Ministers may, by decree published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, amend the Eighth Annex.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Profit margin plans
40.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide in relation to any description of deliveries to which this article applies that may be specified in the Regulations, that a taxable person is entitled to choose, when making deliveries of that description, to be subject to VAT. on the profit margin on deliveries, rather than on their value.
(2) This section applies to the following deliveries, namely—
(a) Deliveries of works of art, antiquities or collection items;
(b) deliveries of motor vehicles-
(c) deliveries of used goods; and
(d) any delivery of goods through a person acting as an agent but in his own name, in relation to the delivery.
(3) An election for the purposes of the Regulations under this article may be exercised, and may be withdrawn, in such manner as may be required by the Regulations.
(4) Subject to subsection (7) below, the margin of profit on a delivery to which this article applies is deemed, for the purposes of the Regulations issued pursuant to this article, to be equal to the amount (if any) by which the price at which the person making the delivery delivers the goods in question exceeds the price at which he acquired them.
(5) For the purposes of this section, the price at which a person has acquired any goods and the price at which he supplies them shall each be calculated in accordance with the provisions of the Regulations issued under this section; and such Regulations may, in particular, provide for the extent to which any VAT levied on the delivery or importation of any goods shall be deemed to be included in the price at which those goods are acquired or delivered.
(6) Regulations under this section may provide that consideration for any services rendered in connection with the supply of goods by a person acting as an agent, but in his own name, in relation to the supply of goods, shall be deemed for the purposes of any such regulations to be an amount taken into account when calculating the profit margin on the supply of goods instead of being separately subject to VAT. the value of the services rendered.
(7) Regulations under this section may provide that the total profit margin on all goods of a specified description supplied by a person in any specified tax period shall be calculated—
(a) Adding together all the prices at which such person acquired goods of that description in that period together with any amount carried over in that period under paragraph (d) below;
(b) aggregating all the prices at which he delivers goods of that description in that period;
(c) considering the total profit margin on goods delivered in that period to be equal to the amount (if any) by which the sum calculated in accordance with paragraph (b) above exceeds the sum calculated in accordance with paragraph (a) above; and
(d) treating any amount by which, for that period, the sum calculated under paragraph (a) above exceeds the sum calculated under paragraph (b) above as an amount carried forward to the next specified tax period to be included, for the period to which it is carried forward, in any sum calculated under paragraph (a) above.
(8) Regulations made under this section may—
(a) To provide differently for different cases; and
(b) provide that the Commissioner may give general or special orders in relation to any matter to which the Regulations relate.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Organizers of tourism businesses
41.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for a different way of rendering the tax in relation to tour operators or in relation to transactions which may be determined by or on the basis of
(2) Without affecting the generality of subsection (1) above, Regulations issued pursuant to this article may provide-
(a) That two or more deliveries of goods or provision of services by a tour operator are considered as a single provision of services;
(b) that the value of the single supply is ascertained, in a manner determined by or under the Regulations, by reference to the difference between the amounts paid or payable to and the amounts paid or payable by the tour operator;
(c) that any legal person is exempted from the application of article 32;
(d) the time at which the supply is deemed to take place.
(3) In this article "tour operator" includes a travel agency acting as a representative and any other person who provides for the benefit of passengers services of the kind normally provided by tour operators and travel agencies.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Special status of farmers
42.—(1)(a) Notwithstanding any other provision of this Law, persons carrying on a business which concerns one or more specified activities and who are not required to register on the basis of sub-paragraph (1) of paragraph (1) of Part I of the First Schedule, shall not be deemed to be taxable persons for the acts or transactions acting in the context of such activities but may be subject to the special status of farmers and are entitled to submit an application for a VAT refund, equal to their input tax, as if they were taxable persons in the Republic:
Provided that, a person who carries out a specified activity and is not obliged to register in accordance with subsection (1) of paragraph (1) of Part I of the First Schedule, may become subject to the tax person, if he requests in writing from the Commissioner to voluntarily register in the VAT Register. in connection with such activity.
(b) Notwithstanding any other provision of this Law, persons who carry on a business which concerns one or more specified activities and are required to register under the provisions of subsection (1) of paragraph (1) of Part I of the First Schedule shall be deemed to be taxable persons for the acts or transactions carried out in the context of such activities, when the provisions of Part I of the First Schedule are met, but they may, if they choose, to submit an application for a VAT refund, equal to their input tax and to be subject to the special status of farmers, instead of being registered in the VAT Register.
(2) Persons who carry out a designated activity and deliver their products from their own store, as well as those who carry out exports to a place outside the member states or deliveries of their products to another member state, are considered to be carrying out two economic activities and to deliver products from the designated activity to the commercial activity.
(3) In this article "defined activities" means activities carried out by a farmer that may be defined in Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers.
(4) Persons who fall under the special status of farmers based on subsection (1) are registered in the Register of the Special Status of Farmers of the VAT. which is kept by the Registrar for the purposes of this Law:
Provided that, persons who are registered in the Register of the Special Status of Farmers of the VAT they cannot be registered in the VAT Register. and vice versa.
(5)(a) The VAT return is acted upon by the Registrar by paying to the person exercising a specified activity an amount, which is obtained by applying a flat rate of 5% to the value of the deliveries of agricultural products and the provision of agricultural services by the person exercising a specified activity, which result from the exercise of one or more specified activities. The Commissioner shall notify the Commission of the rate or rates before they are implemented:
It is understood that, as the value of the delivery of agricultural products or the provision of agricultural services, that obtained from the invoices issued by the person carrying out a specified activity is taken.
(b) in case of delivery of agricultural products by intermediaries who are taxable persons, on behalf of persons carrying out specified activities, the above value is received without tax and commission.
(6) The provisions of subsection (5) do not apply in case of delivery of agricultural products or provision of agricultural services to other persons who have joined the special status of farmers or to non-taxable persons.
(7)(a) The provisions of articles 6, 19, 20, 21 and 43 do not apply to persons who have joined the special status of farmers.
(b) Regardless of the provisions of paragraph (a), the provisions of articles 46 and 47 shall apply.
(8) The special status of farmers does not include persons who carry out an economic activity concerning one or more defined activities and who:
(a) Carry out the prescribed activities in the form of any legal entity;
(b) sell agricultural products of their production, after processing that may give them the character of industrial or craft products;
(c) simultaneously carry out other economic activity for which they are considered taxable persons.
(9)(a) Persons who have joined the special status of farmers may transfer from the special status of farmers to the status of taxable persons and vice versa, after giving notice to the Commissioner, in the first thirty days of each year.
(b) The reclassification is effective from the beginning of the year in which the notice is submitted and cannot be revoked before the expiration of five years, if the person is reclassified as a taxable person.
(c) If during the year one of the conditions of subsection (8) is met, the persons who have joined the special status of farmers, from the following year, are deprived of the right to a VAT refund. provided by the provisions of subsections (1) and (5).
(10) In the case of transfer from the special status of farmers to the status of taxable persons, the transferred persons are entitled to deduct, in their first tax return, the tax with which they have been burdened:
(a) The stocks of raw materials of the designated activity. To this end, the transferred person must, on the date of the transfer, carry out an accurate inventory of these stocks;
(b) the fixed assets of the designated activity, in the part of the tax that corresponds to the remaining years of a five-year period, from the date of their acquisition.
(11) In case of transfer from the status of taxable persons to the special status of farmers, the transferred persons are obliged to pay in their final tax return, the tax with which they have been charged and deducted:
(12) The Council of Ministers may issue Regulations that determine:
(a) The type and manner in which an application for inclusion or cancellation of inclusion or stay in the special status of farmers or notice of transfer from the special status of farmers to the status of taxable persons and vice versa is submitted;
(b) the form and manner in which the VAT refund application is made and the evidence required to support it;
(c) the terms and conditions for VAT refund, to the persons included in the special status of farmers;
(d) the obligation of a registered taxable person to prepare a document for himself, which is subject to the position of the invoice referred to in paragraph (a) of subsection (5), regarding the delivery of goods or the provision of services to that person, by a person who has joined the special status of farmers and concerning specified activities;
(e) the regulation of any issues regarding the implementation and operation of the special status of farmers.

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 27(I)/2003
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Special regime of investment gold
42A-(1) Regardless of the provisions of the Seventh Schedule of the basic law, it is an exempt act:
(a) The delivery, acquisition from another Member State and importation of investment gold including investment gold for which there are certificates by type or by genus or which is the subject of a transaction between accounts in gold, including, in particular, gold loans and swaps, with the right of ownership or claim of investment gold, as well as for investment gold transactions with futures and simple forward contracts (forward contracts) which cause a change of ownership or claim regarding investment gold.
(b) the services of intermediaries (brokers), who act in the name and on behalf of another person, when they mediate the delivery of investment gold to their principal.
(2) For the purposes of this article <investment gold> means:
(a) Gold in bar or plate form, of a weight accepted by the bullion markets, of a fineness equal to or greater than 995 millimetres, whether or not represented by securities. Bars and plates weighing up to one (1) gram do not fall within the scope of this article.
(b)(i) Gold coins which are of a fineness of at least nine hundred (900) millimeters, have been struck after the year 1800, are or have been legal tender in their country of origin, and are sold under normal conditions at a price which does not exceed the normal value of the gold contained in the coins by more than eighty percent (80%).
(ii) Coins that meet the conditions provided for in subparagraph (i) are included in a list published in Issue C of the Official Journal of the European Union before 1 December of each year and valid for the following calendar year; coins included in the published list shall be deemed to meet the criteria of subparagraph (i) throughout the year for which the list is published.
(iii) A currency may be exempted even if it is not listed, if it meets in itself the criteria for exemption set out in sub-paragraph (i):
Provided that, these coins are not considered to be sold for numismatic collecting purposes.
(3) The Comptroller informs the Commission before July 1 of each year about the coins that meet the criteria mentioned in subsection (2) and are traded in the Republic.
(4) (a) Taxable persons who produce investment gold or convert any kind of gold into investment gold as defined in subsection (2) above, may choose to make the deliveries of investment gold to other taxable persons taxable under conditions that may be determined by notification of the VAT Commissioner, which is published in the Official Gazette of the Republic.
(b) Persons who mediate for the realization of the transactions referred to in paragraph (a) of subsection 1 and act in the name and on behalf of the principal, have the right to choose to make their services taxable to the extent that they relate to these operations and if respectively the principal has chosen to make the operations for which they mediate taxable.
(5) The ΄Eforos informs the Commission about the way of implementation of the options referred to in subsection (4).
(6) (a) Taxable persons who carry out the exempted transactions referred to in paragraph (a) of subsection (1) may not deduct input tax except:
(i) input tax with which they are charged in the context of the delivery of investment gold to them by persons who have exercised the right of election to tax the deliveries of investment gold in accordance with subsection 4,
(ii) input tax with which they are charged in the context of the delivery or acquisition from another Member State or the import of another type of gold from investment gold, which was subsequently converted into investment gold by the taxable person himself or by another person on his behalf,
(iii) input tax with which they are charged in the context of receiving services that consist of changing the shape, weight or purity of gold, including investment gold.
(b) Taxable persons who produce investment gold or convert any type of gold into investment gold, have the right to deduct the input tax due or paid by them regarding deliveries or acquisitions from another member state or imports of goods or services related to the production or conversion of the gold in question as if the subsequent delivery of the gold that is excluded based on the provisions of this article was taxable.
(c) Taxable persons who carry out operations described in subsection 1, in addition to the obligations provided for by other provisions of this Law and specifically those referred to in the Tenth Schedule and the Regulations issued based on the Law, must additionally, for all sales of investment gold, keep records and issue invoices, copies of which they must keep for seven years and of which they must must include, among other things, the full name and address of the buyer, the quantity and type of investment gold. Especially in the case of sales of gold coins that meet the criteria mentioned in paragraph (b) of subsection (2), the taxable persons must submit to the VAT Commissioner. a list of the gold coins which were the subject of their transactions during any calendar year or part of the year as the case may be, showing the last price attained before the 1st day of April of the following year.
(d) The Council of Ministers may by Regulations regulate matters concerning the implementation of the provisions of this article.

*(27(I)/2003
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 38(I)/2008
 
 
 
 
 133(I)/2012)*

## Issuance of Regulations in relation to the special regime for services provided by taxable persons not established within the Community
42B.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the creation of a special regime for services provided by taxable persons not established within the Community.
(2) Regulations issued pursuant to this article may-
(a) allow a taxable person not established within the Community to choose to use the special scheme for services supplied to a non-taxable person who is established or has his place of residence or habitual residence in a Member State;
(b) determine the manner of starting or ceasing to use the special regime for services provided by taxable persons not established within the Community;
(c) determine the obligations of the taxable person not established within the Community for rendering an account and paying VAT. for services it provides, regarding periods, in time and in a manner as specified in the Regulations;
(d) allow the Commissioner to transmit information relating to the return of VAT, in accordance with the provisions of paragraph (c), to another Member State in which the non-taxable recipient of the services is established or has his place of residence or habitual residence; and
(e) regulate any issues regarding the application and operation of the special regime for services provided by taxable persons not established within the Community.

*(95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 160(I)/2014
 
 
 
 
 181(I)/2021)*

## Summary Table
42C.-(1) In this article -
"registered in another Member State" means registered in accordance with the measures adopted by the competent authority in another Member State for the purposes of the common VAT system. and “registered” shall be construed accordingly;
"Table" means the Recapitulation table, which a taxable person is required to submit in accordance with subsections (2) and (3);
"quarter" [Deleted]
(2) Every taxable person who during a month has delivered goods and/or provided services to a person who is or was registered in another member state submits to the Commissioner, in relation to the month in question, no later than the fifteenth day after the end of the month to which it relates, a summary table which includes the following:
(a) The persons registered in other Member States to whom he delivered goods in accordance with the conditions provided for in article 10;
(b) persons registered in other Member States to whom it has supplied goods acquired in the context of intra-Community acquisitions of goods in accordance with Article 12E; and
(c) the persons registered in other Member States to whom he provided services in accordance with Article 10A except for the services which are exempt from the payment of VAT in the Member State in which the transaction is taxable, for which the recipient of the services is liable to pay the tax in accordance with the provisions of Article 11.
(2A) In addition to the information referred to in subsection (2), each taxable person shall submit information relating to the VAT registration number of the taxable persons for whom the goods which are dispatched or transferred under the arrangements for stocks at the disposal of a particular acquirer, in accordance with the conditions set out in the Value Added Tax (Movements of Goods for Stock in Disposition of a Specified Acquirer) Regulations 2020, as well as in relation to any change in the information it has already submitted.
(3) Subject to the provisions of subsection (2) and (4), a taxable person who did not make any supplies of goods and/or services to registered persons in other Member States in any month but was required to submit a Schedule in respect of the immediately preceding month, must submit a Schedule stating that he has not made any such deliveries in respect of the month in question, unless -
(a) the Commissioner is satisfied that the taxable person has stopped making supplies of goods and/or services to persons registered in other Member States or
(b) the taxable person notifies the Commissioner in writing that he stops making supplies of goods and/or services to persons registered in other Member States.
and the obligation to submit a Table ceases to exist from the month following the month in which the taxable person made the last delivery of goods and/or provision of services to a person registered in another member state.
(4) In the event that a taxable person finds that any Table he submitted in accordance with the provisions of subsection (2) of this article contains deficiencies or inaccuracies in the information of paragraphs (a) to (c) of the said subsection, then he must, within one (1) month from the end of the month to which that Table relates, submit a corrective form determined by Notification of the Treasurer which is published in the Official Gazette of the Republic.
(5) The Table is submitted using electronic means, which are determined by a Notification of the Treasurer published in the Official Gazette of the Republic:
It is provided that, for the purposes of this Law and any civil or criminal procedure, the code number, which is granted by the Registrar to the person who submits the Table by electronic means, is considered to be subject to the handwritten signature that the Table would bear if it were not submitted by electronic means and the person who submits the Table is considered to have knowledge of its content.
(6) The Commissioner may issue Notices published in the Official Gazette of the Republic for the better implementation of the provisions of this article and generally for the determination of any issue which, by virtue of this article, needs or is susceptible to determination.

*(95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 13(I)/2010
 
 
 
 
 4(I)/2011
 
 
 
 
 79(I)/2020)*

## Special status of city taxis
42D.- (1) For the purposes of this article:
"city taxi" means a motor vehicle for public use in respect of which a city taxi road use license has been granted pursuant to the provisions of the currently applicable Road Transport Regulation Law;
"Commission" means the Commission of the European Communities;
"relevant activity" means the carrying on of a business consisting of the provision of services for the transport of passengers and their accompanying luggage by public taxi;
(2) Irrespective of the provisions of the First Annex of the basic law and especially those concerning the obligation to register, persons who exclusively carry out the relevant activity are obliged to join the special regime of urban taxis and must pay to the Commissioner an annual fixed amount of VAT. for each urban taxi road license held by:
It is understood that a person carrying out the relevant activity can choose to become a taxable person if he requests in writing from the Registrar to be registered in the VAT Register. in relation to such activity:
It is further provided that, a person who carries out a relevant activity and is included in the special regime of urban taxis or has chosen to become a taxable person, can incorporate the VAT in the taxi meter of the urban taxi that is obliged to operate. with the current VAT rate as determined or derived from relevant provisions of the basic law.
(3) A person who falls under the special status of urban taxis based on subsection (2) above shall be registered in the Register of the special status of urban taxis which is maintained by the Registrar for the purposes of this Law:
It is understood that a person who is registered in the Register of the special status of city taxis cannot also be registered in the VAT Register. and vice versa.
(4)(a) The annual flat-rate amount of VAT, which must be paid to the Commissioner by a person subject to the special city taxi regime, is determined at £300 (three hundred Cyprus pounds) for each city taxi road use license that he holds.
(b) This flat-rate VAT amount is determined taking into account macroeconomic and statistical data of the economic activity sector of urban taxis, the inputs of the activity and the corresponding VAT. which burdens them and the outputs of the activity and the corresponding VAT. which corresponds.
(c) The Commissioner notifies the Commission of the annual flat rate VAT amount. which applies to the special regime of urban taxis and consults the Commission regarding it.
(d) In the event that a city taxi road use permit is granted during the year, the flat rate VAT amount is calculated and paid for the twelfths corresponding to the months of use of the city taxi:
It is understood that the first day of the month in which the permit is granted is taken as the date of granting the road use permit.
(e) When transferring the urban taxi road use permit, the person transferring as well as the person to whom the urban taxi road use permit is transferred must simultaneously and jointly notify the Registrar accordingly:
Provided that the first day of the month in which the transfer takes place will be taken into account as the transfer date. The transferor must pay to the Registrar as many as twelfths of the annual flat-rate amount of VAT. correspond to the months until the date of transfer and the transferee must join the special urban taxi regime and pay to the Registrar as many as twelfths of the annual flat-rate amount of VAT. correspond to the months from the date of transfer.
(5)(a) The provisions of articles 6, 19, 20, 21 and 45 of the basic law do not apply to the persons subject to the special VAT regime. city ​​taxis.
(b) Regardless of the provisions of paragraph (a), the provisions of articles 46, 47 and 48 of the basic law shall apply.
(6) Persons who carry out relevant activities and who -
(a) Concurrently carry on any other activities and carry out any other taxable transactions, or
(b) they have been granted or transferred and hold more than two (2) city taxi road use permits:
It is understood that such persons are considered as taxable persons and are registered in the VAT Register. in relation to all their activities.
(7)(a) A person subject to the special status of urban taxis may be transferred to the status of taxable persons and vice versa, after submitting a notice to the Commissioner in the first thirty days of each year.
(b) The reclassification is effective from the beginning of the year in which the notification is submitted and may not be revoked before the expiration of five years from the date of the reclassification to the taxable person status.
(c) If during the year one of the conditions of subsection (6) is met, the inclusion of the person in the special city taxi regime is canceled on the last day of the month in which the condition is met and from the first day of the following month this person is required to be registered in the VAT Register. of the status of taxable persons.
(8) In the event of a transfer from the special status of city taxis to the status of persons subject to personal tax, the transferring persons are entitled to deduct in their first tax return the input tax with which the fixed assets of the relevant activity have been burdened, according to the part of the tax corresponding to the remaining years of a five-year period from the date of their acquisition.
(9) In case of transfer from the status of persons subject to tax to the special status of urban taxis, the transferred persons are obliged to pay in their final tax return the input tax, which they have incurred and deducted for the fixed assets of the relevant activity, for the part of the tax that corresponds to the remaining years of a five-year period from the date of its recovery.
(10)(a) The annual fixed amount of VAT constitutes VAT owed to the Republic by the persons subject to the special VAT regime. civil taxis and regarding its recovery and collection, the provisions of paragraph (4) of the Tenth Appendix of the basic law shall apply.
(b) Any person who delays or fails to submit to the Commissioner the forms prescribed by Regulations issued under paragraph (a) of subsection (11) of the special urban taxi scheme within the prescribed time limits shall be liable to a fine of fifty pounds (£50) for each month that the failure continues unless the form is an application for cancellation of inclusion in the special urban taxi scheme, therefore, this person is subject to a one-time fine of eighty-five euros (€85.00).
(c) Any person who fails to submit to the Registrar the form specified by Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph (b) of subsection (11) for the payment of the flat-rate amount of VAT. of the special city taxi regime within the specified time frames, is subject to a monetary charge of thirty pounds (£30), unless the form is an application for the cancellation of the inclusion in the special city taxi regime, in which case, this person is subject to a one-off monetary charge of eighty five euros (€ 85.00).
(d) Any person who fails to pay the Commissioner the annual lump sum of VAT. within the time frames and manner determined by Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph (b) of subsection (11), is subject to an additional tax equal to ten percent (10%) of the flat-rate amount of VAT due.
(e) Every person subject to additional tax by virtue of paragraph (d) above shall pay to the Commissioner interest at the rate of nine percent (9%) per annum on the flat rate amount of VAT payable. (including the additional tax) from the day the amount in question became due.
(11) The Council of Ministers may issue Regulations that determine:
(a) The type and manner in which the application is submitted for the mandatory inclusion or cancellation of inclusion or stay in the special urban taxi regime, or notification of transfer from the special urban taxi regime to the regime of persons subject to tax and vice versa or for the simultaneous and joint notification of the transfer of a city taxi road use license by the person transferring as well as by the person to whom the urban road use license is transferred taxi;
(b) the type and manner in which the annual flat rate VAT is paid. from the persons subject to the special status of urban taxis;
(c) the obligation of a person subject to the special urban taxi regime to issue a VAT receipt;
(d) the regulation of any issues regarding the application and operation of the special VAT regime. city ​​taxis.

*(100(I)/2005
 
 
 
 
 37(I)/2008)*

## Cash accounting regime
42E.(1) A taxable person has the right to apply the special regime provided for in the provisions of this article, according to which the date:
(a) return for output tax, is the date of collection of the consideration for the transaction, whether it is monetary or non-monetary; and
(b) deduction for input tax, is the date of payment of the consideration for the transaction, whether it is monetary or non-monetary.
(2) A taxable person has the right to apply the regime provided for by the provisions of this article, from the beginning of any specified tax period, if:
(a) the value of the transactions carried out in the previous twelve (12) months, ending at the beginning of the specified tax period, have not exceeded twenty-five thousand euros (€25,000),
(b) has submitted to the Registrar all the tax declarations up to and including the date on which the special regime provided for by the provisions of this article begins to apply, within the period provided for by the provisions of this Law and has paid any amount of tax that he has declared in them as payable,
(c) has paid any amount of tax assessed under the provisions of this Law and
(d) has not been convicted of any crime, pursuant to article 46 of this Law.
(3) The special regime does not apply to the following transactions:
(a) transactions for which VAT assigned by the recipient,
(b) goods imported from a third country or acquired from another member state, pursuant to article 12A of the Law,
(c) hire purchase contracts, sales contracts with conditions, sales contracts on credit:
Provided that the taxable person is obliged to inform the VAT Commissioner in writing. for the implementation of the provisions of this article.
(4) A taxable person has the right to cease applying the special regime provided for by the provisions of this article, from the end of any specified tax period.
(5) The Commissioner may terminate the right of a taxable person to apply the special regime provided for in this article, when the taxable person does not comply with any of the obligations provided for in the provisions of this Law or for the purposes of protecting public revenues.
(6) The Commissioner may, by notification published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, determine further details for the implementation of this article.

*(164(I)/2013)*

## Issuance of regulations in relation to the special regime for taxable persons carrying out distance sales of goods, deliveries of goods within the Republic carried out via electronic interfaces and for the provision of services by taxable persons within the Republic, but not established in the Member State of consumption
42F.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the creation of a special regime for taxable persons who carry out distance sales of goods, deliveries of goods within the Republic carried out through electronic interfaces that facilitate such deliveries and for the provision of services by taxable persons within the Republic, but not established in the Member State of consumption.
(a) allow a taxable person belonging to the Republic to choose to use the special scheme for distance sales of goods, deliveries of goods within the Republic made through electronic interfaces that facilitate such deliveries and for the provision of services by taxable persons established within the Republic, but not established in the Member State of consumption;
(b) determine how to start or stop using the relevant special scheme;
(c) determine the obligations of the established taxable person for rendering an account and paying VAT. for the specific special regime, with respect to periods, in time and in a manner as specified in the Regulations; and
(d) regulate any issues regarding the implementation and operation of the relevant special regime.
(3) For the purposes of the relevant special regime, when a taxable person facilitates through the use of an electronic interface, such as a market, platform, web portal or similar means, the delivery of goods in the Republic from a taxable person not established within the Republic to a non-taxable person, the said taxable person is deemed to have received and delivered the goods.

*(160(I)/2014
 
 
 
 
 181(I)/2021)*

## Fiscal handling of vouchers
42G. The tax treatment of vouchers is governed by the Fourteenth Schedule.

*(70(I)/2019)*

## Special regime for distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries
42H.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide for the creation of a special regime for distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries.
(a) allow a taxable person belonging to the Republic to elect to use the special scheme for distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries;
(b) determine how to start or stop using the special regime for distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries;
(c) determine the obligations of the established taxable person for rendering an account and paying VAT. for the transactions of this special regime, in relation to periods, at a time and in a manner as specified in the Regulations; and
(d) regulate any issues regarding the implementation and operation of this special regime.
(3) For the purposes of the relevant special regime, when a taxable person facilitates through the use of an electronic interface, such as a market, platform, web portal or similar means, the distance sales of goods imported from third territories or third countries in parcels with an internal value not exceeding one hundred and fifty euros (€150), the taxable person is deemed to have received and delivered these goods.

*(181(I)/2021)*

## General provisions related to the management and collection of VAT
43. Any matters concerning the collection and administration of VAT. as well as the fulfillment of any obligations deriving from this Law are regulated based on the provisions of the Tenth Annex respecting the provisions of article 3A(6).

*(95(I)/2000
 
 
 
 
 95(I)/2004)*

## Netting of receivables
44.—(1) Any interest payable by the Commissioner to a person on an amount due to him pursuant to the provisions of this Law shall be deemed to be a deductible amount pursuant to section 20(3).
(2) Subsection (1) above shall be disregarded for the purpose of determining a person's right to interest or the amount of interest to which he is entitled.
(3) Subject to subsection (1) above, in the event that—
(a) Amount owed by the Commissioner to any person pursuant to the provisions of this Law, and (b) such person is liable to pay an amount as VAT, additional tax, pecuniary charge or interest, then the amount referred to in paragraph (a) above shall be offset against the amount referred to in paragraph (b) above and the obligations of the Commissioner and the person concerned shall be fulfilled accordingly, to the extent of the offset.
(4) Subsection (3) above shall not apply where the amount referred to in paragraph (a) of that subsection becomes due to such person—
(a) At a time when his property is transferred to a bankruptcy trustee; or
(b) at a time when his property goes to a liquidator; or
(c) at a time when a receiver or administrator of his property had been appointed; or
(d) at a time when a compromise or scheme of arrangement had been approved.

*(95(I)/2000)*

## Violations
45.-(1) Any person who fails to comply with the provisions of paragraphs 5, 6, or 7 of Part I, paragraph 3 of Part II, paragraph 3 of Part III and paragraph 2(1) of Part V of the First Schedule is subject to a fine of eighty-five euros (€85) for each month that the omission lasts.
(2) If on the last day on which the taxable person has an obligation in accordance with the Regulations issued pursuant to this Law to submit a tax return for a specified tax period, the Commissioner has not received the tax return, then the person in question is subject to a monetary charge of one hundred euros (€100):
It is understood that the above financial burden is not imposed on a taxable person who had an obligation to submit the tax return that expired on 10h April 2020, 27h April 2020, 10h May 2020, 27h May 2020, 10h June 2020, 27h June 2020 and 27h July 2020.
(3) If
(a) On the last day on which a taxable person has an obligation in accordance with Regulations issued pursuant to this Law to submit a tax return for a specified tax period, the Commissioner has received that tax return but has not received, either himself or his authorized representative, the amount of VAT. shown in the tax return as payable by that person in respect of that tax period; or
(b) on the last day on which a taxable person has an obligation in accordance with Regulations issued pursuant to this Law to submit a tax return for a specified tax period the Commissioner has neither received that tax return nor has he received, either himself or his authorized representative, the amount of VAT due, but accepts a tax return submitted after the aforementioned day and in which an amount is shown VAT as payable by that person in respect of that tax period; or
(c) the Registrar certifies the VAT amount. as due from any person,
then this person is subject to an additional tax equal to ten percent (10%) of the VAT due:
Provided that, in the case of a tax return submitted pursuant to the regulations issued under section 20 for the specified tax periods ending on 30 June 2013, 31 July 2013, 31 August 2013 and 30 September 2013, an additional tax of five percent (5%) shall be imposed, provided that the taxable person submits the tax return for the these periods and pay the amount of VAT due until December 10, 2013:
It is further provided that, in the case of a tax return submitted on the basis of the Regulations issued pursuant to article 20 for the specified tax periods ending on 29h February 2020, on 31h March 2020 and the 30or April 2020, no additional tax and interest is imposed, provided that the taxable person submits the tax return on time, in accordance with the provisions of the Law, for these periods and may pay the amount of VAT due in six (6) equal installments, each payable no later than the tenth of each calendar month, in the period starting from the month of November 2020 and expires on the 10thh April 2021:
Provided further that, the above reservation does not apply to taxable persons with economic activity codes belonging to the following categories: 35111 (Electricity producers), 36001 (Collection and distribution of water (for water supply), 47111 (grocery stores and supermarkets mainly for food), 47112 (kiosks-mini markets), 47191 (Retail trade variety of goods in department stores where food, beverages and tobacco do not predominate), 47211 (Retail trade of fruits and vegetables - fruit stores), 47221 (Retail trade of meat and meat products including poultry), 47231 (Retail trade of fish and seafood - fish, molluscs), 47241 (Retail trade of bread and other goods bakery), 47242 (Retail trade of confectionery), 47301 (Retail sale of fuel), 47411 (Retail trade of computers, peripheral equipment and software incl. video games), 47611 (Retail trade of books), 47621 (Retail trade of newspapers and stationery), 47651 (Retail trade games of any kind except video games), 47731 (Pharmacies), 61101 (Cyprus Telecommunications Authority), 61201 (Internet services), 61301 (Satellite telecommunications services), 61901 (other telecommunications services except ATIK):
It is further provided that, in the case of a tax return submitted on the basis of the Regulations issued pursuant to article 20 for the specified tax period ending on 31h December 2020 and the specified tax period ending on 31h January 2021, no additional tax and financial burden is imposed, provided that the taxable person whose economic activity does not fall into the categories listed below, submits the tax return for this period within the specified period and pays the amount of VAT due in three equal installments payable on 10h April 2021, 10h May 2021 and 10h June 2021:
Supermarkets and grocery stores with the exception of the provinces of Famagusta and Paphos, mines and quarries, forestry and logging, satellite and other telecommunications services, electricity It is further provided that, in the case of a tax return submitted on the basis of the Regulations issued pursuant to the provisions of article 20 for the specified tax period ending on 31h December 2021 or VAT amount due on 10h February 2022, no additional tax and financial burden is imposed, provided that the taxable person submits the tax return for this period within the specified period and pays the amount of VAT due in three (3) equal installments, payable by 10h February 2022, on the 10h March 2022 and the 10h April 2022.
(4) Any person who is subject to additional tax by virtue of subsection (3) above shall pay to the Commissioner interest at the rate of nine percent per annum on the amount of VAT payable. from the day the amount in question became due.
(5) Any person who fails to comply with—
(a) The provisions of paragraphs 11 and 12 of Part I, paragraph 5 of Part II, paragraph 5 of Part III and paragraph 3(1) of Part V of the First Schedule; or
(b) any regulations made under section 37 which require a VAT representative, for the purposes of registration, to notify the Commissioner that his appointment has come into force or has ceased to have effect; or
(c) paragraphs 5(1) or 6 of the Tenth Schedule; or
(d) a requirement of Regulations made under paragraph 1(2B) of the Tenth Schedule; or
(e) section 13A in relation to the type of condition imposed by the Commissioner under subsection (1) of that section. or
(f) the obligations he has and are provided for in any Regulations or instructions of the Commissioner pursuant to this Law,
subject to a charge of fifty pounds (£50).
(6) Any person who fails to comply with the provisions of paragraph 5(3) of the Tenth Schedule shall be liable to a fine of two hundred pounds (£200).
(7) Any person who issues one or more invoices showing an amount that is VAT. or that it includes an amount attributable to VAT. without being—
(a) A person registered pursuant to this Law; or
(b) a legal entity considered for the purposes of article 32 as a member of a group; or
(c) a person who is considered a taxable person pursuant to Regulations issued pursuant to Article 35(3);
(d) a person authorized to issue an invoice under Regulations made under paragraph 1(8) of the Tenth Schedule; or
(e) a person acting on behalf of the Republic,
(7A) Person:
(a) to whom one or more transactions are or are to be carried out delivers to the supplier a certificate for the purposes of section 13B(2)(d) or 13C(1)(c) and the certificate is inaccurate; or
(b) who makes, or is about to make, an acquisition of goods from another Member State prepares a certificate for the purposes of section 13B(1)(d) and the certificate is inaccurate,
(7B) Any person who fails to comply with the provisions of article 11 or 11A or 11B or 11C or 11D or 11E or 12A is subject to a monetary charge of two hundred euros (€200) per tax period, which cannot exceed a total of four thousand euros (€4,000). [S.S. : see Footnote no. 15 of Law 122(I)/2020].
(7C) A person who fails to comply with the obligation to submit detailed records of beneficiaries and payments in relation to the payment services he provides, in accordance with the provisions of paragraph (1) of Regulation 3 of the Value Added Tax (Obligations of Payment Service Providers) Regulations 2024 issued pursuant to paragraph 5A of the Tenth Schedule, is subject to a monetary charge in the amount of fifteen thousand euros (€15,000).
(7D) A person who fails to comply with the provisions of the regulations issued pursuant to paragraph 5A of the Tenth Schedule, with the exception of the provisions of paragraph (1) of Regulation 3 of the Value Added Tax (Obligations of Payment Service Providers) Regulations 2024, is subject to monetary charges of up to fifteen thousand euros (€15,000), which are provided for in a Decree issued by the Minister.
(8) The additional taxes and financial charges provided for by the provisions of this Law or the Regulations issued pursuant to this Law are imposed by the Commissioner without examining the existence of fraud, recklessness or negligence on the part of the person liable for the tax and are collected as if they were VAT.
(9) Any person who fails to comply with the Regulations issued pursuant to paragraph 1A of the Tenth Schedule, according to which he is obliged to issue a legal receipt, is subject to a monetary charge equal to twenty percent (20%) of the value of the transaction to which the legal receipt relates.

## Inaccuracies in summary tables
45A.-(1) A person who submits to the Registrar a recapitulation table, in accordance with the provisions of subsection (2) of article 42C, which contains deficiencies or inaccuracies and has not submitted a correction form within the time period provided for in subsection (4) of said article is subject to a monetary charge of fifteen euros (€15).

## Failure to submit a summary table
45B. If, on the last day on which a person has an obligation in accordance with subsection (2) of article 42C to submit a Recapitalization Table for a specified period to the Commissioner, the Commissioner has not received the Recapitalization Table, then the person in question is subject to a monetary charge of fifty euros (€50):
It is understood that the above financial burden is not imposed on a taxable person who had an obligation to submit a Recapitalization Table that ended on 31h March 2020, 30or April 2020 and 31h May 2020.

*(95(I)/2004
 
 
 
 
 86(I)/2006
