The taxable person who makes use of the exemption provided for in paragraph (1) of point 3 in a member state in which said person is not established in accordance with the procedure described in sub-paragraphs (a) to (e) of paragraph (1) of point 3, submits for each calendar quarter in the member state of establishment, including the VAT number referred to in sub-paragraph (c) of paragraph (1) of point 3, the following:
(a) The total value of deliveries or services made during the calendar quarter in the Republic or "0", in case no deliveries or services have been made;
(b) the total value of deliveries or services made during the calendar quarter in each of the Member States other than the Republic or "0", in case no deliveries or services have been made.
6. The taxable person communicates the information specified in point 5 within one month from the end of the calendar quarter.
7. In case of exceeding the limit of the annual turnover within the European Union referred to in Article 284, paragraph 2, point a) of Directive (EU) 2020/285, the taxable person informs the Tax Commissioner within fifteen (15) working days and, at the same time, submits data on the value of the supplies or benefits referred to in paragraph 5 and which have been carried out since the beginning of the current calendar quarter to the date on which the annual turnover limit was exceeded within the European Union.
8. The Tax Commissioner may require the submission of the information provided for in subparagraphs (a) to (d) of paragraph (1) of point 1 and points 6 and 7 by electronic means, in accordance with the conditions he defines.
9. The taxable person who is established in the Republic and makes use of the exemption in accordance with point 3 and in any member state in which the person in question is not established is not obliged with regard to supplies or services covered by the exemption in the member state of establishment to submit a VAT return in accordance with the provisions of Regulation 17 of the Value Added Tax (General) Regulations.
10. By way of derogation from point 8, in the event that a taxable person does not comply with the rules provided for in points 5, 6 and 7, the Tax Commissioner may require the said person to fulfill the obligations regarding VAT as referred to in point 8.
11. For the purposes of this Section, the conversion of any value into euros is done at the exchange rate in force on the first day of the calendar year according to the exchange rate published by the European Central Bank for that day or, if there was no publication on that particular day, the exchange rates of the next day of publication.
12. (1) The Tax Commissioner shall immediately either deactivate the identification number referred to in paragraph (1) of point 3 or, in the event that the taxable person continues to make use of the exemption in another member state or other member states, adjust the data received pursuant to paragraph (1) of point 3 with regard to the interested party or member states, in the following cases:
(a) In the event that the total value of taxable transactions within the European Union declared by the taxable person exceeds the annual turnover limit within the European Union referred to in Article 284, paragraph 2, item a) of Directive (EU) 2020/285;
(b) where the Tax Commissioner granting the exemption has notified that the taxable person is not entitled to make use of the exemption or the exemption has ceased to apply in that Member State;
(c) in a case where the taxable person has notified his decision to cease to apply the exemption; or
(d) in a case in which the taxable person has informed or it can be concluded in another way that his activities have ceased.
PART K
REGISTRATION IN RELATION TO TAXABLE TRANSACTIONS:
PERSONS NOT ESTABLISHED IN THE REPUBLIC
1.-(1) A person becomes liable to register in accordance with this Part at any time, provided that conditions A to D referred to in paragraphs (2) to (5) are met.
(2) Condition A implies that –
(a) the person carries on taxable transactions, or
(b) there are reasonable grounds to believe that the person will carry out taxable transactions during the thirty (30) day period beginning at that point in time.
(3) Condition B implies that these transactions, or any of them, are or will be carried on in the course of, or in furtherance of, any business carried on by the person.
(4) Condition C implies that the person does not have any business establishment or any other permanent establishment in the Republic in relation to the business carried on by him.
(5) Condition D implies that the person is not registered under the provisions of this Law.
2.-(1) A person shall not be required to register under paragraph 1(2)(b) of this Part if the reason it is believed that taxable transactions will take place in the said period of thirty (30) days is that the business or part of the business carried on by a taxable person will be transferred to that person as an active economic unit during that period.
(2) In the case that the transfer takes place, the person to whom the business is transferred becomes obliged to register under this Part, at the time of the transfer if the conditions A to D of paragraph 1 are met in relation to said person at that time.
(3) In determining for the purposes of subsection (2) of this paragraph whether condition B is met, the reference in paragraph 1(3) to a business shall be deemed to be a reference to a business or part of a business which is transferred to the person who acquires it.
3. A person is deemed to have become liable to register under this Part at any time when the person would have become liable under paragraph 1 or 2 except for any registration which is subsequently canceled under–
(a) paragraph 11;
(b) paragraph 13(3) of Part I of the First Schedule;
(c) paragraph 6(2) of Part II of the First Schedule; and
(d) paragraph 6(3) of Part III of the First Schedule;
4.-(1) A person does not cease to be obliged to be registered by virtue of this Part with the exception of the case of sub-paragraph (2) of this paragraph.
(2) A person who has become liable to register under this Part ceases to be liable to register at any time if the Registrar is satisfied in relation to that time that the person in question –
(a) ceased to carry on taxable transactions in the course of or in furtherance of any business carried on by such person; or
(b) is no longer a person in respect of whom condition C specified in paragraph 1(4) applies.
5.-(1) A person who becomes obliged to register pursuant to paragraph 1(2) or 2(2), must notify the Registrar of this obligation before the end of the thirty (30) day period starting from the day on which the obligation is created.
(2) The Registrar shall register any such person, whether or not such person has notified him, and his registration shall have effect retrospectively from the day on which the liability arises.
6.-(1) A person who becomes obliged to register pursuant to paragraph 1(2)(b) notifies the Registrar of this obligation before the end of the period, with reference to the creation of the obligation.
(2) The Registrar shall register any such person, whether or not the said person has notified him, and his registration shall have effect from the beginning of the period by reference to which the liability arose.
Notice of termination of obligation
7.-(1) A person registered pursuant to paragraph 5 or 6, who ceases to carry out or intends to carry out taxable transactions within the framework or for the promotion of the business carried on by him at any time, shall notify the Registrar of this fact within sixty (60) days from the day of his departure.
(2) The person need not notify the Registrar whether on that day he would otherwise be liable or entitled to register under this Act.
8.-(1) The Registrar cancels the registration of the person pursuant to this Part when –
(a) is satisfied that the person has no obligation to register under this Part; and
(b) the person requires cancellation.
(2) Cancellation is effected from –
(a) the day on which the demand is made; or
(b) a later day to be agreed between the Treasurer and that person.
(3) The Registrar shall not cancel the registration with effect from any time unless he is satisfied that the person would not have been liable to register under this Act at that time. 9.-(1) The Registrar may cancel the registration of the person pursuant to this Part when he is satisfied that the person has ceased to have an obligation to be registered pursuant to this Part.
(3) The Registrar shall not cancel the registration with effect from any time unless he is satisfied that the person would not have been liable to register under this Act at that time.
10. For the purposes of paragraphs 8 and 9 in determining whether the time is the time at which a person would be liable, or entitled, to register under this Law, any provision of this Law which prevents a person from becoming liable or entitled to registration when he is already registered or when he becomes liable to register under any other provision, shall not be taken into account.
11.-(1) The Registrar may cancel the registration of a person under this Part if he is satisfied that the person was not required to register under this Law on the day he was registered.
(2) The cancellation is effective from the day of the person's registration.
Exemption from registration
12.-(1) The Commissioner may exempt a person from registration under this Part if the person satisfies the Commissioner that the taxable transactions carried out or intended to be carried out –
(a) subject to the zero rate; or
(b) would be subject to the nil rate if the person were a taxable person. or
(c) are such that they could be subject to the same handling.
(2) The authority granted to the Registrar under the provisions of sub-paragraph (1) may be exercised only upon the request of the person and if the Registrar deems it appropriate.
(3) In the event that there is a substantial change in the nature of the transactions carried out by a person who is exempt under the provisions of this paragraph, the person shall notify the Registrar regarding said change –
(a) within thirty (30) days of the day the change occurred; or
(b) within thirty (30) days from the end of the quarter in which the change occurred, in case a specific day cannot be determined.
(4) If the Registrar decides that no further action is required on such claim on or after any day or such claim has been withdrawn on any day, the Registrar shall register the person who made the claim with effect from that day.
(5) Any reference in this paragraph to transactions shall be considered as a reference to transactions carried out within the framework of or in furtherance of the business carried on by the person.
13. Any notice which is required to be submitted to the Registrar under this Part, shall be submitted in such form and contain such information as may be determined by Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers.
REGISTRATION IN RELATION TO TAXABLE TRANSACTIONS:PERSONS ESTABLISHED IN MEMBER STATES AS REGARDS PERFORMANCE FOR SMALL BUSINESSES
1. Notwithstanding the provisions of Section 1 of Part IA, points 2 to 13 shall apply for the purposes of this Section.
2. For the purposes of this Section - "exempt small business" means any taxable person established within the territory of the European Union which realizes an annual turnover within the territory of the European Union which does not exceed one hundred thousand euros (€100,000), and which benefits or wishes and is entitled to benefit from an exemption in the Republic in accordance with point 3 of this Section. "annual turnover within the Republic" means the annual total value of of taxable transactions without VAT, carried out by a taxable person within the Republic during a calendar year.
3. Exempt small business that does not become liable for registration and makes use of this right in a member state other than the Republic-
(a) provides prior notification to its Member State of establishment, with the individual identification number of its Member State of establishment;
(b) inform its Member State of establishment of any changes to information provided pursuant to paragraph (a);
(c) informs the Member State of its establishment of its intention to make use of the option provided to the Republic for exemption from the obligation to register, due to its annual turnover;
(d) inform the Member State of its establishment of the cessation of the exemption from the obligation to register in the Republic and such cessation shall take effect from the first day of the next calendar quarter following the receipt of the information or, in the event that the information is received during the last month of the calendar quarter, from the first day of the second month of the following calendar quarter.
4. Prior notification made pursuant to point 3 of this Section shall contain the following information:
(a) The name, economic activity, legal status and address of the business referred to in point 3;
(b) the date on which the Member State of establishment confirms the number to the non-established person, as a consequence of the update when it updates the details, including the individual identification number, which it has previously notified;
(c) the total value of taxable transactions carried out in the Member State in which the taxable person is established and in each of the other Member States during the preceding calendar year;
(d) the total value of the taxable transactions carried out in the Member State in which the taxable person is established and in each of the other Member States during the current calendar year before notification:
It is understood that the taxable person is not obliged to provide the information referred to in this point, as long as the information has been included in data reports pursuant to point 3, excluding the information that needs modification: It is further understood that, updating a previous notification, made pursuant to this point, includes the identification number of the Member State of establishment.
5. (1) An exempt small business established in a member state other than the Republic and which becomes liable for registration in the Republic by virtue of any provisions of this Annex, shall be exempted from this obligation, when-
(a) the Registrar is satisfied that the value of the annual turnover within the Republic, during the period of one year beginning at the time in which it would be liable to be registered by virtue of paragraph (1) of point 1 of Section 1, of Part I of this Annex, does not exceed fifteen thousand six hundred euros (€15,600); and
(b) its annual turnover within the European Union does not exceed one hundred thousand euros (€100,000).
(2) Annual turnover within the European Union means the annual total value of taxable transactions excluding VAT, carried out by a taxable person within the territory of the European Union during a calendar year and includes the following:
(a) The value of the taxable transactions, to the extent that they would be taxable if they had not been carried out by an exempt small business;
(b) the value of transactions subject to the zero rate under the provisions of Article 25;
(c) the value of exports;
(d) the value of intra-Community supplies;
(e) the value of real estate transactions, the financial transactions provided for in paragraphs (a) to (f) of point 3 of Table B of the Seventh Schedule and the value of transactions in point 1 of Table B of the Seventh Schedule, unless these transactions are incidental.
(3) Transfers of tangible or intangible investment goods of a taxable person are not taken into account for the determination of turnover.
(4) For the purposes of this Part, the conversion of any value into euros is done at the exchange rate in effect on the first day of the calendar year in accordance with the exchange rate published by the European Central Bank for that day or, if there was no publication on that particular day, the exchange rates of the next day of publication.
6. (1) The exemption from the obligation to register in the Republic applies pursuant to-
(a) the date of prior notification, from the date on which the individual identification number is notified to the taxable person by the Member State of establishment;
(b) of the update of a previous notification, from the date on which the Member State of establishment confirms the number to the taxable person as a consequence of the update.
(2) The date provided for in sub-paragraph (a) does not exceed thirty-five (35) working days from the receipt of the previous notification or the update of the previous notification referred to in sub-paragraphs (a) to (e) of paragraph (2) of point 5, excluding special cases in which for the prevention of tax evasion or avoidance the Commissioner of Taxation may determine a longer period for the carrying out the necessary checks.
7. An exempt small business that has made use of the right provided for in point 3 of this Section, submits within one month from the end of each calendar quarter to the Member State of its establishment-
(a) his individual identification number;
(b) the total value of the supplies or services made during the calendar quarter in the Member State of establishment or "0", in the event that no supplies or services have been made;
(c) the total value of the supplies or services made during the calendar quarter in each of the Member States other than the Member State of establishment or "0", in the event that no supplies or services have been made.
8. In case of exceeding the limit specified in paragraph (b) of point 5, the taxable person-
(a) inform the Member State of its establishment within fifteen (15) working days of the end of the calendar quarter in which the exceedance occurred; and
(b) submit data on the value of taxable transactions, expressed in euros, that have taken place from the beginning of the current calendar quarter to the date on which the annual turnover limit was exceeded within the European Union.
9. A taxable person who has made use of the right in the Republic provided for in point 3 of this Section, is not obliged for the taxable transactions covered by the exemption in the Republic-
(a) obtain a VAT registration number;
(b) to submit a tax return, as provided by Regulations issued pursuant to article 20.
10. In the event that an exempted small business established in another member state, which makes use of the right provided for in point 3 in the Republic, does not comply with the obligations imposed under the provisions of this Part, the Tax Commissioner may, by a notification he issues, impose the obligation on the taxable person to secure a VAT tax registration number and submit a tax return to the Republic, as provided for in Regulations issued pursuant to Article 20.
11. An exempted small business in the Republic in accordance with Article 284, paragraph 1 of Directive (EU) 2020/285 cannot benefit from said exemption, in the event that the annual turnover limit within the European Union referred to in Article 284, paragraph 2, point a) of Directive (EU) 2020/285 has been exceeded during the previous calendar year:
Provided that, in the event that during a calendar year the annual turnover limit within the European Union referred to in Article 284, paragraph 2, point a) of Directive 2020/85 is exceeded, the exemption provided for in Article 284, paragraph 1, which has been granted to the Republic, ceases to apply from that moment.
12. For the purposes of this Section, the conversion of any value into Euro is done at the exchange rate in force on the first day of the calendar year in accordance with the exchange rate published by the European Central Bank for that day or, if there was no publication on that day, the rates of the next day of publication.
13. The Commissioner may issue a notice which specifies the objects for which the small business regime can be applied and which concern objects for which the Margin Scheme can be applied.
PART II:
[Deleted]
PART III
REGISTRATION IN CONNECTION WITH ACQUISITIONS
FROM OTHER MEMBER STATES
1.-(1) A person who -
(a) is not registered under this Act; and
(b) is not liable to registration under Part I or Part II of this Schedule.
becomes liable to registration under this Part at the end of any month if, during the period beginning on 1 January of the year in which that month falls, that person has made relevant acquisitions the value of which exceeds six thousand pounds (L.K. 6,000).
(2) A person who is not registered or liable for registration as referred to in sub-paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) becomes liable for registration under this Part at any time if there are reasonable grounds to believe that the value of his relevant acquisitions during the thirty (30) day period commencing from that point in time will exceed six thousand pounds (L.K. 6,000).
(3) A person is deemed to have become liable for registration under this Part at any time at which he has become so liable under the preceding provisions of this paragraph, except for any registration which is subsequently canceled under paragraph 6(3), paragraph 13(3) of Part I or paragraph 6(2) of Part II of this Schedule.
(4) A person shall not cease to be liable for registration under this Part, except in accordance with paragraph 2.
(5) For the determination of the value of the relevant acquisitions of any person for the purposes of this paragraph, the amount of consideration for any acquisition as far as it represents any obligation of the supplier, in accordance with the law of another member state, for the payment of VAT. on the deed pursuant to which the acquisition is effected is not taken into account.
(6) In determining the value of acquisitions of a person for the purposes of paragraphs (1) and (2), acquisitions to which section 13B(4) applies shall not be taken into account.
2.-(1) Subject to subsection (2), a person who has become liable for registration under this Part ceases to be liable at any time if –
(a) his relevant acquisitions during the year ending 31 December did not exceed six thousand (6,000) pounds, and
(b) the Commissioner is satisfied that the value of his relevant acquisitions for the year immediately following that year will not exceed six thousand pounds (£6,000).
(2) A person shall not cease to be liable for registration under this Part at any time if there are reasonable grounds for believing that the value of his relevant acquisitions in the thirty (30) day period commencing from that point in time will exceed six thousand pounds (L.K. 6,000).
3.-(1) A person who becomes liable for registration pursuant to this Part must notify the Registrar of his obligation -
(a) in the case of an obligation under paragraph 1(1) within thirty (30) days from the end of the month in which it becomes liable; and
(b) in the case of an obligation under paragraph 1(2), before the end of the thirty (30) day period within which the obligation is created.
(2) The registrar shall register any such person, whether or not he notifies the registrar, and his registration shall take effect from the relevant time or from such earlier time as may be agreed between the registrar and such person.
(3) In this paragraph "the relevant time"-
(a) in the case falling within subsection (1)(a), means the end of the month following the month at the end of which the liability arose; and
(b) in the case falling within sub-paragraph (1)(b), means the beginning of the period in which the liability arose.
Right to registration, etc.
4.-(1) When a person, who is not required to be registered under this Law and is not already registered, satisfies the Registrar that he is making relevant acquisitions, the Registrar, if requested in writing by the said person, proceeds with his registration and his registration is valid from the date he requested it or from an earlier date that could be agreed upon between the Registrar and that person.
(2) When a person who is not required to register under this Law, and is not already registered -
(a) satisfies the Commissioner that it intends to make relevant acquisitions from a specified date, and
(b) apply in writing to be registered under this Part,
the Registrar may, subject to such conditions as he deems fit to impose, register it and the registration shall be effective from such day as may be agreed upon between the Registrar and that person.
(3) Conditions imposed by virtue of subsection (2) -
(a) may be enforced in whole or in part by reference to, or without reference to, any conditions specified for the purposes of this paragraph, and
(b) may, whenever imposed, be subsequently amended by the Commissioner.
(4) When a person who is eligible for registration pursuant to paragraphs 9 or 10 of Part I of this Annex, requests in writing to be registered pursuant to this paragraph, he shall be registered pursuant to Part I and not pursuant to Part III of this Annex.
Notification of issues affecting continued registration
5.-(1) Any person registered pursuant to this Part who ceases to be registrable pursuant to this Law must notify the Registrar of this fact within sixty (60) days from the day of its occurrence.
(2) A person registered under paragraph 4(2) must notify the Registrar within thirty (30) days of the first event after his registration when he makes a relevant acquisition, that he has made such an acquisition.
(3) For the purposes of this paragraph, a person ceases to be registrable under this Law when -
(a) ceases to be a person who would be liable or entitled to register under this Act if his registration and any legislation preventing a person from being liable to register under different provisions at the same time were disregarded; or
(b) in the case of a person who has registered under paragraph 4(2), was not such a person at the time of his registration, ceases to have any intention of making relevant acquisitions.
6.-(1) Subject to paragraph 7, when a person registered under this Part satisfies the Commissioner that he is not required to be registered, the Commissioner, if the person so requests in writing, cancels his registration and the cancellation takes effect from the day he requested it or from a later date that could be agreed between the Commissioner and that person.
(2) Subject to paragraph 7, when the Registrar is satisfied that a person registered under this Part has ceased to be registrable under this Part, he may cancel his registration and the cancellation shall take effect from the day of cessation or from such later date as may be agreed between the Registrar and that person.
(3) Where the Commissioner is satisfied that, on the day on which a person was registered under this Part, he -
(a) was not registrable under this Part; and
(b) in the case of a person registered under paragraph 4(2), did not have the intention in respect of which it was registered,
the Registrar may cancel his registration and the cancellation is effective from that day.
(4) Subject to paragraph 7, when the Commissioner is satisfied that a person who has been registered under paragraph 4(2) and is not currently required to be registered under this Part -
(a) has not, from the date specified in its application for registration, commenced making relevant acquisitions; or
(b) breached any term or condition relating to its registration;
the Registrar may cancel his registration and the cancellation is effective from a specific date or, as the case may be, from the date of the violation or from another later date that could be agreed between the Registrar and that person.
(5) For the purposes of this paragraph, a person is registrable under this Part at any time during which he is required to be registered under this Part or is a person who makes relevant acquisitions.
Cancellation terms
7.-(1) The Registrar does not cancel the registration of a person pursuant to paragraph 6(1), with the cancellation effective from any time, unless he is satisfied that in relation to this time the person in question would not be liable for registration pursuant to this Law.
(2) The Registrar shall not cancel the registration of a person pursuant to paragraph 6(2) or (4) of this Part, with effect from the cancellation from any time, unless he is satisfied that in relation to that time, the person in question would not be liable, or entitled, for registration pursuant to this Law.
(3) Subject to subsection (4), the registration of a person who -
(a) is registered under paragraph 4 of this Part; or
(b) would not, if not registered, be liable or entitled to registration under any provision of this Law other than that of paragraph 4 of this Part,
is not canceled with the effect of the cancellation from any time before January 1 of the year following the completion of twenty-four (24) months from the date on which its registration became effective.
(4) Sub-paragraph (3) does not apply to cancellation of registration under paragraph 6(3) or (4) of this Part.
(5) In determining for the purposes of this paragraph, whether a person would be liable or entitled to registration at any time, any provision of this Law which prevents a person from becoming liable or entitled to registration when he is already registered or when he becomes liable to register by virtue of any other provision, shall not be taken into account.
Power to vary specified sums by decree
8. The Council of Ministers may by decree replace any of the amounts specified in this Part with other amounts.
9. Regulations may provide that any notice required to be submitted to the Registrar under this Part shall be submitted in a certain form and contain certain particulars.
10. For the purposes of this Part, an acquisition of goods from another Member State is a relevant acquisition when -
(a) is a taxable acquisition of goods other than goods subject to excise duty or relating to new means of transport;
(b) is not an acquisition pursuant to a taxable transaction and is deemed, for the purposes of this Law, to be made within the Republic; and
(c) the event which, in relation to that acquisition, is the first relevant event for the purposes of taxation of that acquisition.
Part IV
Submission of notices by electronic means
In cases where any person chooses to use electronic means to submit any notification provided for in this Annex, then for the purposes of this Law and any civil or criminal proceedings, the code number which is granted by the Commissioner to the person submitting the notifications by electronic means, is considered to be in place of the handwritten signature that the notifications would have if they were not submitted by electronic means and the person submitting the notifications is considered to have knowledge of their content.
The Registrar may, by means of a Notice published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, determine the electronic means for the submission of notices by a person referred to in the previous paragraph.
Part V
Registration for services provided to taxable persons in another member state for which VAT is payable. is the recipient:
1.-(1) Any person carrying on business-
(a) is not registered under this Law, and
(b) is not liable to registration under Parts I, II and III of this Schedule;
becomes liable for registration under this Part, on any day that person provides relevant services.
(2) For the purposes of this Part, "related services" are services provided to a taxable person in another Member State for which VAT is due in that Member State by the recipient, by virtue of provisions applicable in that Member State, corresponding to the provisions of article 11 of this Law.
Notice of the registration obligation
2.-(1) A person who becomes obliged to register pursuant to paragraph 1 above, must notify the Registrar of his obligation within thirty (30) days after the day the obligation is created.
(2) The Registrar shall register any person, whether or not he gives notice to the Registrar, and the registration shall take effect from the day on which the liability arises.
Cancellation of Registration
3.-(1) When a person registered under this Part satisfies the Registrar that he is not required to be registered, the Registrar, if requested in writing by that person, cancels his registration and the cancellation takes effect from the date he requested it or from a later date that could be agreed between the Registrar and that person.
(2) A person who has become liable for registration under this Part ceases to be liable for registration under this Part, at any point in time, if the Registrar is satisfied that he does not intend to provide relevant services, within the twelve months following that point in time.
(3) A person who ceases to be liable for registration pursuant to the provisions of this Part must notify the Registrar of this fact within sixty (60) days from the day of its occurrence.
(4) The Registrar shall not cancel the registration of a person pursuant to subsection (1), with effect from the cancellation from any time, unless he is satisfied that in relation to that time the person in question would not be liable for registration pursuant to this Law.
SECOND APPENDIX
(Article 8)
CONSIDERED ACTS
DELIVERY OF GOODS
OR PROVISION OF SERVICES
1.—(1) Any transfer of full ownership of goods constitutes a delivery of goods but, subject to the provisions of sub-section (2) below, the transfer—
(a) Of any undivided share of ownership, or
(b) possession of the goods;
constitutes a provision of services.
(2) If possession of the goods is transferred—
(a) Under an agreement to sell the goods, or
(b) by virtue of agreements which expressly provide that ownership shall also pass at some time in the future (to be determined or ascertained by the agreements but in any event not later than the time when the goods are fully repaid);
then in both cases it constitutes a delivery of the goods.
2. The supply of electricity, gas, heat or cold or similar goods constitutes a delivery of goods.
3. Constitute a delivery of goods—
(a) The transfer of real property;
(b) the transfer of an undivided ideal share in immovable property;
(c) the transfer of possession of immovable property—
(i) by virtue of a contract or agreement to sell it, or
(ii) pursuant to an agreement which expressly provides that the real property will also be transferred at some future time;
(d) the assignment or grant of any real rights in real property;
(e) the ownership, private use, lease or use for any purpose of the immovable Property referred to in the provisions of subparagraph (b) of paragraph 1 and in the provisions of paragraph 4 of the Eighth Schedule;
(f) the substantive transfer of the right to dispose of the immovable property as owner or of the right to dispose of the immovable property.
4.—(1) Subject to the provisions of subsection (2) below, where goods forming part of the assets of a business are transferred or disposed of under the direction of the person carrying on the business so as to no longer form part of those assets, whether for consideration or not, that constitutes a delivery by that person of the goods.
(2) Subsection (1) above does not apply if the transfer or disposal of the goods is—
(a) A gift given in the course of or in furtherance of business (unless it forms part of a series or succession of gifts given to the same person from time to time) provided that the cost to the donor does not exceed ten pounds (£10); or
(b) subject to the provisions of subsection (3) below, a sample of goods given as a gift to any person.
(3) When—
(a) The same person gives a number of samples to another person (whether all on one occasion or on several occasions), and
(b) those samples are identical or have no substantial differences between them;
then subsection (1) above applies to all but one of the said specimens or, as the case may be, applies to all but the first given.
(4) When, by virtue of the instructions of the person carrying on a business, goods which form part of the assets of the business or which are owned or used by the business are put to any private use or are used, or are made available to any person for use, for any purpose other than the purpose of the business, whether for consideration or not, this constitutes a supply of services.
(4A)(a) Subsection (4) does not apply to—
(i) the tangible goods, owned by the company and placed by it in permanent exploitation, without including in their value any costs of repair and maintenance, excluding immovable property;
(i) immovable property as specified in paragraphs 1(b) to 4 of the Eighth Schedule;
(b) Sub-sub-paragraph (a) does not apply to assets on which the person or his predecessors incurred VAT. before 1 January 2011.
(c) In the case where the person has been charged with VAT. in relation to the asset before January 1, 2011, the VAT incurred by the person on or after 1 January 2011, in relation to the asset, is not deemed to be related to the purposes of the person's business by virtue of the provisions of paragraph 4(4) and paragraph 6, if, and to the extent that the asset is used or is to be used for the private use of the person or the private use of his staff, or generally for purposes other than the purposes of his business.
(d) For the purposes of this paragraph-
(i) “asset” means anything falling within paragraph (a) of this paragraph;
(ii) "person" means the person carrying on the business as referred to in sub-clause (4) of paragraph 4;
(iii) “the VAT charged to the person in respect of an asset” means -
(k) VAT upon delivery of the asset to the person;
(l) VAT on the delivery to the person of any goods or services, the cost of which is a cost attached to the asset;
(m) VAT on the acquisition by the person from another Member State of the asset or anything that constitutes it; and
(n) VAT which was paid or became payable by the person upon the importation of the asset or anything that constitutes it from a place that is not a member state.".
(5) Neither sub-paragraph (1) nor sub-paragraph (4) above require anything which the person carrying on the business carries out without consideration in relation to any goods to be regarded as a supply or supply, unless that person is entitled under sections 20 and 21 to deduct all or any part of the VAT. on the delivery, acquisition or importation of the goods or other goods contained therein.
(6) Anything which constitutes a supply of goods or provision of services under sub-paragraph (1) or (4) above shall be deemed to be carried out within the framework of or in furtherance of the business (even if not otherwise regarded as such); and in the case of a business carried on by a natural person—
(a) Sub-paragraph (1) above shall apply to any transfer or disposal of the goods for his personal benefit; and
(b) subsection (4) above applies to goods used, or made available for use, by him personally.
4A.-(1) Where, in relation to a case not falling within paragraph 4(1), goods which constitute business assets -
(a) are transferred from any member state pursuant to the instructions of the person carrying on the business, and
(b) are transferred within the framework of or in furtherance of such business with the aim of being taken to a place within another Member State other than that from which they are transferred,
then, regardless of whether or not the transfer is linked to an act for consideration, it constitutes a delivery of goods by that person.
(2) Subsection (1) does not apply—
(a) for the carriage of the goods from any Member State in the course of their carriage from one part of that Member State to another part of the same Member State; or
(b) to goods which have been moved from a place outside the Member States to enter the territory of the Community and are moved from a Member State before the time when any Community customs debt would be incurred in respect of any Community customs duty on the entry of the goods into that territory.
(c) when goods have been shipped or transported for the needs of the delivery of gas through the natural gas system located in the Republic or through any network connected to such a system, the delivery of electricity or heat or cold through the heating or cooling networks, under the conditions of subsections (12) or (13) of article 10.
5. When in the case of a business carried on by a taxable person goods that are part of the assets of the business are sold by another person by virtue of any authority exercised by the other person, in satisfaction of a debt owed by the taxable person, they are considered to be delivered by the taxable person within the framework of or for the furtherance of his business.
6.—(1) When a person ceases to be a taxable person, any goods which at that time form part of the assets of a business carried on by him shall be deemed to have been delivered by him in the course or furtherance of his business immediately before he ceased to be a taxable person, unless—
(a) The business is transferred as an operating economic unit to another taxable person; or
(b) the business is continued by another person who, pursuant to Regulations issued pursuant to article 35(3), is considered a taxable person; or
(c) VAT on fictitious delivery it would not exceed two hundred pounds.
(2) This paragraph does not apply to any goods in respect of which the taxable person can satisfy the Commissioner that-
(a) He has not been allowed a deduction of input tax in respect of the supply of the goods, their acquisition from another Member State or their importation from a place outside the Member States; and
(b) did not acquire the goods as part of the business assets transferred to it as a going concern from another taxable person.
(3) The Council of Ministers may by decree modify the amount specified in subsection (1)(c) above.
FOURTH APPENDIX
PART I
(Article 14 and 14A)
VALUE — SPECIAL CASES
1.—(1) In any case in which—
(a) The value of a transaction (notwithstanding the provisions of this paragraph) carried out by a taxable person for monetary consideration is less than its normal value and
(b) the person making the transaction and the person to whom it is made are related, and
(c) if the transaction in question is taxable, but the person to whom it is carried out is not entitled under articles 20 and 21 to deduct the entire VAT. who burdens her,
then the Registrar may determine that the value of the transaction will be considered to be its normal value.
(2) Any decision under this paragraph shall be notified in writing to the person carrying out the transaction, but no such decision may be notified after the lapse of three years from the time of the transaction.
(3) A decision notified to a person under this paragraph in relation to a transaction carried out by that person may also include a decision that the value of any transaction—
(a) Made by it after the notice, or after such later date as may be specified in the notice, and
(b) for which the conditions of paragraphs (a) to (c) of subsection (1) above are satisfied,
shall be deemed to be its open market value.
(4) For the purposes of this paragraph, any question as to the extent to which one person is related to another shall be determined in accordance with the following provisions:
(a) A person is related to another person if the first person is the spouse or relative of the second person, or is the spouse of the relative of the second person, or is the relative of the spouse of the second person;
(b) a person is related to any person with whom he maintains a partnership, and to the spouse or relative of any person with whom he maintains a partnership;
(c) a company is connected with another company—
(i) if the same person controls both, or if one person controls one and persons connected with him, or he and persons connected with him, control the other; or
(ii) if a group of two or more persons has control of each company, and the groups either consist of the same persons or could be deemed to consist of the same persons by considering (in one or more cases) a member of either group to be replaced by another person with whom he is connected;
(d) a company is connected with another person if that person controls it or that person and persons connected with it control it;
(e) any two or more persons acting together to secure or exercise control of a company shall be deemed in relation to that company to be connected with each other and with any person acting at the direction of any of them to secure or exercise control of the company;
(f) in this paragraph—
"company" includes any body corporate or association of persons without legal personality, but does not include a partnership;
"control", in relation to a body corporate, means the power of a person to ensure that—
(i) by holding shares or voting rights in or in relation to the body corporate, or in relation to any other body corporate, or;
(ii) by virtue of any powers conferred on it by the articles of association or other document governing the operation of that body corporate or any other body corporate;
the affairs of the first legal person are conducted according to the wishes of that person, and, in relation to a partnership, means the right to a share in excess of one-second of the assets, or in excess of one-second of the income of the partnership;
"relative" means a brother, sister, ascendant or descendent in the direct line or their spouses.
(5) This paragraph does not apply to a transaction to which paragraph 7 below applies.
1A.-(1) When -
(a) any goods the delivery of which entails their transfer to the Republic -
(i) are charged in connection with their transfer to the Republic with excise duty; or
(ii) concern new means of transport which, in connection with their transport to the Republic, are subject to consumption tax; or
(iii) for such transfer we are subject, in accordance with any provision in force from time to time for transitional purposes in connection with the accession of any State to the European Union, to any import duty or excise duty or excise duty;
then the value of the delivery shall, for the purposes of this Law, be deemed to be the sum of its value, and the amount, to the extent not already included in that value, of excise duty or, as the case may be, of import duty or excise duty or excise duty which has or will be paid in respect of the goods.
2.—(1) Where goods are delivered or services rendered for cash consideration and on terms allowing a discount for early payment, the consideration shall be taken for the purposes of section 14 as reduced by the discount, whether or not the payment is made in accordance with those terms.
(2) This paragraph does not apply where the terms include any reference to payment by instalments.
3. Where the right to receive goods or receive services embodied in a voucher is granted for consideration, the consideration is disregarded for the purposes of this Law except to the extent (if any) that it exceeds the amount stated on the voucher.
4.—(1) When there is delivery of goods pursuant to—
(a) Regulations issued pursuant to article 8(5); or
(b) of paragraph 4(1) of the Second Schedule (but not against consideration); or
(c) of paragraph 6 of the Second Schedule,
(d) of paragraph 3(e) of the Second Schedule to this Law,
then, except where paragraph 7 below applies, the value of the delivery is determined in accordance with the next subparagraph.
(2) The value of the delivery is—
(a) Such consideration as would be payable by the person making the delivery if, at the time of delivery, he were to purchase goods identical in all respects (including age and condition) to the goods in question; or
(b) if the value cannot be ascertained in accordance with paragraph (a) above, such consideration as would be payable by the said person if he were at the time to purchase goods similar and of the same age and condition as the goods in question; or
(c) if the value cannot be ascertained under either paragraph (a) or (b) above, the cost of production of the goods in question if they were to be produced at that time.
(3) For the purposes of subsection (2) above, the amount of consideration which would be payable by any person if he were to purchase any goods shall be deemed to be the amount which would be payable after deducting any amount included in the purchase price as VAT. upon delivery of the goods to said person.
5. When there is provision of services pursuant to—
(a) Regulations issued pursuant to article 8(4); or
(b) of paragraph 4(4) of the Second Schedule (but not for consideration), then the value of the supply is taken to be the full cost to the taxable person of providing the services except where paragraph 7 below applies.
6. Where any provision of services is deemed under section 11 to be made by the person receiving them, the value of the provision shall be deemed to be—
(a) In the event that the consideration for which the services therein were actually rendered was monetary, such amount as is equal to that consideration; and
(b) in the event that the consideration was not or was not wholly monetary, such amount as is equivalent to that consideration.
7.—(1) This paragraph applies where a supply of goods or provision of services, whether or not for consideration, is made by an employer and consists of—
(a) In providing, within the context of catering, food and drinks to its employees, or
(b) in providing accommodation to its employees in a hotel, inn or similar accommodation.
(2) The value of the transaction to which this paragraph applies shall be taken to be nil, unless the transaction is carried out for consideration wholly or partly in money, in which case its value shall be determined without regard to any non-monetary consideration.
8.—(1) Subject to the following provisions of this paragraph, when—
(a) There is delivery of goods or provision of services, and
(b) any amount relevant to the determination of the value of the transaction is expressed in a currency other than the Cypriot pound,
then, for the purposes of determining the value of the transaction, this amount is converted into Cypriot pounds taking into account the exchange rate of this currency to the Cypriot pound, on the relevant day, in accordance with the customs legislation in force at the time.
(2) The time by reference to which the relevant exchange rate is determined for the purposes of determining the value of any transaction is the time when the transaction takes place; and, accordingly, the date on which it takes place is the relevant date for the purposes of subsection (1) above.
9. A decision taken pursuant to paragraph 1 above may be modified or withdrawn by the Registrar by subsequent written decision.
10. When there is a lease of the immovable property, specified in paragraph 9 (a)ii of Table A of the Fifth Schedule and in paragraphs 1(b) to 4 of the Eighth Schedule, with a right of redemption, the value of the transaction is the agreed price after deducting the partial rents paid for which a separate tax is due, as a provision of services, at the time provided from the agreement to be paid.
PART II
VALUE OF ACQUISITIONS FROM OTHER MEMBER STATES-
SPECIAL CASES
1.-(1) When goods, in such cases as the Council of Ministers may by Regulations determine, are acquired in the Republic from another member state -
(a) are charged in connection with their transfer to the Republic with excise duty; or
(b) concern new means of transport which, in connection with their transport to the Republic, are subject to consumption tax; or
(c) for such transfer are subject, in accordance with any provision in force from time to time for transitional purposes in connection with the accession of any State to the European Union, to any import duty or excise duty or excise duty;
then the value of the delivery shall be deemed for the purposes of this Law to be the sum of its value without regard to this paragraph and the amount, to the extent not already included in that value, of the excise duty or, as the case may be, the import duty or the excise duty or the consumption tax that has or will be paid in relation to the goods in question.
2.-(1) Where goods are acquired from another Member State pursuant to anything which is deemed to be a transaction for the purposes of this Law by virtue of paragraph 4(1) or 4A of the Second Schedule, the value of the relevant transaction shall be determined, in the case where there is no consideration, in accordance with subsection (2).
(2) The value of the deed is -
(a) such amount of consideration as would be payable by the delivering supplier if, at the time of acquisition, it were to purchase goods identical in all respects, including age and condition, to the goods in question; or
(b) if the value cannot be ascertained under paragraph (a), such amount of consideration as would be payable by the supplier if he were at the time to purchase goods similar and of the same age and condition as the goods in question; or
(c) if the value cannot be ascertained either by virtue of paragraph (a) or by virtue of paragraph (b), the cost of production of the goods in question if they were to be produced at that time.
(3) For the purposes of subsection (2), the amount of consideration which would be payable by any person if he were to purchase any goods shall be deemed to be the amount which would be payable after deducting any amount included in the purchase price as VAT. upon delivery of the goods to said person.
3.-(1) Subject to the following provisions of this paragraph, when -
(a) goods are acquired from another Member State; and
(b) any amount relevant to the determination of the value of the relevant transaction is expressed in a currency other than euros,
then, for the purposes of determining the value of the relevant transaction, this amount is converted into euros taking into account the exchange rate of this currency to the euro, on the relevant day, according to the exchange rate published by the European Central Bank.
(2) Where goods are acquired by a Member State, the appropriate exchange rate should be determined for the purpose of determining the value of the relevant transaction by reference to the relevant time. and accordingly, the day on which that time falls is the relevant day for the purposes of subsection (1).
4. In this Part -
"relevant act", in relation to any acquisition of goods from another Member State, means the act under which the goods are acquired;
“the relevant time”, in relation to any such acquisition, means—
(a) if the person from whom the goods are acquired is not a taxable person and the time of the acquisition is not determined in accordance with Regulations made under section 12C(3), the time of the event which, in relation to that acquisition, is the first relevant event for the purposes of taxation of the acquisition; and
(b) in any other case, the time of acquisition.

(Article 18)
DELIVERIES OF GOODS AND PROVISION OF SERVICES
SUBJECT TO THE REDUCED FACTOR OF FIVE HUNDRED (5%)
Table A: Deliveries of Goods
1. Coffins.
2(a) Deliveries of fertilizers classified in Chapter 31 of the combined nomenclature as established by Regulation (EEC) no. 2658/87 of the Council on the tariff and statistical nomenclature and the common customs tariff as amended or replaced from time to time.
(b) Deliveries of insecticides, rodenticides, fungicides, herbicides, anti-vegetation and plant growth regulators, disinfectants and similar products, intended exclusively for use in agricultural production.

4. Water deliveries. [S.S.: Added with the K.D.P. 54/2011, Par. III(I), E.E. 4478, 18.2.2011 and valid from 10.1.2011].
5. [Deleted].
6. [Deleted].

Excluded from the provisions of this paragraph are bread, milk, eggs, baby food, coffee and sugar. [NB: This exemption is valid from November 1, 2023 until April 30, 2024].
The following products are excluded from the provisions of this paragraph: [NB: This exemption is valid from December 1, 2023 to May 30, 2024].
Meat of bovine animals, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0201, Meat of bovine animals, frozen falling within CN codes 0202, Meat of swine, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes 0203, Meat of sheep or goats, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes ex 0204, edible offal of bovine animals, swine, sheep, goats fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes ex 0206, meat and offal edible, fresh, preserved with chilled or frozen, of poultry of heading 0105 falling within CN codes 0207, other edible meat and offal, fresh, chilled or frozen, of rabbits, hares falling within CN headings ex 0208.

Excluded from the provisions of this paragraph are the following products [S.S.: This paragraph which was added with the C.D.P. 337/2025, E.E. Par. III(I), No. 5981, 21/11/2025, valid from January 1, 2026 until December 31, 2026]:
(i) deliveries of baby milk
(ii) Fresh or chilled potatoes falling within CN codes ex 0701, fresh or chilled tomatoes falling within CN codes 0702, onions, shallots, garlic, leeks and other similar vegetables, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0703, cabbages, cauliflowers and broccoli, curly cabbages, fresh or chilled turnips falling within CN codes ex 0704, lettuce (Lactuca sativa) and radishes (Cichorium spp.), fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0705, carrots, turnips, salad radishes, scallions, turnips, radishes and similar edible roots, fresh or chilled of heading CN 0706, cucumbers and gherkins, fresh or chilled of heading CN 0707, leguminous vegetables, with or without pods, fresh or preserved with chilled falling within CN codes 0708, aubergines, celery, mushrooms, peppers, spinach, artichokes, unprepared olives, pumpkins, courgettes, salad other than lettuce (coriander, parsley, slider, rocket, cabbages), capers, fennel, fresh or chilled which classified under the codes of class ex CN 0709."

Meat of bovine animals, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0201, Meat of bovine animals, frozen falling within CN codes 0202, Meat of swine, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes 0203, Meat of sheep or goats, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes 0204, edible offal of bovine animals, swine, sheep, goats fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes 0207, other meat and offal edible, fresh, frozen chilled or frozen rabbits. rabbits classified under CN codes ex 0208.

14. Supply of electricity falling under the Single Registration Domestic Tariff with code 01 and Double Registration Domestic Tariff with code 02, electricity supply falling under the special domestic tariff for specific categories of vulnerable consumers with code 08 and the thermal energy storage tariff with code 56 for consumers falling under Tariffs 01, 02 and 08 of the Cyprus Electricity Authority [S.S.: This paragraph applies to electricity bills and is valid for electricity metering that takes place from 1.5.2026 to 31.3.2027].

16. Child car seats:
It is understood that child car seats must be configured for use in land transport for people up to 12 years of age.
17. Objects of artistic value which have been executed entirely by the hand of the artist:
It is understood that the objects of artistic value concern only the objects that are sold by a gallery or by an auctioneer or directly by the artist and are divided into three categories:
i. Paintings, collages and similar small paintings, paintings and drawings, executed entirely by the artist's hand, excluding architectural, engineering and other industrial, commercial, topographical or similar designs, decorative handicrafts, painted fabrics for theater scenery, workshop decoration or similar uses and are classified in CN code 9701.
ii. original engravings, lithographs and lithographs, that is, designs printed directly in a limited number of copies, in black and white or in colour, from one or more plates made entirely by the artist's hand, regardless of the technique or material used, excluding any mechanical or photomechanical method and falling within CN code 9703 00 00.
iii. works of statuary or sculpture, originals of any material, as long as they have been made entirely by the artist's hand, casts of sculptures, eight copies at the most, which are made under the supervision of the artist himself or his successors and are classified in the CN code 9703 00 00. [S.S.: The above paragraph 17 was added with the K.D.P. 23/2014, E.E. 4752, Par. III(I), 24.1.2014 and its effective date is 24h January 2014.]
Table B: Service Provisions
1. Funeral home services.
2. [Deleted].
3. Services of authors, composers or artists and performers of works of art, and their rights.
4. Transport of persons and the luggage they bring with them by city and rural buses with a fare for each passenger.
5. Renting space in a camp or campsite for caravans.
6. Right of entry to shows, circuses, fairs, amusement parks, concerts, museums, zoos, cinemas, exhibitions and similar cultural events and related venues.
7. Right of entry to sports events and right to use sports facilities.
8. Repair of medical equipment and products covered by the provisions of paragraph 6 of Table A of this Annex.
9. Provision of medical and dental services and spa therapy that are not exempt based on paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Seventh Schedule of this Law. Excluded from this paragraph are medical examinations or operations of an exclusively cosmetic or aesthetic nature.
10. Hairdressing services.
The VAT Commissioner may, by means of a Notice published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, determine the details of the application of this paragraph.
11. Renovation and repair of private homes, carried out by a taxable person and consisting of plumbing, electrical, carpentry, painting and construction works, excluding materials that constitute more than fifty percent (50%) of the value of the service provision.
For the purposes of this paragraph -
<renovation> means intervention with additions, improvements, repairs in a private house, which is considered old, i.e. at least three years have passed since the first installation in it. The period of eighteen (18) months referred to in the concept of first use coincides with the three years;
<repair> means the restoration to the original good condition of a private residence which is considered to be old, i.e. at least three years have passed since it was first occupied;
<first installation> means the first use of the private residence after its delivery or erection, including ownership, occupancy, lease or any other use which continues on a regular basis.
<first use> means the use or exploitation of the private residence after its delivery or construction, which is done systematically for a period of at least eighteen (18) months.
<dwelling> means a specially designed and constructed, roofed building, which is used as a place of residence.
The VAT Commissioner may, by his Notification, published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, determine the details of the implementation of this paragraph.
11A. Renovation and repair of private homes, carried out by a taxable person and consisting of static upgrading works, energy efficiency improvement or other plumbing, electrical, carpentry, oil painting and construction works related to these objectives.
"renovation" means intervention with repairs, improvements, repairs in a private house which concern improving its energy efficiency or strengthening its statistical efficiency, which is considered old, i.e. at least three years have passed since the first installation in it. The period of eighteen (18) months referred to in the concept of first use coincides with the three years.
'repair' means the restoration to the original good condition of a private residence which is considered to be old, i.e. at least three years have passed since the first installation in it.
'first establishment' means the first use of the private residence after its delivery or erection, including ownership, occupancy, letting or any other use which continues on a regular basis.
'first use' means the use or exploitation of the private residence after its handover or construction, which takes place systematically for a period of at least eighteen (18) months.
"dwelling" means a specially designed and designed, roofed building, which is used as a place of residence for vulnerable consumers, who have a special domestic tariff with code 08 of the Cyprus Electricity Authority, or is used as a place of residence and is located in an area that is designated as critical.
"static upgrading" means intervention in the structure of the building in order to increase its resistance to the earthquake, so as to meet the requirements of the guidance document of the Ministry of the Interior as a minimum.
The Registrar may, by means of a notice published in the Official Gazette of the Republic, determine the details of the implementation of this paragraph.
12. Food activities from school canteens.
The term "food activities" has the meaning assigned to that term in paragraph 16 of the Sixth Schedule of the Law.
(13) Construction and renovation of the following public and private buildings used for public interest activities:
of buildings intended for educational institutions, which provide exempted education in accordance with paragraph 9 sub-paragraphs (a), (b) excluding tutoring schools, (c) and (h) of the Seventh Schedule:
Provided that this paragraph applies to school buildings whose infrastructure is used for exempt education in accordance with paragraph 9 sub-paragraphs (a), (b) excluding tutoring schools, (c) and (h) of the Seventh Schedule.
(14) [Deleted]
Table C: Delivery or Construction of Residence
1. Delivery of a building or part of it, including the plot of land purchased together with it or an undivided ideal share on it, as long as the delivery takes place before the first installation in it, which is used as a main and permanent place of residence in the Republic.
2. Transfer of possession of a building or part of it, including the plot of land that is transferred together with it or of an undivided ideal share on it, by virtue of a contract of its sale or by virtue of an agreement that expressly provides that the building will be transferred together with the plot of land that is transferred together with it at some time in the future or by virtue of its lease with right of redemption, as long as it takes place before the first installation on it and is used as the owner and permanent place of residence in the Republic.
3. Erection of a building or part of it on buildable land, specially designed to be used as a main and permanent place of residence in the Republic, as long as it is carried out before the first installation in it:
It is provided that, for the buildings referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 and 3, a duly completed application for securing a planning permit must have been submitted after May 1, 2004 to the "competent authority" and in the case of buildings, for which the filing of a planning permit is not required pursuant to a general or special decree based on the Town Planning and Spatial Planning Law must have been duly submitted to the "competent authority" completed application for the issuance of a building permit after May 1, 2004:
It is further understood that the construction of a building or part of it, as provided for in paragraph 3, also includes the following cases:
(j) Erection of a residence on Government land by a refugee, who has been granted permission to use Government land and on which he has erected a residence at his own expense;
(ii) construction of a house on a government plot granted by lease deed on favorable terms to a needy family;
(iii) building a house on buildable land belonging to a relative of the person who builds it, up to the fourth degree of blood or blood relationship.
The provisions of this Table apply to the first one hundred and thirty (130) square meters of buildable residential area, as determined based on the building factor, in accordance with the architectural building plans submitted to the competent authority to secure a planning permit or where this is not required by virtue of a general or special decree based on the Urban Planning and Spatial Planning Law to secure a building permit, up to and including value of three hundred and fifty thousand euros (€350,000), provided that the total value of the transaction does not exceed four hundred and seventy-five thousand euros (€475,000) and the total buildable area one hundred and ninety (190) square meters.
In the case of a person with a disability, the provisions of this Table apply to the first one hundred and ninety (190) square meters of buildable area of the house, as determined based on the building factor, regardless of the total buildable area of the house.
The value of the transaction may be revised by notifying the Tax Commissioner, following a decision of the Council of Ministers.
In the case of large families, the total area of the residence is increased both in the case of construction and in the case of delivery of the residence for each additional child beyond three children by fifteen (15) square meters.
The beneficiary submits a responsible declaration on a special form determined by the Commissioner with notification published in the official gazette of the Republic, in which he must state, among other things, that he has not secured another residence in the Republic, which he uses as his main and permanent place of residence, paying the reduced VAT rate. The responsible declaration can be submitted at any stage during the construction of the residence or in the case of delivery before the residence comes into the possession of the beneficiary:
Provided that, the Registrar may allow the responsible declaration to be submitted within twelve (12) months from the date the beneficiary acquires possession of the residence, as long as he is satisfied, provided that full supporting information is submitted to him, that the non-timely submission of the responsible declaration is due to the beneficiary's absence from the Republic, his illness or any other event which, at the discretion of the Registrar, may sufficiently justify the non-timely submission of the responsible declaration.
Attached to the responsible statement are:
(j) contract of sale, in the case of the purchase of a residence or contract in the case of the construction of a residence;
(ii) evidence proving the construction, in the case of construction of a residence;
(iii) architectural plans of the residence (elevations, 1/100 scale block plan, area measurement)
(iv) any evidence proving that the beneficiary uses the residence as a place of residence, which may be:
(1) Copy of telephone bill,
(2) copy of water supply bill;
(3) copy of electricity bill,
(4) copy of municipal or community tax bill,
(5) any other evidence proving that the beneficiary uses the residence as his main and permanent place of residence:
Provided that, the information referred to in paragraph (iv) is submitted to the Registrar and is an integral part of the application, within twelve (12) months from the time the beneficiary acquires possession of the residence;
(v) any other elements, which are to support the request and which are deemed necessary when examining the request for a reduced tax rate for the acquisition of a residence;
(vi) an exact copy of the application submitted to the "competent authority" to secure a planning permit duly deemed to have been received by the "competent authority" or where this is not required, a copy of the application submitted to the "competent authority" to secure a building permit duly deemed to have been received by the "competent authority" and
(vii) declaration by the contractor that he holds an annual license for the class and category of the corresponding construction or technical project in accordance with the Law on Registration and Control of Contractors, Construction and Technical Projects.
The Superintendent may determine any further details regarding the application of this Table in a notification published in the Official Gazette of the Republic.
A married person may apply for a reduced tax rate for the construction or purchase of a single residence:
Provided that, when a declaration is submitted by a married person in accordance with this Table, a declaration by the spouse must be submitted at the same time that he/she does not own any other residence in the Republic which he/she uses as his/her main and permanent place of residence.
The entitled person receives a certified copy of the application that he submits to the Registrar, which he delivers to the seller of the residence or, in the case of the construction of a residence, to the contractor who undertakes the construction of the residence, when the seller or the contractor is obliged to impose a reduced rate of VAT.
A person who exercised the right to acquire a residence at a reduced VAT rate, pursuant to the provisions of this Table, or the repealed provisions of this Law, may exercise said right for the purposes of acquiring another residence before the expiration of ten (10) years, provided that he complies with the provisions of the tenth subparagraph of this Table and regardless of the provisions of article 63:
Provided that, in the event that it is later established that a person is not entitled to a reduced rate, in accordance with the provisions of this Table, he must pay the full amount of the tax.
In the event that a beneficiary ceases to use the residence as a place of residence before ten (10) years have passed, he must, within thirty (30) days from the date he has ceased to use it as a place of residence, notify the Registrar and pay the amount of the difference between the amounts of tax resulting from the application of the reduced rate and the normal rate, as they were in force at the time of delivery or construction of the residence, corresponding to the period of time that he has not use the residence for owner-occupation purposes, except in case of death of the beneficiary or in case of transfer by the beneficiary to any of his adult children, as long as said child is a beneficiary at the time of transfer.
For the purposes of this Table –
<Republic> means the Republic of Cyprus;
<beneficiary> means any natural person, who:
(i) has reached the age of eighteen (18) on the date on which he applies for the imposition of a reduced rate for the construction or delivery of a residence;
(ii) acquires a residence for use as a main and permanent place of residence in the Republic;
(iii) he does not have any other residence in the Republic, which he has acquired by imposing a reduced rate of VAT;
(iv) in case he has received any sponsorship for the acquisition of a residence based on the Special Sponsorship (Purchase or Construction of a Residence) Law, he has returned it as provided for in the provisions thereof.
<homestead> means living in a homestead;
<dwelling> means a specially designed and constructed, roofed building, which is used or intended to be used by the eligible person as a main and permanent place of residence in the Republic;
<large family> means a family with at least four children,
<first establishment> means the first use of the building after its delivery or erection, including occupancy, occupancy, lease or any other use which continues on a regular basis;
<first use> means the use or exploitation of the building after its handover or erection, which takes place systematically for a period of at least eighteen (18) months;
<total area> [Deleted].
<children> means the unmarried:
(j) legitimate children, who have not completed the eighteenth year of their age;
(ii) recognized illegitimate children, legally adopted children and unmarried ancestors who have not completed their eighteenth year of age; and
(iii) children who are permanently incapable of supporting themselves, who reside with the beneficiary.
SIXTH APPENDIX
(Article 25)
DELIVERIES OF GOODS AND PROVISIONS OF SERVICES SUBJECT TO
AT THE ZERO FACTOR
1. Services related to the importation of goods from outside the Member States, the value of which is included in the taxable value of the importation.
2. Services consisting of work carried out on movable goods which have for this purpose been acquired or received in any Member State, or imported into the interior of any Member State, and are then to be, and are actually exported to a place outside the Member States -
(a) by or on behalf of the service provider; or;
(b) where the recipient of the services belongs to a place outside the Member States, by or on behalf of the recipient.
3. (a) Deliveries, conversions, repairs, maintenance works, charters and leases of ships falling into the following categories:
(i) ships used in the navigation of the high seas and carrying passengers for hire or in which a commercial, industrial or fishing activity is carried on;
(ii) lifeboats and other maritime assistance vessels, as well as coastal fishing vessels.
(b) Deliveries, leases, repairs and maintenance works of objects - including fishing equipment - which are incorporated in the vessels of subparagraph (a) above or serve for their exploitation.
4. (a) Deliveries, conversions, repairs, maintenance, charters and leases of aircraft, which are used by airlines primarily engaged in international freight transport.
(b) Deliveries, leases, repairs and maintenance of items, which are incorporated into the aircraft of sub-paragraph (a) above or serve for their operation.
5. (a) Services to serve the immediate needs of the ships referred to in paragraph 3(a) of this Annex and the aircraft referred to in paragraph 4(a) of this Annex, in a port, airport or outside the Republic - excluding the leasing of goods.
(b) Services for the handling or storage of cargo, which transport the ships referred to in paragraph 3(a) of this Annex and the aircraft referred to in paragraph 4(a) of this Annex, to a port or airport - excluding the hire of goods.
(c) Navigation, rescue and towing services.
(d) Surveys and surveys of ships or aircraft and classifications of ships or aircraft for the purposes of any register, provided that they are of those referred to in paragraph 3(a) and 4(a) of this Schedule.
(e) Transport of passengers from within the Republic to abroad or vice versa, to the extent that the transport is carried out within the Republic.
(f) Transport of goods from a place within the Member States to a place outside the Member States or vice versa to the extent that the transport is provided within the Republic.
(g) Deliveries of goods intended to supply warships, falling under Combined Nomenclature (CN) code 8906 10 00, which leave their territory for a port or bay outside the Republic.
6. Services, including transport and ancillary work, but not services exempted under Article 26 and the Seventh Schedule, which are directly connected with—
(a) The export of goods outside the member states; or
(b) the importation of goods intended to be subject to the regimes of paragraph 10
7. Deliveries of gold to the Central Bank.
8. Deliveries of goods to non-profit organizations, which export them to a place outside the member states in the context of their humanitarian, charitable or educational activities outside the member states.
(8A) Intra-community transport services of goods within the meaning of the Value Added Tax (General) Regulations from time to time in force, from or to the islands that make up the autonomous regions of the Azores and Madeira or between these places, to the extent that the services are considered to be provided in the Republic.
9.
