Services provided by brokers or other intermediary persons, acting in the name and on behalf of another person, which concern the operations provided for in the previous paragraphs of this Annex or any Regulations issued pursuant to Article 25(5) and the operations taking place outside the Member States. The provision of services by travel agencies acting in the name and on behalf of travelers is excluded if they are carried out in another Member State.
10.(a) Deliveries of goods subject to import duty or excise duty or excise duty which take place after they have entered the Republic from a place outside the Member States but before they have received a customs destination.
(b) Deliveries and acquisitions of goods intended -
(i) be presented to customs and possibly placed in temporary storage warehouses;
(ii) be placed in a free zone or free warehouse;
(iii) be placed under customs warehousing or inward processing,
(iv) to be accepted in the territorial sea waters of the Republic, to be incorporated into drilling or exploitation rigs, for the purpose of their construction, repair, maintenance, retrofitting or equipment or for the connection of these rigs to land, as well as for the supply of these drilling or exploitation rigs,
(v) to be placed in a tax warehouse as provided for in the Law on Consumption Taxes in force at any given time, as long as they are products for which the Community provisions provide that they fall under the regime of tax warehouses of Council Directive 92/12/EOK of 25 February 1992 regarding the general status, possession, circulation and controls of products subject to excise taxes.
(vi) to be placed in areas under customs surveillance, supply warehouses based on Regulation (EC) no. 800/1999 of the Commission of 15 April 1999 on the common detailed rules for the implementation of the export license regime for agricultural products.
The regimes referred to in cases (i) to (v) are understood as defined by the customs legislation in force at any given time.
(c) Provision of services related to the deliveries and acquisitions of goods referred to in subparagraph (b) of this paragraph.
(d) Deliveries of goods and provision of services carried out:
(i) in the premises listed in sub-paragraph (b)(i) to (b)(iv), maintaining one of the situations mentioned in that sub-paragraph.
(ii) in the places listed in sub-paragraph (b)(n) while maintaining, within the Republic, the situation referred to in said sub-paragraph.
(e) Deliveries of goods:
(i) by maintaining temporary importation regimes, by full exemption from import duties or external transit, by maintaining the internal Community transit procedure;
(ii) as well as the provision of services directly related to these deliveries.
11. The provision of a specified travel service enjoyed outside the Community, to the extent that the service is enjoyed outside the Community.
12. The deliveries of goods made to the taxable person who facilitates through the use of an electronic interface, such as a market, platform, web portal or similar means, the delivery of goods in the Republic from a taxable person not established within the Community to a non-taxable person, in accordance with article 42H.
13. Deliveries of baby milk.
14. Deliveries of baby diapers and adult diapers.
15. Products for feminine hygiene protection.
16. Typewriters with Braille characters and special electronic typewriters (electronic pocket communication devices) of CN Code ex. 84.69, as well as the new type embossing typewriters of Code CN ex. 8472 90 80, for people with disabilities.
17. Armchair type carriages and other vehicles for disabled persons, whether or not with a motor or other propulsion mechanism of CN Code 87.13, provided they are intended exclusively for personal use by disabled persons.
18. [Deleted]
19. [Repealed]
20. [Repealed]
21. [Repealed]
22. [Repealed]
23. [Repealed]
24. Delivery of in vitro diagnostic medical devices and vaccines for the covid-19 disease.
This paragraph applies only to in vitro diagnostic medical devices for the disease COVID-19 that comply with the applicable requirements of Directive 98/79/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council or Regulation (EU) 2017/746 of the European Parliament and of the Council and with other applicable Union legislation and only for vaccines against the disease COVID-19 that have been approved by the Commission or by the Member States. [S.S.: This provision comes into effect from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Republic, from April 1 to May 31, 2023.].
(25) (a) Fresh or chilled potatoes falling within CN codes ex 0701, fresh or chilled tomatoes falling within CN codes 0702, onions, shallots, garlic, leeks and other similar vegetables, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0703; cabbages, cauliflowers and broccoli, curly cabbages, turnips fresh or chilled falling within CN codes ex 0704, lettuce (Lactuca sativa) and radishes (Cichorium spp.), fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0705, carrots, turnips, salad radishes, scallions, turnips, radishes and similar edible roots, fresh or chilled of CN heading 0706, cucumbers and gherkins, fresh or chilled of CN heading 0707, leguminous vegetables, with or without pods, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0708, aubergines, celery, mushrooms, peppers, spinach, artichokes, unprepared olives, pumpkins, courgettes, lettuce other than lettuce (coriander, parsley, scallions, rocket, cabbages), capers, fennel fresh or chilled falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0709.'
(b) Fresh bananas falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0803, fresh figs, pears of the avocat variety falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0804, fresh citrus fruits falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0805, fresh grapes falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0806, fresh melons and watermelons falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0807, fresh apples and pears falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0808, fresh apricots, peaches, cherries, nectarines and plums falling within the codes of heading ex CN 0809, fresh strawberries and kiwis falling are classified in the codes of class ex CN 0810.
[S.S.: The following paragraphs (c) and (d) which were added with the K.D.P. 168/2026, E.E. Par. III(I), No. 6012, 27.3.2026, valid from 6 April 2026 until 30 September 2026]:
(c) Meat of bovine animals, fresh or chilled falling within CN codes 0201, Meat of bovine animals, frozen falling within CN codes 0202, Meat of swine, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes 0203, Meat of sheep or of goats, fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes ex 0204, edible offal, bovine, porcine, ovine, caprine fresh, chilled or frozen falling within CN codes ex 0206, meat and offal edible, fresh, chilled or frozen, of poultry of heading 0105 falling within CN codes of heading 0207, other edible meat and offal, fresh, chilled or frozen, of rabbits, hares falling within CN headings ex 0208.
(d) Fresh, chilled or frozen fish falling within CN codes 0302, 0303 and 0304.
SEVENTH APPENDIX
(Article 26)
EXCEPTIONAL DEALS
Table A: Exemptions of certain activities of general interest
1. Provision of services by the Department of Postal Services and the subsequent delivery of goods.
2. Hospital and medical treatment, as well as closely related supplies of goods and services, performed by public hospitals or private hospitals registered in the register maintained by the Department of Medical Services in accordance with the Private Hospitals (Control) Law.
3. Medical care provided by—
(a) Physicians registered in the Medical Register provided for by the Law on the Registration of Physicians;
(b) dentists registered in the Dental Register provided for by the Law on the Registration of Dentists;
(c) nurses and midwives registered on the register maintained under the Nursing and Midwifery Act 1988;
(d) chiropractors registered in the register maintained under the Registration of Chiropractors Act 1991;
(e) physiotherapists registered in the register maintained under the Physiotherapists Registration Act 1988;
(f) clinical laboratories registered under the Registration and Operation of Clinical Laboratories Act 1988;
(g) podiatrists;
(h) dietitians and clinical dietitians registered in the Register maintained in accordance with the Registration of Food Scientists, Food Technologists and Dietitians and Other Related Matters Act of 1996 as amended or replaced from time to time;
(i) psychologists registered in the Register maintained in accordance with the Law on the Registration of Professional Psychologists, as amended or replaced from time to time;
(j) any persons holding other medical or paramedical specialties, which the Superintendent determines in a notification or otherwise and who provide medical treatment in the context of practicing medical and paramedical professions based on relevant legislation, which provides for the keeping of a Register.
4. Delivery of human organs, blood and human milk.
5. Provision of services by dental technicians registered in the register maintained in accordance with the Regulation of the Practice of the Dental Technicians Act of 1996, carried out in the context of their profession, as well as the delivery of dental prosthetics by dentists and dental technicians.
6. Provision of services by associations of persons to their members, whose members carry out transactions excluded or outside the scope of VAT, as long as these services are directly necessary for the exercise of the activities of the members and are provided against a contribution to the common costs, provided that this exemption does not create a risk of distorting the conditions of competition.
7. Provision of services and delivery of goods closely related to social welfare and insurance, including those provided by nursing homes and carried out by non-profit organizations.
8. Provision of services and delivery of goods, which are closely related to the protection of children and young people and are carried out by non-profit organizations or by Nurseries and Child Care Centers registered in the register maintained in accordance with the Children's Law.
9. Education of all levels, vocational training, training, retraining, as well as the provision of services and supplies of goods closely related thereto, provided by—
(a) Public schools;
(b) private schools and boarding schools registered in the register maintained under the Private Schools and Boarding Schools Act 1971;
(c) private schools of higher education registered in the register maintained under the Schools of Higher Education Act 1996;
(d) dance schools;
(e) conservatories and music schools;
(f) examination centers;
(g) Recognized maritime training schools under the Merchant Shipping (Issue and Recognition of Certificates and Maritime Education) Act 2008.
(h) persons, organizations or other institutions that are recognized by the Commissioner upon application on their behalf for the following services, insofar as they relate to the services specified in subparagraph (c):
(i) Training services provided by educational institutions specified in subparagraphs (a), (b) and (c) to students of higher education, when provided in the context of the students' studies and closely related to their education.
(ii) Clinical practice of higher education students in public hospitals and private hospitals registered in the register maintained by the Department of Medical Services in accordance with the Private Hospitals (Control) Law, when provided in the context of the students' studies and is closely related to their education.
(iii) courses taught by visiting professors at private schools of higher education, specified in subparagraph (c);.
(iv) any other services related to the above exemption and approved by the Tax Commissioner, provided that the exemption does not create a risk of distorting the conditions of competition. [S.S.: Paragraph (h) was added by the Value Added Tax (Amendment of the Seventh Schedule) Ordinance of 2019, C.D.P. 385/2019].
(i) educational organizations, which, in accordance with the provisions of the Law on Vehicle Technicians, are authorized to provide an educational program, organize relevant examinations, as well as to grant a certificate of basic vehicle technician training.
(j) Organizations approved by the Department of Civil Aviation for obtaining a professional qualification as determined on the basis of European Regulations (EC) 1139/2018, 1321/2014 and 1178/2011, and regarding professional licenses:
i. Aircraft Engineering (PART-66)
ii. Aircraft Pilots (PART-FCL CPL or ATPL and related qualifications and certificates)
iii. Cabin Crew Attestations. [S.S.: Paragraph (j) was added by the Value Added Tax (Amendment of the Seventh Schedule) Ordinance 2021, C.D.P. 229/2021].
(k) Certified training providers including vocational training centers, by the Cyprus Human Resources Development Authority.
(l) Theater Schools and Theater Workshops. [S.S.: Force of the Value Added Tax (Amendment of the Seventh Schedule) Decree of 2024, C.D.P. 95/2024]
(m) Training providers of the knowledge subjects (fields) to acquire knowledge of professional competence as defined in the Auditors Law, and lead to the acquisition of a professional title of the body of Auditors recognized by the Council of Ministers.
10. Special courses delivered by teachers and related to school or university education.
11. Availability of personnel from religious or philosophical institutions for the activities provided for in paragraphs 2, 7, 8 and 9 of this Annex and for purposes of spiritual assistance;
12. Provision of services and the closely related delivery of goods by organizations of a non-profit nature, which pursue political, trade union, religious, patriotic, philosophical, charitable or civil education purposes, to their members in the context of their collective interest in exchange for assistance, provided that this exception does not create a risk of distorting the conditions of competition.
13. Provision of services closely related to sports or physical education by non-profit organizations to persons engaged in sports or physical education.
14. Provision of services of a cultural nature and the closely related delivery of goods from—
(a) Organizations under public law;
(b) other cultural organizations of a non-profit nature, whose administration and management are exercised essentially without salary by persons who have no direct or indirect interest in their activities,
provided that this exemption does not create a risk of distorting the terms of competition to the detriment of other taxable persons.
For the purposes of this paragraph "services of a cultural nature" include—
(a) The services provided to the public by libraries, archives and record keeping centers;
(b) entry to museums, galleries, monuments, historical sites, botanical and zoological gardens;
(c) theatrical, musical, choreographic and cinematographic performances; and
(d) the organization of exhibitions and lectures.
15. Provision of services and delivery of goods by the persons referred to in paragraphs 2, 7, 8, 9, 12, 13 and 14 of this Annex on the occasion of events organized by them for their financial support, provided that this exception does not create a risk of distorting the conditions of competition.
16. Transportation of sick or injured by ambulance vehicles.
17. The transactions of the Cyprus Radio Foundation, excluding those of a commercial nature.
Table B: Other exceptions
1. Insurance and reinsurance operations, including related services performed by insurance agents, brokers and broker agents.
2. (a) Supply of goods that were used exclusively for exempt transactions, provided that their acquisition did not give rise to a right to deduct input tax.
(b) Delivery of goods whose acquisition or import did not create a right to deduct input tax pursuant to Regulations issued pursuant to subsection 7 of article 20 of this Law.
3. The following financial services:
(a) The granting and negotiation of the following credit facilities as well as their management by the person granting them:
(i) grant of any loan-
(ii) advance against bill of lading;
(iii) discounting of a bill of exchange;
(i) the granting of any credit, including credit by a person, in connection with the delivery of goods or the provision of services by him, for which a separate charge is made which is disclosed to the person to whom the goods are delivered or the services are rendered;
(n) granting of credit financing facilities repayable in installments in a hire purchase, conditional sale or sale on credit, for which facilities a separate charge is made which is disclosed to the person to whom the goods are delivered;
(b) the granting of any financial guarantee or the assumption of any other financial responsibility or obligation and the negotiation for the undertaking of these tasks, as well as the management of credit guarantees by the person granting them;
(c) the operations, including negotiation, of accepting deposits, current debit accounts, handling money, issuing and managing payment instruments (including credit cards, traveller's checks and banker's cheques) and other securities, excluding operations—
(i) collection of third party claims,
(ii) business claims agency and
(iii)processing, clearing and authorization services for the movement of funds in connection with credit cards;
(d) operations, including negotiation, involving foreign exchange, banknotes and coins used as legal tender, excluding collectibles. Gold, silver or other metal coins, as well as banknotes, which are not normally used in their capacity as legal tender or which are of collectible interest, are also considered to be collectible;
(e) business, including trading but excluding custody and management, relating to shares, debentures, debentures, corporate and other securities and rights thereon, public securities, shares and other securities or securities, excluding—
(i) the documents of title to goods, and
(ii) of real estate registration papers;
(f) the management of mutual funds.
4. Delivery at face value of stamps in circulation, banknotes and other similar insignia.
5. Delivery of lotteries and betting slips for horse racing or football matches, acceptance of bets and other games of chance.
6. The introduction of gold by the Central Bank.
EIGHTH APPENDIX
(Article 39)
REAL ESTATE
1. The following transactions are excluded transactions:
(a) lease of immovable property, excluding -
(i) the provision of accommodation within the hotel or similar sector, including accommodation in campsites,
(ii) the lease of real estate or spaces for parking vehicles;
(iii) the lease of permanently installed equipment and machinery,
(iv) the rental of safe deposit boxes, and
(n) the lease of the immovable property specified in paragraphs 1(b) to 4 of this Schedule, with right of redemption,
(vi) the leasing and/or renting of immovable property to a taxable person for the purpose of carrying out a taxable business activity, excluding the case of leasing immovable property which is used as a residence:
Provided that, the taxation covers the lease and/or rental of immovable property whose lease and/or rental start date takes place on or after the entry into force of the Value Added Tax (Amendment) (No. 3) Law of 2017:
It is further provided that, a lessor may choose to inform the Tax Commissioner of the non-taxation of the lease and/or rental of immovable property, under terms and conditions specified in a notification of the Tax Commissioner, which is published in the Official Gazette of the Republic:
Provided further that, the lessor's initial choice for non-taxation of the lease and/or rental of the immovable property based on the provisions of the second proviso may not be varied by the lessor later.
(vii) the case referred to in subparagraph (f) of paragraph 3 of the Second Schedule.
(b) delivery of immovable property excluding the following transactions:
(i) transfer of buildings or their parts and the plot of land that is transferred together with them or of an undivided ideal share on them, as long as it takes place before the first installation on them,
(ii) transfer of possession of buildings or parts thereof and the plot of land that is transferred together with them or of an undivided ideal share in them, by virtue of a contract for their sale or by virtue of an agreement that expressly provides that the buildings will be transferred together with the plot of land that is transferred together with them at some time in the future or by virtue of their lease with right of redemption, if it takes place before the first settlement in them,
(iii) transfer of possession, transfer of an undivided ideal share, transfer of possession by virtue of a contract or agreement of sale or by virtue of an agreement which expressly provides that ownership will be transferred at a future time, or by virtue of a lease agreement with the right to purchase undeveloped building land which is intended for the construction of one or more fixed structures and is carried out in the context of an economic activity:
Provided that, the Council of Ministers has the authority to issue Regulations, which are approved by the House of Representatives, that determine the category of land parcels that fall under the concept of undeveloped buildable land, as well as the point in time that is considered as the time of making this transfer:
Provided further that the provisions of sub-paragraph (iii) shall not include the delivery of land -
(a) Which has been transferred based on the provisions of the Transfer and Mortgage of Real Estate Law before the entry into force of the Value Added Tax (Amendment) (No. 3) Law of 2017; or
(b) for which a contract of sale has been filed with the competent authority for the purposes of the Sale of Real Estate (Special Execution) Law or with the Commissioner of Taxation prior to the entry into force of the Value Added Tax (Amendment) (No. 3) Law of 2017.
2. For the purposes of this Law, buildings are considered to be buildings in general and any kind of construction connected to the buildings or to the ground in a fixed and permanent way.
3. For the purposes of this Annex,
(a) <first establishment> means the first use of the building after its delivery or erection, including occupancy, occupancy, lease or any other use which continues on a regular basis.
(b) <first use> means the use or exploitation of the building after its handover or erection, which takes place systematically for a period of at least eighteen (18) months.
4. (a) The provisions of paragraphs 1(b)(i) and (ii), 2 and 3 of this Annex do not apply to buildings for which a duly completed application for the issuance of a planning permit has been submitted before May 1, 2004.
(b) In the case of buildings for which the filing of a planning permit is not required pursuant to a General or Special Development Decree, the provisions of paragraphs 1(b) I and II, 2 and 3 of this Annex apply to buildings for which a duly completed application for the issuance of a building permit has been filed after May 1, 2004.
For the purposes of this Schedule-
"planning permit" means the planning permit issued in accordance with the currently applicable Law on the issuance of planning permits
"building permit" means the building permit issued in accordance with the currently applicable Building Permit Law.
NINTH APPENDIX
(Article 31)
TRANSACTIONS OF PUBLIC AUTHORITIES RELATING TO TH
WHICH
THEY ARE CONSIDERED AS PERSONS SUBJECT TO TAX
1. Telecommunications
2. Delivery of water, gas, electricity and thermal energy.
3. Transport of goods.
4. Provision of port and airport services.
5. Transportation of persons.
6. Delivery of new goods manufactured for sale.
7. Transactions of agricultural organizations, including boards of trade and committees, relating to agricultural products.
8. Exploitation of commercial fairs and exhibitions.
9. Storage.
10. Commercial Advertising Agency Transactions.
11. Travel Agency Transactions.
12. Operation of staff gas stations, cooperatives, canteens and similar outlets.
13. Transactions of radio and television organizations, of a commercial nature.
TENTH APPENDIX
(Article 43)
ADMINISTRATION, COLLECTION AND ENFORCEMENT
FULFILLMENT OF OBLIGATIONS
Performance of VAT and payment of VAT
1.—(1) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may require the keeping of accounts and the submission of tax returns in such form and manner as may be specified in the Regulations.
(2) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may require submission to the Commissioner by taxable persons, at such times and intervals, in such cases and in such form and in such a manner as may -
(a) set out in the Regulations; or
(b) determined by the Commissioner in accordance with the powers conferred on him under the Regulations,
returns containing such particulars of transactions involving taxable persons and which transactions involve the movement of goods between Member States, and of the persons involved in such transactions, as may be specified in the Regulations.
(2A) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may provide for cases where -
(a) any goods subject to excise duty or relating to new means of transport are acquired in the Republic from another Member State by any person;
(b) the acquisition of the goods is a taxable acquisition and is not in accordance with a taxable transaction; and
(c) such person is not a taxable person at the time of the acquisition;
as required by the person acquiring the goods to serve on the Commissioner such notice in respect of the acquisition, and as required by any VAT. upon acquisition to be paid, at such time and in such form and in such manner as may be specified in the Regulations.
(2B) Regulations under this paragraph may provide in relation to notification under paragraph (2A) -
(a) contain such details of the acquisition as are disclosed in the notice and any VAT; levy on acquisition as may be specified in the regulations; and
(b) be served in specified cases, by a personal representative, trustee in bankruptcy, receiver, administrator, liquidator or person otherwise acting in a representative capacity in relation to the person making the acquisition.
(3) Regulations under this paragraph may make special arrangements for such taxable supplies of goods or services by retailers as may be determined by or under the Regulations and, in particular—
(a) Allow the value taken to be the value of transactions in any specified tax period or part thereof to be determined, subject to any limitations, by a method described in any notice of the Commissioner published in the Official Gazette under the Regulations or as may be agreed with the Commissioner; and
(b) determine the proportion of transactions attributable to any description of transactions and
(c) adjust that value and ratio for periods consisting of two or more specified tax periods or parts thereof.
(4) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may provide, in the cases and subject to the conditions determined by or based on the Regulations, that the VAT in relation to a transaction may be attributed and paid with reference to the time when the consideration for the transaction is received; and any such Regulations may make such amendments to the provisions of this Act (including in particular, but without prejudice to the generality of the power, the provisions as to the time when, and the circumstances under which, a deduction of input tax is allowed) which the Cabinet deems necessary or expedient.
(5) Regulations under this paragraph may provide that, in such cases and under such conditions as may be determined by or on the basis of the Regulations.
(a) VAT in relation to any supply of dutiable goods by a taxable person, or
(b) VAT in relation to the acquisition of dutiable goods by any person from another Member State, may be attributed and paid, and any matter relating to the inclusion of any excise duty or import duty in determining the value of the transaction or acquisition, with reference to the time when any excise duty or excise duty becomes chargeable with reference to such later time as the Commissioner may allow.
(5A) Regulations under this paragraph may provide for the time at which any invoice described in Regulations made for the purposes of section 9(8)(b) or 12C(1)(b) is deemed to have been issued and provide for the rendering and payment of VAT, with reference to the date of issue of such invoice, to be limited to VAT. on only so much of the value of the transaction or acquisition as shown on the invoice.
(5) The Registrar may specify in his notice reasonable requirements that he considers necessary to ensure that the information contained in any documents is made immediately available to him as if the records themselves had been preserved.
(6) Regulations under this paragraph may provide—
(a) That VAT chargeable in a specified tax period is deemed to be chargeable in another such period; and
(b) the manner in which entries are made to the accounts for adjustment purposes, whether to correct errors or otherwise; and
(c) to make adjustments in respect of entries made to the accounts for the purpose referred to in paragraph (b) above.
(7) Regulations under this paragraph may provide differently for different situations and may provide for different dates as the beginning of the specified tax periods applicable to different persons.
(8) The provisions of the Regulations issued pursuant to this paragraph concerning cases in which goods are deemed to have been delivered by a taxable person pursuant to paragraph 5 of the Second Schedule may require that VAT be attributed and paid. the obligor upon delivery, and to be furnished with particulars of him, by another person and in such manner as may be specified in the Regulations.
(9) When, at the end of a specified tax period, the amount of VAT due by any person or the amount of any VAT credit balance. would be less than one pound, that amount is treated as zero.
VAT invoices
1A.-(1) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may require a taxable person to ensure the issuance of an invoice (hereinafter referred to as a "VAT invoice"), by him, his client or on his behalf and on his behalf for the deliveries of goods and the provision of services to another person, established in the Republic, in another member state or in a third country, or VAT receipts. for deliveries of goods and/or provision of services to another person in the Republic.
(2) The VAT invoice must contain -
(a) such particulars of the transaction and of the persons by and to whom the goods are delivered or the services rendered;
(b) indication of whether VAT is chargeable. on the transaction under this Law or the law of another member state;
(c) such details of any VAT. who can be imposing,
as may be specified in Regulations.
(3) Regulations may give power to the Commissioner to permit a variation of the requirements of any Regulations in respect of the particulars to be contained in a VAT invoice.
(4) Regulations may -
(a) require that the VAT invoice required to be delivered in connection with a transaction of any description, to be delivered within a specified time after the time at which the transaction is deemed to take place or at such time before the time at which the transaction is deemed to take place, as may be specified in the Regulations.
(b) allow a VAT invoice to be issued. at a time later than that required by the Regulations when issued in accordance with general or special instructions of the Treasurer.
(5) Regulations may -
(a) to provide for the manner in which a VAT invoice is delivered, including provisions specifying conditions which must be met in the event that the VAT invoice issued by a third party on behalf of the supplier;
(b) determine the conditions that must be met in the case of a VAT invoice. which relates to more than one transaction;
(c) to provide, in relation to a document or message referred to in a VAT invoice. and aims to amend such provisions corresponding to those that may be provided for a VAT invoice, at the discretion of the Director.
(6) Regulations may empower the Commissioner to require a person who has received a VAT invoice to in the Republic, which is not, or part of which is not expressed in an official language of the Republic, to provide a translation of the invoice, or part of the invoice in an official language of the Republic.
(7) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph -
(a) provide for the place and manner of storage of VAT invoices.
(b) set conditions for the storage of VAT invoices. outside the Republic.
(8) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph -
(a) may be issued to apply only to particular cases or only in relation to transactions with persons of a particular description;
(b) may provide differently for different cases.
Self-billing
1B.-(1) This paragraph applies where a taxable person delivers to himself a document (hereinafter referred to as a "self-invoicing invoice") which is considered a VAT invoice in relation to supplies of goods or services to him by another taxable person.
(2) Subject to such conditions as may -
(a) be required;
(b) be specified in a Notice of the Commissioner published in the Official Gazette of the Republic;
(c) imposed in a specific case by virtue of any Regulations, a self-invoicing invoice shall be deemed to be the VAT invoice. which is required to be delivered pursuant to Regulations based on paragraph 1A.
(3) For the purposes of Article 9(4), a self-invoicing invoice is not deemed to be issued by the person carrying out the transaction.
(4) For the purposes of section 9(5) and (6), a self-invoicing invoice in respect of which the conditions specified in subsection (2) are satisfied and subject to any additional conditions that may be specified, shall be deemed to be issued by the person carrying out the transaction.
In such event, any notice or request given for the purposes of section 9(5) or (6) by the person providing the self-invoicing invoice shall be deemed for those purposes to have been given by the person effecting the transaction.
(5) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph -
(a) may be designed to apply only in particular cases or only in relation to transactions with persons of a particular description;
VAT invoices transmitted electronically
means and storage of VAT invoices
2.-(1) Regulations may require, or provide that the Commissioner imposes in any case, conditions which must be satisfied in relation to -
(a) the transmission by electronic means of any item to which this paragraph applies.
(b) maintaining and maintaining by electronic means any such item or information contained in any such item.
(2) The items to which this paragraph applies are -
(a) any VAT invoice;
(b) any document referred to in the VAT invoice. and intended to amend it;
(c) any tariff referred to in regulations made for the purposes of section 9(8)(b) or 12C(1)(b).
(3) Regulations pursuant to this paragraph may provide differently for different cases.
(2) Compliance with a provision related to VAT invoices is not considered. the introduction by computer of any material other than a document, or the delivery of such material so produced or the transmission referred to in subsection (1) above, unless the person producing or delivering the material or making the transmission and, in the case of such material delivered or transmitted, the person receiving it—
(a) Has given the Commissioner at least one month's notice in writing that he intends to produce or deliver such material or make such transmission or, as the case may be, receive such material or transmissions; and
(b) comply with such requirements as may be specified in the Regulations or as the Commissioner may from time to time impose in his case.
Power to demand security and produce proof
3. —(1) The Commissioner may require, as a condition of allowing or refunding any input tax to any person, the production of documents relating to VAT. which may have been delivered to that person and may, if it considers it necessary for the protection of the public revenue, require, as a condition of accepting any VAT credit balance, the provision of security for such amount of payment as it considers appropriate.
(2) Without prejudice to his authority pursuant to article 37(7), when the Commissioner deems it necessary for the protection of public revenue, he may require a taxable person, as a condition for that person to deliver goods or provide services in a taxable transaction, to provide a guarantee or further guarantee, in such an amount and in a manner that he may determine, for the payment of any VAT. which is or may become due therefrom.
Recovery of VAT
4. —(1) VAT due from any person is collectable as a debt due to the Republic and any proceedings in court for the collection of the debt shall be brought in the name of the Treasurer.
(2) When a VAT invoice or receipt shows that a supply of goods or services is subject to VAT. levied on it, is recoverable from the person who issued the invoice an amount equal to that shown on the invoice as VAT. or if no VAT is presented separately, to so much of the total amount shown as payable as is taken to represent VAT. on the transaction.
(3) Subsection (2) above applies regardless of whether—
(a) The invoice is a VAT invoice. or the receipt is a VAT receipt issued in accordance with paragraph 1(1) above; or
(b) the transaction shown on the invoice actually receives or has received the country or the amount shown as VAT. or any amount of VAT, is or was leviable on the transaction; or
(c) the person issuing the invoice is a taxable person, or not;
and any amount chargeable by a person under subsection (2), if in any case VAT, is chargeable as VAT. and is otherwise recoverable as a debt due to the Republic.
(4) [Deleted].
(5) [Deleted].
Prescription of Conductable Right
4A. The enforceable right of the Treasurer to claim any amount owed by any person, in accordance with the provisions of this Law, is barred after a period of six years from:
(a) the date on which the Commissioner has received a tax return for a specified tax period, in accordance with Regulations made under this Act, but the amount of VAT shown in the tax return as payable by the taxable person in respect of that tax period has not been paid, or
(b) the date on which a taxable person was required to submit a tax return for a specified tax period, in accordance with Regulations issued under this Law, but such return was not submitted and the amount of VAT due in respect of that tax period was not paid, or
(c) the date on which any amount which the Commissioner has certified under the provisions of sections 49 and 49A is notified, or
(d) the date on which a taxable person had an obligation to pay to the Commissioner the annual flat-rate amount of VAT, pursuant to the provisions of article 42D and the Regulations issued pursuant to this Law and has not done so, or
(e) the date on which a taxable person has failed to comply with any obligation imposed under the provisions of this Law to pay a pecuniary charge, additional tax or interest:
It is provided that, in the case of an appeal in accordance with the provisions of Article 146 of the Constitution, the time period between the appeal and the issuance of a final decision is not included in the statute of limitations of the Commissioner's right to conduct:
It is further provided that, if in any case more than one of the above sub-paragraphs (a) to (e) apply, with the result that the Conductor's right is barred on different dates, then the Conductor's right is barred at the end of the later time period.
Record keeping duty
5.—(1) Every taxable person shall keep such records as may be required by Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers and every person who, during the time when he is not a taxable person, acquires in the Republic from another Member State any goods subject to excise duty or relating to new means of transport, shall keep records in relation to the acquisition, if it is a taxable acquisition and is not in accordance with a taxable transaction, as may be to be required by the Regulations.
(2) Regulations pursuant to subsection (1) above may provide differently for different cases and may be formulated with reference to files that may be specified in a notification by the Treasurer published in the Official Gazette of the Republic pursuant to said Regulations.
(3) The Regulations referred to in the above subparagraph may require that any documents kept under this paragraph shall be kept for a period not exceeding seven years.
(4) The obligation under this paragraph to keep records may be satisfied by preserving the information contained therein by such means as the Superintendent may approve; and where such information is so preserved, a copy of any document forming part of the records shall, subject to subsection (5) below, be admissible in evidence in any proceeding, whether civil or criminal, to the same extent as the records themselves.
(5) The Commissioner may impose, as a condition of approving under subsection (4) above any means of preserving information contained in any records, such reasonable requirements as he considers necessary to ensure that the information will be made immediately available to him as if the records themselves had been preserved.
(6) A statement contained in a document produced by a computer is not admissible as evidence in civil or criminal proceedings except in accordance with the provisions of the Evidence Law.
(7) Every person, other than a taxable person, who supplies goods or renders services in the course of or in furtherance of any business carried on by him, shall for a period of seven years maintain—
(a) Any invoices and receipts issued to it in relation to transactions with it for the purposes of its business;
(b) any invoices and receipts issued by the person himself in relation to his dealings; and
(c) any customs declarations and other documents in relation to its imports and exports.
5A.-(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers pursuant to this paragraph may require payment service providers, as defined in said Regulations, to keep records, submit them to the Tax Commissioner and determine the manner of keeping them, as well as their content.
(2) Regulations issued pursuant to subsection (1) may provide for different arrangements for different cases and may be formulated with reference to records that may be specified in a notification by the Commissioner, which is published in the Official Gazette of the Republic pursuant to said Regulations.
(3) The Regulations provided for in sub-paragraph (1) may determine the time period for keeping records, in accordance with the provisions of this paragraph.
(4) The obligation to keep records pursuant to this paragraph may be fulfilled by maintaining the information contained in them by means that the Commissioner may determine in his notification and when said information is preserved in this way, a copy of any document that forms part of the records, subject to the provisions of subsection (5), is accepted as evidence in any proceeding, whether civil or criminal, to the same extent that the records themselves are accepted.
Providing information and bringing documents
6.—(1) Regulations issued by the Council of Ministers may provide that taxable persons are required to notify the Registrar of any details of changes concerning the persons themselves or concerning any business carried out by them, which the Registrar deems necessary for the purposes of updating the VAT Register.
(2) Any person involved (in any capacity) in the delivery of goods or provision of services within the framework of or in furtherance of a business or to whom such transaction is carried out, any person involved, in any capacity, in the acquisition of goods from another Member State and any person involved, in any capacity, in the importation of goods from outside the Member States in the context of or in furtherance of business—
(a) furnish to the Commissioner, within such time and in such manner as he may reasonably require, such information relating to the goods or services or to the transaction, acquisition or importation, as the Commissioner may reasonably determine; and
(b) must, on the demand of an authorized person, bring or cause to be brought for inspection by the authorized person—
(i) at the principal place of business of the person against whom the claim is made or at such other place as the authorized person may reasonably require, and#
(ii) at such time as the authorized person may reasonably require, any documents relating to the goods or services or the transaction, acquisition or importation;
(3) If, by virtue of subsection (2) above, an authorized person has power to require the production of any documents from any person referred to in that subsection, the authorized person has a similar power to require the production of those documents from any other person whom the authorized person considers to be in possession; but if any such other person claims a right of attachment any document brought therefrom shall be served without prejudice to the right of attachment.
(4) For the purposes of this paragraph, documents relating to the delivery of goods or the provision of services, the acquisition of goods from another Member State or the importation of goods from a place outside the Member States" and the addition before the period of the phrase "or, in the case of acquisition from another Member State), relating to any business or other activities of the person from whom the goods are acquired, are deemed to include any profit and loss account and balance sheet relating to the business in the context of which the goods are delivered or services are rendered or goods are imported.
(5) The authorized person may take copies or copy extracts of any document produced under subsection (2) or (3) above.
(6) If he deems it necessary, the authorized person may receive within a reasonable time and detain for a reasonable period any document brought under sub-paragraph (2) or (3) above and, if requested, issue proof of receipt of the documents; and where a lien is claimed on a document brought under sub-paragraph (3) above, the receipt of the document under this of subparagraph shall not be deemed to infringe the right of lien.
(7) When a document received by an authorized person pursuant to paragraph (6) above is reasonably required for the smooth conduct of the business, the authorized person shall deliver, within a reasonable time, a copy of the document, without any financial charge, to the person who brought it or caused it to be brought.
(8) Where any documents received under the powers conferred by this paragraph are lost or destroyed, the Commissioner shall reimburse the owner for any costs reasonably incurred in replacing or repairing the documents.
(8A) For the purposes of this paragraph, a person to whom a right has been conferred to receive all or part of consideration for the delivery of goods or the provision of services is deemed to be the person involved in the transaction.
(9) The Commissioner may require any state authority or local government authority or public law organization to provide him with information that may be necessary for the purposes of this Law.
(10) Any employee of a state authority, local government authority or public law organization having in the custody of the register, books, records or other documents, the examination of which may assist in the application of this Law, must allow any authorized person to examine them and take copies or copy extracts thereof, without payment of any fee or fee.
Sampling power
7. —(1) Any authorized person, if he deems it necessary for the protection of public revenue, may, at any time, take, from the goods in the possession of a person who delivers goods or acquires goods from another Member State, or in the possession of the tax warehouse, such samples as the authorized person may require in order to determine in what manner the goods or the materials from which they were made had to be handled for VAT purposes.
(2) Any sample taken under this paragraph shall be disposed of in such manner as the Commissioner may determine.
(3) Where a sample is taken under this paragraph from the goods in the possession of any person and is not returned to him within a reasonable time and in good condition, the Commissioner shall compensate the said person with an amount equal to the cost of the sample incurred.
Entry and search of real estate or places and persons
8. —(1) For the purposes of exercising any powers granted under this Law, an authorized person may at any reasonable time enter premises, or place, excluding residences, used in connection with the conduct of business.
(2) Where an authorized person has reasonable cause to believe that real estate or place is being used in connection with taxable supplies of goods or services, he may at any reasonable time enter and inspect said real estate or place, excluding dwellings, and inspect any goods and documents therein.
(3) Notwithstanding any other power conferred by this Act, if there is reasonable cause to believe that an offense relating to VAT is being committed, has been committed or is about to be committed. in any premises or place or that evidence of the commission of such offense shall be found there, then any authorized person may enter the said premises or place excluding dwellings, and search them.
(4) Without prejudice to the provisions of subsection (3) of any other power conferred by this Law, when a judge is satisfied by a written affidavit of an authorized person that there is reasonable cause to believe that an offense relating to VAT is being committed, has been committed or is about to be committed. in any premises or place or that evidence of the commission of such offense shall be found there, then the judge may issue a warrant authorizing such person or any other person named in the warrant to enter and search the premises or place named in the search warrant.
(5) Each search warrant bears the signature of the judge who issues it, the date and time of issuance, as well as the judge's certification that he is reasonably satisfied that there is a need to issue the warrant.
(6) Any person who enters premises or place by virtue of sub-paragraphs (3) and (4) may -
(a)seize and remove any documents or other articles on the premises or place which it has reasonable cause to believe may serve as evidence for the purposes of any legal proceedings; and
(b) search or cause to be searched any person on the premises or place whom he has reasonable cause to believe to be in possession of any such documents or other articles;
Provided that the in-person search will be conducted by an authorized officer of the same sex.
(7) A person who has authority based on this paragraph to enter premises, or a place, may use as much force as is reasonably necessary for the exercise of this authority.
Access to Registered Information Order
9.—(1) Where a judge, on the application of an authorized person, is satisfied that there is reasonable cause to believe that—
(a) Offense in relation to VAT. is committed, has been committed or is to be committed, and
(b) any recorded information (including any document of any kind) which may serve as evidence for the purposes of any proceedings in relation to the said offense is in the possession of any person, then he may make an order under this paragraph.
(2) An order under this paragraph constitutes an order that the person whom the judge finds to be in possession of registered information to which the application relates—
(a) Grant access to them to an authorized person, and
(b) permit an authorized person to receive and detain any of them which he thinks reasonably necessary, at such time as may be at the end of the period of 7 days beginning on the date of the order or such longer period as the order may specify.
(3) The reference in subsection (2)(a) above to granting an authorized person access to registered information to which the application relates includes a reference to granting permission to the authorized person to obtain copies or to copy extracts thereof.
(4) If the recorded information consists of information contained in a computer, an order under this paragraph has effect as an order to produce the information in a form that is visible and legible and, if the authorized person wishes to move it, in a form that can be moved.
(5) This paragraph does not affect paragraphs 6 and 8 above.
(6) Without prejudice to the preceding provisions of this paragraph, any authorized person shall have power, if he has reasonable cause to believe that an offense under section 46 or 47 is being committed or has been committed or is about to be committed, to require any person reasonably believed to be aware of the facts or incidents of the offense to attend his office or at any other reasonable place to examine and take evidence from him in relation to the offence, and in such case the provisions of sub-section (2) of section 5 of the Criminal Procedure Law shall apply mutatis mutandis.
Proof with a certificate, etc.
10.—(1) Certificate of the Treasurer certifying that—
(a) Any person was or was not, at any date, registered under this Act; or
(b) any tax return required by or under this Act has not been filed or has not been filed at any date; or
(c) any VAT shown as due in any tax return submitted or tax certificate issued or decision of the Minister of Finance made under this Act, has not been paid; or
(d) any pecuniary charge, interest or additional tax imposed by the Commissioner under this Act has not been paid, is sufficient evidence of the fact he asserts, until the contrary is proved;
(e) any statement or notice required to be made or served on the Commissioner under any Regulations by virtue of paragraph 1(2) or (2A) has not been made or served or had not been made or served at any date.
(2) A photograph of any document produced to the Registrar for the purposes of this Act and certified by him to be a photograph of the said document shall be admissible in evidence in any proceeding, whether civil or criminal, to the same extent as the document itself.
(3) Any document purporting to be a certificate by virtue of subsection (1) or (2) above shall be deemed to be such a certificate until the contrary is proved.
Debt cancellation
11. Debts to the Registrar created by the application of the provisions of this Law and which, due to the circumstances of the debtor, cannot be collected, are written off based on the provisions of the Accounting and Financial Management and Financial Control Law.
Registration of a lien on real property for tax due or any other amount
12.-(1)(a) In the event that any person refuses or omits or delays or neglects to pay to the Registrar the amount of tax owed by him or any other amount and exceeds five thousand euros (€5,000), excluding the tax or any other amount for which-
(i) all administrative and judicial procedures for its determination have not been exhausted; or
(ii) security has been provided for the payment of the amount due, as the Commissioner considers appropriate;
then the Registrar may make any immovable property of that person, worth up to twice the tax due or any other amount, and which is registered in his name in the books of the Provincial Land Office, as a guarantee for the payment of the tax due or any other amount.
(2)(a) Real property becomes security for the payment of the tax due or any other amount by depositing in the District Land Office of the Province where the property sought to be charged is located, a note dated and signed by the Commissioner or his agent authorized for that purpose, claiming that the taxable person's interest in the real property shall remain bound for the payment of the tax due or any other amount.
(b) The filing of the note pursuant to subparagraph (a) of subparagraph (2) shall constitute the registration of an encumbrance on the real property for purposes of the Transfer and Mortgage of Real Estate Law.
(3) Immediately after the registration of the encumbrance in accordance with subsection (2), the Registrar must serve a written notice to the taxable person for said registration.
(4) In the event that an encumbrance has been registered on a person's immovable property pursuant to subsection (2), the taxable person has the right within thirty (30) calendar days from the date of the written notice received pursuant to subsection (3)-
(a) either submit a written notice of objection to the Registrar, for reconsideration of the registration of the encumbrance, and the Registrar must decide on the objection within thirty (30) calendar days from the date of submission of the objection;
(b) either to request from the Court the issuance of a decision to remove the registration of the encumbrance
for the reason that-
(i) the taxable person previously paid the amount of tax due or any other amount and no longer owes; or
(ii) the choice of any of the measures provided for in paragraphs 13 and 14 would cause him less disfavor than the measure chosen by the Commissioner under this paragraph, without defeating the purpose of collecting the tax due or any other amount;
Provided that, in case of rejection of the objection by the Registrar, the taxable person reserves the right to request from the Court the issuance of a decision to remove the registration of the encumbrance for the aforementioned reasons:
It is further provided that, in the event that the Registrar accepts the objection or the Court issues a decision to remove the registration of the encumbrance, the Registrar, within fifteen (15) days from the day of acceptance of the objection or the issuance of the Court's decision, informs the Director of the Department of Cadastre and Survey to withdraw the registration of the encumbrance:
It is further provided that, in the event that an encumbrance has been registered based on the procedure provided for in this paragraph and the amount of tax due or any other amount has already been recovered based on the procedures provided for in paragraphs 13 and 14, then the Registrar within fifteen (15) days of the recovery of the due tax or any other amount requests the Director of the Department of Cadastre and Land Surveying to withdraw the registration encumbrance relating to the specific immovable property.
(5)(a) During the validity of the registration of the lien, the taxable person's interest in the property is charged with the payment of the tax due or any other amount in priority to all debts or obligations of the taxable person which were not specifically charged to the property before the filing of the Commissioner's note.
(b) Notwithstanding any transfer or mortgage made after the registration of the charge, the property, or so much of it as may be necessary to be sold in satisfaction of the tax due or any other sum, is ordered by the Court at any time while the registration remains in force, to be sold in satisfaction of the tax due or any other sum:
Provided that, as a remedy for any person in whose name it may have been transferred or to whom it may have been mortgaged, only the claim for damages remains against the person who transferred or mortgaged the property therein.
(5A) Irrespective of the provisions of any Law in force, in the event that a property is acquired by the lender in the context of restructuring, any encumbrance on said property, pursuant to the provisions of paragraph 12 of the Tenth Schedule, during the transfer from the borrower to the lender shall be transferred to the specific property:
Provided that, the Registrar may request the transfer of said encumbrance to another property belonging to the borrower, the value of which amounts to twice the amount of tax owed by the borrower, including interest and charges:
It is further provided that, the Registrar may, at his discretion, enter into an agreement with the borrower to settle the amounts of taxes due, including interest and charges for which the lien in question has been placed, so as to make it possible to exempt the property from the lien:
It is further provided that, the provisions of this sub-paragraph are applicable with regard to a borrower that is a company, as long as the following conditions are cumulatively met:
(a) The company is or is likely to become insolvent.
(b) No resolution regarding the liquidation of the company has been approved and published in the Official Gazette of the Republic.
(c) No decree for the winding up of the company has been passed.
For the purposes of this sub-paragraph, a company is deemed to be insolvent if-
(a) It is unable to repay its debts at the time they become payable,
(b) the value of its assets is less than the amount of its liabilities, taking into account its contingent and future liabilities:
Provided that, the provisions of this sub-paragraph apply to a borrower who is a natural person, as long as the following conditions are cumulatively met:
(a) Has his habitual residence in the Republic:
Provided that, a person who had his habitual residence in the Republic for at least three (3) years before the date of entry into force of the Value Added Tax (Amendment) Law of 2015 is not required to fulfill the condition of habitual residence at the time of the restructuring.
(b) He is insolvent.
(c) He is not an unrehabilitated insolvent.
(5AA) In the event that the Commissioner, in the context of restructuring, finds that an exemption from the obligation to pay tax or the imposition of zero tax based on the provisions of the Value Added Tax (Amendment) Law of 2015 is incorrect due to acts of the person which are proven not to be genuine or are fictitious, he has the authority, as despite the provisions of the above law, to proceed to impose on that person, the correct amount tax.
(6) The provisions of articles 58 to 72 of the Civil Procedure Law regarding the registration of a judicial decision shall apply, mutatis mutandis, to the registration of the lien by the Registrar pursuant to subsection (2) and the said registration of the lien shall be deemed to have the same effect as the registration of a judicial decision.
(7) The Council of Ministers may issue Regulations that determine the procedure for registering encumbrances on immovable property, the imposition and recovery of costs, charges and rights in relation to anything carried out for the purposes of this subsection.
Freeze and confiscation of amounts in bank accountss
13.-(1)(a) In the event that any person refuses or omits or delays or neglects to pay to the Registrar the amount of tax owed by him or any other amount and which exceeds three thousand euros (€3,000), excluding the tax or any other amount for which-
then the Commissioner, regardless of any provision of any law in force, including any legislative provision in relation to the observance of bank secrecy and with the written consent of the Attorney General of the Republic, may address credit institutions with a written, computerized, electronic or other notification and request the immediate seizure of any free and available amount belonging to the taxable person and which is deposited in bank accounts with one or more credit institutions and which does not exceed the amount of tax due and any other amount:
(b) In the event that the total amount pledged by virtue of sub-paragraph (a) of sub-paragraph (1) to one or more credit institutions exceeds the amount of tax due or any other amount, the Commissioner is obliged, within one (1) working day, to instruct the credit institutions to release the excess amount, keeping only the amount corresponding to the amount of tax due and any other amount withheld.
(2) It is prohibited for the Commissioner to freeze any free and available amount of money belonging to the taxable person, which leaves less than two thousand euros (€2,000) in the total bank accounts of the person in question.
(3) In the event that a frozen amount is deposited into an account of the taxable person, which is shared with another or other persons, then the credit institution informs these persons about the freezing of the amount;
For the purposes of this paragraph-
"free and available amount belonging to the taxable person" means-
(a) any monetary credit balance in any account with a credit institution and does not include any amount which is subject to a right of lien or other charge or the amount of a check which is pending for settlement or security in satisfaction of a claim from a court decision:
Provided that, a free and available amount is considered the credit balance that remains after the credit institution has exercised the rights or powers granted to it by law or by any relevant agreement it has entered into with the taxable person and proceeded to set off any credit balance of said person with a debt owed to the credit institution itself, before receiving the notice from the Registrar of the attachment; and
(b) amount, which is deposited in an account in the name of the taxable person, including an account shared with another person or persons, in which the defaulting person has the right against the credit institution for the return of the entire quantity of money without the complicity of the other persons, excluding customer accounts or accounts which the taxable person is declared to keep under his/her capacity as administrator, fiduciary, guardian, trustee, partner, member of the management of an association, club, institution or other organization with or without legal personality, representative or in any other capacity for the benefit and/or on behalf of a third person;
"credit institution" has the meaning assigned to this term by the Central Bank of Cyprus Law.
(4)(a) Any consultant, chief executive officer, manager, officer, employee or representative of a credit institution refrains from disclosing information regarding the Commissioner's notice and when such persons make such disclosure and/or any other act or omission, which adversely affects the possibility of collecting an amount of tax due or any other amount, then such person commits a criminal offense and in case of conviction is subject to a prison sentence of up to one (1) year or a fine of up to five thousand euros (€5,000) or both of these penalties.
(b) In the event that -
(i) a person referred to in sub-clause (a) is found guilty of the said criminal offence; or
(ii) it is not possible to collect the tax due or any other amount due to the non-sequestration of the amount of tax due or any other amount;
the credit institution is obliged to pay to the Registrar the amount of the tax due or any other amount which, as a result of the acts of the persons referred to in sub-sub-paragraph (a) of sub-paragraph (4), could not be collected.
(5) In the event that an amount has been frozen pursuant to this paragraph, the taxable person has the right within fifteen (15) calendar days from the date of the freezing of the amount-
(a) either to the Registrar with a written notice of objection, for reconsideration of the seizure of the amount; the Registrar must decide on the objection within fifteen (15) calendar days from the date of submission of the objection:
It is provided that, in case of rejection of the objection by the Registrar, the taxable person reserves the right to request from the Court the issuance of a decision to lift the seizure of all or part of the frozen amount;
(b) either apply directly to the Court for a decision to unfreeze all or part of the frozen amount;
for the reason that -
(ii) is not a free and available amount in accordance with the provisions of this Law;
(iii) the choice of any of the measures provided for in paragraphs 12 and 14 would cause him less disfavor than the measure chosen by the Commissioner under this paragraph, without defeating the purpose of collecting the tax due or any other amount.
(6) In case-
(a) the period provided for in subsection (5) elapses without an objection being lodged with the Registrar or an application being registered with the Court; or
(b) an objection has been lodged in accordance with sub-clause (a) of sub-clause 5 and the Commissioner has rejected the objection and the taxable person has not applied to the Court requesting the lifting of the attachment of all or part of the attached amount within fifteen (15) days; or
(c) an application has been made to the Court to unfreeze the frozen amount and a decision has been issued rejecting in whole or in part the claims raised in the application,
then the credit institution, following a relevant notification from the Treasurer, transfers the corresponding amount that it kept reserved, to the Permanent Fund of the Republic.
(7) In the event that the Registrar accepts the objection or the Court issues a decision to lift the pledge of all or part of the pledged amount, the Registrar gives instructions to the credit institution to lift the pledge of the corresponding amount.
(8) The Court may not, when examining the application for lifting the pledge of all or part of the pledged amount, examine the legality of the imposed taxation or the accuracy of the amount owed or any other amount.
(9) The credit institution does not collect any fees from any person for the handling of the process of seizing an amount and the transfer of the sequestered amount to the Permanent Fund of the Republic.
(10) The credit institution does not bear any responsibility towards any person for the actions it takes in accordance with the provisions of this paragraph.
(11) The Commissioner may determine, by notification in the Official Gazette of the Republic, the hours of the actions provided for in this paragraph, the manner and procedure of transferring the pledged amount to the Permanent Fund of the Republic, the type of notification that the Commissioner sends to credit institutions pursuant to sub-paragraph (a) of sub-paragraph (1) as well as any other procedural detail that needs to be determined for the application of this paragraph.
Confiscation of other movable property
14.-(1)(a) In the event that any person refuses or omits or delays or neglects to pay to the Registrar the amount of tax owed by him or any other amount and which exceeds three thousand euros (€3,000), excluding the tax for which-
then the property, excluding immovable property, of that person, valued up to twice the tax due or any other amount, is subject to confiscation pursuant to the provisions of this paragraph.
(2) Anything subject to confiscation under the provisions of this paragraph may be confiscated by the Commissioner or by any person authorized by him.
(3) For the purposes of exercising the authority of the Commissioner granted pursuant to this paragraph, a person authorized by him may, at any reasonable time, enter any property or place, excluding residences.
(4) After the end of the confiscation, the Superintendent or the person authorized by him, must deliver a written notice of the seizure of the property as subject to confiscation, stating also the reasons on which the seizure is based.
(5) After the lapse of thirty (30) days from the day on which the Registrar or the person authorized by him has seized the property as subject to confiscation, it shall be deemed to have been declared in confiscation, unless within the period of thirty (30) days a written challenge to the confiscation is submitted to the Registrar and in such a case, the Registrar must act so that a court order is issued for the subject of forfeiture, and, if the Court finds that the property when seized was actually subject to forfeiture, then the forfeiture thereof shall be judicially declared.
(6) The Council of Ministers may issue Regulations that determine the procedure, terms and conditions for the confiscation of property, as well as the powers and duties of the Commissioner and the persons authorized by him, in relation to the implementation of this paragraph.
15. For the purposes of paragraphs 12, 13 and 14, "any other amount" means interest, additional tax and/or pecuniary charges imposed under this Act.
With the entry into force of the Value Added Tax Law of 2014, the Commissioner, for the purposes of paragraphs 12, 13 and 14, shall submit quarterly to the House of Representatives, beginning on 30h September 2014, a progress report on the situation of immediately due amounts owed, which includes a relevant table that will show by category of amounts owed, the number of debtors and the corresponding total amount due, as well as the actions of the Value Added Tax Service pursuant to the provisions of paragraphs 12, 13 and 14.
ELEVENTH APPENDIX (Article 13C)
GOODS ENTITLED TO BE STORED IN
TAX WAREHOUSE
1. Fresh or chilled potatoes (S.O. 0701).
2. Olives (S.O. 071120).
3. Coconuts, Brazil nuts and cashew nuts, fresh or dry, whether or not in shell or shell. Other nuts, fresh or dried, whether or not in shell or skin (S.O. 0801 and 0802).
4. Unroasted coffee, with or without caffeine (S.O 0901 11 00 and 0901 12 00).
5. Tea, whether or not flavored (S.O. 0902).
6. Wheat and semolina, rye, barley, oats, corn. Rice with its husk (paddy rice). Grain sorghum. Edible buckwheat (black wheat), millet and long millet and other cereals (S.O. 1001, 1002, 1003, 1004, 1005, EX1006, 1007, 1008).
7. Soybean hulls, linseeds, turnip or rapeseeds, sunflower seeds and other seeds and oilseeds, all of the above whether or not broken. Peanuts, not roasted or otherwise prepared, whether or not shelled or cracked. Copra (S.O. 1201, 1202, 1203, 1204, 1205, 1206 and 1207).
8. Soybean oil, olive oil, palm oil, sunflower oil, safflower or cottonseed oil, safflower oil, safflower oil or babassu oil, rapeseed oil, rapeseed oil or mustard oil and their fractions, other oils and their fractions obtained exclusively from olives and mixtures of these oils or fractions with oils or fractions of heading 1509, all the above whether or not refined, but not chemically modified (S.O. 1507, 1508, 1509, 1510, 1511, 1512, 1513, 1514 and 1515).
9. Cane or beet sugar, in a solid state, raw, without added flavoring or coloring matter (S.O. 1701 11 and 1701 12).
10. Cocoa in seeds and fragments of seeds, raw or roasted (S.O. 1801).
11. Oils not refined from petroleum or bituminous minerals (crude oil). Oils from petroleum or from bituminous minerals, but from crude oils. Preparations not elsewhere specified or included, containing by weight 70% or more of oils from petroleum or bituminous minerals and in which these oils form the main ingredient. Liquefied propane and butanes (headings 2709, 2710 and 2711 12 and 2711 13).
12. Inorganic chemical products and inorganic or organic compounds of precious metals, radioactive elements, rare earth metals or isotopes (S.O. 2801 to 2851).
13. Organic chemical products (S.O. 2901 to 2942).
14. Natural, synthetic and artificial rubber derived from oils, balata, gutta-percha, guayule, chicle and similar natural gums, mixtures of products of heading 4001 with products of heading 4002, in primary forms or in plates, sheets or strips (S.O. 4001, 4002).
15. Hair not combed or combed (S.O. 5101).
16. Silver (including gold-plated and platinum-plated silver), in unwrought or semi-wrought forms or in powder form. Platinum, palladium or rhodium, in unwrought or powdered form (S.O. 7106, 7110 11 00, 7110 21 00 and 7110 31 00).
17. Unrefined copper. Copper anodes for electrolytic cleaning. Refined copper and copper alloys in raw form. Copper parent alloys. ,Copper wires. (S.O. 7402, 7403, 7405 and 7408).
18. Nickel in raw form (S.O. 7502).
19. Aluminum in unwrought form (aluminium) (S.O. 7601).
20. Lead in raw form (S.O. 7801).
21. Zinc in unrefined form (S.O. 7901).
22. Tin in unrefined form (S.O. 8001).
23. Indium in raw form, waste, fragments, powders and other forms (S.O. E.X. 811291, 811299).
TWELFTH APPENDIX
(Article 18A)
DELIVERIES OF GOODS AND PROVISIONS OF SERVICES WHERE
SUBJECT TO THE REDUCED FACTOR
EIGHT HUNDRED (8%)
(1) Transportation of passengers and their accompanying luggage within the Republic by urban, interurban, and rural taxis as regulated by the applicable Road Transport Regulation Legislation and by tourist, excursion and interurban buses.
(2) Restaurant and catering services. Restaurant and catering services are services consisting of the delivery of prepared or unprepared food or beverages, or both, for human consumption, accompanied by adequate support services to enable their immediate consumption. The provision of food or drink or both is only one element of the whole in which services greatly excel. Restaurant services cover the provision of such services on the premises of the provider and catering services cover the provision of such services outside the premises of the provider.
The delivery of prepared or unprepared food or beverages or both, with or without transport, which is not accompanied by other support services, are not considered restaurant and catering services.
(3) Accommodation in hotels, tourist accommodation and similar places, including the provision of holiday accommodation.
(4) Maritime domestic carriage of passengers and their accompanying baggage.
(5) Provision of services and delivery of goods by nursing homes, which are not exempt transactions pursuant to point 7, Table A of the Seventh Schedule.
(6) S.S.: According to paragraph 3 of the C.D.P. 167/2026, Par. III(I), E.E. No. 6012, date 27.3.2026, this paragraph is amended by deleting it from 30.4.2026. Electricity supply falling under the Single Enrollment Household Tariff with code 01 and the Dual Enrollment Household Tariff with code 02, electricity supply falling under the special household tariff for certain categories of vulnerable consumers with code 08 and the thermal energy storage tariff with code 56 for consumers falling under Tariffs 01, 02 and 08 of the Cyprus Electricity Authority. [S.S.: The provisions of this paragraph concern electricity bills and apply to electricity metering that takes place from 1.4.2026 to 31.3.2027].
THIRTEENTH APPENDIX
PLACE OF PROVISION OF SERVICES: SPECIAL RULES
GENERAL EXCEPTIONS
Provision of services in relation to real estate
1.-(1) Provision of services to which this paragraph applies is deemed to be carried out in the country in which the immovable property to which the provision of the service relates is located.
(2) This paragraph applies to:
(a) the grant or assignment of a right over immovable property or a license to possess or use any other contractual right exercised over or in relation to immovable property,
(b) any construction, demolition, conversion, reconstruction, alteration, extension, repair or maintenance of a building or civil engineering work,
(c) the services provided by real estate agents, auctioneers, architects, experts or supervision companies or persons whose services are closely related to matters related to real estate and the provision of services for the preparation and coordination of construction works,
(d) provision of accommodation in the hotel sector and in sectors of a similar nature, such as holiday camps, sites designed for camping.
Provision of transport services
2.-(1) Provision of services consisting in the carriage of passengers or the carriage of any luggage or motor vehicle accompanying the passenger shall be deemed to be carried out in the country in which the carriage takes place.
(2) In the event that the transport is carried out in more than one country, the provision of services is considered to be carried out in each country separately according to the distances traveled in each country.
