(3) For the purposes of paragraph 2(1), any transport which takes place partly outside the territorial jurisdiction of a country shall be deemed to be wholly supplied in that country when–
(a) it is carried out as part of a journey between two points in that country whether or not it is part of a longer journey involving travel to or from another country; and
(b) the means of transport used, (except in an emergency), do not enter, land or enter another country in the course of a journey between those two points.
(4) For the purposes of subsection (1), a pleasure cruise is deemed to be the carriage of passengers, and the services provided or goods delivered as part of the pleasure cruise are deemed to be provided or delivered at the same place as the carriage of passengers is deemed to be provided.
This paragraph does not apply to access to events, when participation is virtual.
(5) For the purposes of subsection (3), “pleasure cruise” includes a cruise undertaken wholly or partly for educational purposes.
Rental of means of transport
3.-(1) Subject to sub-paragraphs (3) and (4), a provision of services consisting of the short-term rental of a means of transport is deemed to take place in the country in which the means of transport is actually made available to the customer.
(2) For the purposes of this Annex, a short-term lease of a means of transport means the continuous possession or use of a means of transport for a period not exceeding thirty days, while in relation to watercraft, the continuous possession or use for a period not exceeding ninety days.
(3) When-
(a) a supply of services consisting of the hiring of means of transport would otherwise be regarded as taking place in the Republic, and
(b) the services to any extent are actually used and exploited in a country that is not a member state,
services to that extent are deemed to be performed in that country.
(4) When-
(a) a supply of services consisting in the hiring of means of transport would otherwise be regarded as taking place in a country which is not a Member State, and
(b) the services to any extent are actually used and exploited in the Republic,
the services to that extent are considered to be performed in the Republic.
Access to cultural, artistic, sports, etc. events.
4.-(1) Provision of services to a relevant taxable person for which this paragraph applies is considered to be carried out in the place where these events actually take place.
(2) This paragraph applies to the following services:
(a) access to cultural, artistic, sporting, scientific, educational, entertainment or similar events, such as trade and other exhibitions, and
(b) ancillary services relating to access to these events.
Provision of restaurant and catering services
5.-(1) Provision of services to which this paragraph applies is considered to be carried out in the country in which these services are actually performed.
(2) This paragraph applies to restaurant and catering services, except for the provision of services for which paragraph 6 applies.
Provision of restaurant and catering services for consumption on ships, aircraft or trains
6.-(1) Place of provision of restaurant and catering services which are carried out on board ships, aircraft or trains during the part of a passenger transport carried out within the Member States, is the point of departure of the passenger transport.
(2) For the purposes of sub-paragraph (1), as:
(a) "part of the carriage of passengers carried out within the Member States" means the part of the carriage carried out, without an intermediate stop outside the Member States, between the point of departure and the point of arrival of the carriage of passengers;
(b) "point of departure of the carriage of passengers" means the first scheduled point of embarkation within the Member States, possibly after a stopover outside the Member States;
(c) "point of arrival of the carriage of passengers" means the last scheduled point of disembarkation, within the Member States, of passengers who have embarked within the Member States, possibly before a stopover outside the Member States.
(3) For the purposes of this paragraph, the return route, in the provision of a return transport service, is considered as independent transport.
Leasing of movable goods
7.-(1) When-
(a) a supply of services consisting in the leasing of movable property, excluding means of transport, would otherwise be deemed to be carried out in the Republic; and
(b) the services to any extent are actually used and exploited in a country which is not a member state,
(2) When-
(a) a supply of services consisting in the leasing of movable property, excluding means of transport, would otherwise be regarded as being carried out in a country which is not a Member State, and
Provision of telecommunication, radio and television services
8.-(1) This paragraph applies to the provision of services consisting of:
(a) telecommunications services, or
(b) television or radio services.
(2) For the purposes of this Annex, "telecommunications services" are the services related to the transmission, emission or reception of signals, texts, images or information of any kind, by wire, radio-electric, optical or other electromagnetic systems, including -
(a) the assignment or grant of the right to use facilities for such transmission, transmission or reception;
(b) providing access to global information networks.
(a) supply of services to which this paragraph applies, would otherwise be deemed to be carried out in the Republic, and
(4) When -
(a) a supply of services to which this paragraph applies would otherwise be regarded as being carried out in a country which is not a Member State, and
Transport of goods
9.-(1) When -
(a) a supply of services consisting in the transport of goods, other than the intra-Community transport of goods, to a relevant taxable person, would otherwise be regarded as taking place in the Republic, and
(2) When -
(a) a supply of services consisting in the transport of goods, other than the intra-Community transport of goods, to a relevant taxable person, would otherwise be regarded as taking place in a country which is not a Member State, and
EXCEPTIONS FOR TRANSACTIONS IN RELATED SUBJECTS
PERSONAL TAX
Provision of electronic services
10.-(1) When-
(a) a supply of services consisting of the supply of electronic services to a relevant taxable person, would otherwise be considered to be carried out in the Republic, and
(a) a supply of services consisting of the supply of electronic services to a relevant taxable person, would otherwise be considered to be carried out in a country which is not a Member State, and
(3) For the purposes of this Annex, the provision of electronic services includes -
(a) creation and hosting of websites and remote maintenance of programs and equipment;
(b) providing software and upgrading it;
(c) providing images, texts, information and making databases available;
(d) provision of music, films and games, including any form of gambling;
(e) provision of political, cultural, artistic, sporting, scientific or entertainment broadcasts or events;
(f) provision of distance learning:
It is understood that the communication by e-mail between the service provider and the service recipient alone is not sufficient for this service to be considered as a service provided electronically.
EXCEPTIONS FOR TRANSACTIONS WITH PERSONS WHO ARE NOT RELATED SUBJECTS
Provision of services by intermediaries
11. The country of supply of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person by an intermediary acting in the name and on behalf of a third party is the country in which the transaction in which the intermediary intervenes took place.
Transport of goods: in general
12.-(1) A supply of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person consisting of the transport of goods is deemed to be carried out in the country where the transport takes place, to the extent that it takes place in that country.
(2) Any transport which takes place partly outside the territorial jurisdiction of a country shall be deemed to be supplied wholly in that country when-
(a) it is undertaken in the course of travel between two points in that country whether or not as part of a longer journey involving travel to or from another country; and
(b) the means of transport used do not enter, land or enter another country in the course of the journey between the two points in question.
(3) This paragraph does not apply to the transport of goods starting from one Member State and ending in another Member State ("intra-Community transport of goods").
Intra-Community Transport of Goods
13.-(1) The provision of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person consisting of the intra-Community transport of goods shall be deemed to take place at the place of departure.
(2) For the purposes of this paragraph, as:
(a) "intra-Community transport of goods" means any transport of which the place of departure and the place of arrival are in the territories of two different Member States;
(b) "place of departure" means the place where the carriage of the goods actually begins, without taking into account the journeys made to the place where the goods are located;
(c) "place of arrival" means the place where the carriage of the goods actually ends.
Activities incidental to transportation
14.-(1) The provision of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person consisting of services incidental to the transport are considered to be carried out where these services are actually performed.
(2) "Services incidental to transport" are considered the services of loading, unloading and arrangement of transported items and similar activities.
Long-term leasing of means of transport
14A.-(1) Subject to subsections 3(2), (3) and (4) and subsection (2) below, a supply of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person (“the recipient”) consisting of the hire of a means of transport, other than a short-term hire, is the country of establishment or permanent residence or habitual residence of the recipient.
(2) Supply of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person ("the recipient") consisting of the hire of a pleasure craft, other than a short-term hire, is the country where the pleasure craft is actually placed at the disposal of the recipient, when the supplier actually provides that service from the business or some other permanent establishment of his enterprise situated in that country.
Cultural, artistic, educational and similar services
15A.-(1) Provision of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person to whom this paragraph applies, is considered to be carried out in the place where the events to which the provision of services relates actually take place.
However, when the services and ancillary services concern activities in continuous streaming or are available virtually in another way, the place of supply of the services is the place where the non-taxable person is established, has his permanent residence or has his usual residence.
(2) Subsection (1) applies to the following services:
(a) cultural, artistic, sporting, scientific, educational, entertainment or similar activities, such as trade and other exhibitions, and
(b) organizer services and ancillary services of the above activities.
Expertises and works concerning movable tangible goods
15. The provision of services to a person who is not a relevant taxable person consisting of expert opinions or work concerning movable tangible goods are considered to be carried out where these services are actually performed.
16. Provision of electronic services by a person belonging to a country that is not a Member State, as defined in paragraph 10(3) of this Annex to a person (“the recipient”) who –
(a) is not a relevant taxable person, and
(b) belongs to a Member State,
deemed to take place in the recipient's country of residence.
Provision of services to persons outside the member states
17.-(1) Provision of services for which this paragraph applies to a person ("the recipient") who-
(b) belongs to a country which is not a Member State,
(2) This paragraph applies to—
(a) assignment and grant of intellectual property rights, patent rights, licenses, trade marks and other similar rights;
(b) advertising services;
(c) provision of services by consultants, engineers, design offices, lawyers, accountants and other similar services, as well as data processing and provision of information; excluding any services related to immovable property.
(d) assuming an obligation not to exercise a professional activity, in whole or in part, or rights provided for in subparagraph (a) of this paragraph;
(e) banking, financial and insurance operations, including reinsurance operations, with the exception of the rental of safe deposit boxes;
(f) staff allocation;
(g) lease of tangible movable property, excluding means of transport;
(h) providing access to a natural gas system or a network connected to this system, the electricity system or heating or cooling networks, the transmission or distribution through these systems or networks and the provision of other services directly related to them;
Provision of telecommunication, radio and television and electronic services to non-taxable persons
18.-(1) The place of provision of the following services, to a person who is not a relevant taxable person, is the place of establishment or permanent residence or habitual residence of that person:
(a) telecommunications services within the meaning given to that term by subsection (2) of paragraph 8;
(b) radio and television services;
(c) electronically supplied services within the meaning given to that term by subsection (3) of paragraph 10:
It is understood that, when the provider and the recipient communicate via e-mail, it does not necessarily mean that it is a service provided electronically:
It is further provided that, when the provision of services to which this paragraph applies, would otherwise be considered to be performed in a country that is not a member state and the services to any extent are actually used and exploited in the Republic, the services to that extent are considered to be performed in the Republic.
(2) The provisions of subsection (1) do not apply in the event that the following conditions are met:
(a) The service provider is established or, in the absence of an establishment, has his place of residence or habitual residence in a single Member State;
(b) services are provided to non-taxable persons who are established, domiciled or habitually resident in any Member State other than the Member State referred to in subparagraph (a); and
(c) the total amount, without VAT, of the benefits provided for in subparagraph (b) does not exceed, during each calendar year, ten thousand euros (€10,000) and does not exceed this amount during the previous calendar year.
(3) In the event that during a calendar year the limit provided for in sub-sub-paragraph (c) of sub-paragraph (2) is exceeded, the provisions of sub-paragraph (1) shall apply from that point in time.
(4) A person who provides services provided for in subsection (1) and has his establishment in the Republic from which he provides services or, in the absence of an establishment, has his place of permanent residence or habitual residence in the Republic, has the right to choose the place of provision of these services, which in any case covers two (2) calendar years.
FOURTEENTH APPENDIX (Article 42G)
TAX HANDLING OF VOUCHERS
1. For the purposes of this Annex, the following definitions apply:
(a) "voucher" means an instrument for which there is an obligation to accept it as consideration or part of consideration for a delivery of goods or provision of services and for which the goods to be delivered or services to be provided or the identities of potential suppliers are indicated either on the instrument itself or on the relevant documentation, including the terms and conditions of use of that instrument;
(b) "specific purpose voucher" means a voucher, for which the place of delivery of the goods or provision of the services concerned and the VAT due on those goods or services are known at the time the voucher is issued;
(c) "multi-purpose voucher" means a voucher other than a single-purpose voucher.
2. (a) (i) Any transfer of a special purpose voucher made by a taxable person acting on his own behalf shall be deemed to be a supply of the goods or provision of services to which the voucher relates, and the actual delivery of the goods or the actual provision of services against a special purpose voucher which is accepted by the supplier as consideration or part of the consideration shall not be deemed to be an independent transaction.
(ii) In the event that a transfer of a specific purpose voucher is carried out by a taxable person acting on behalf of another taxable person, such transfer shall be deemed to be a delivery of the goods or services to which the voucher relates, carried out by the other taxable person on whose behalf the taxable person is acting.
(iii) In the event that the supplier of goods or services is not the taxable person who, acting on his own behalf, issued the voucher for a specific purpose, the said supplier is deemed to have supplied the goods or rendered the services to which the voucher relates to the said taxable person.
(b) (i) The actual delivery of goods or the actual provision of services against a multi-purpose voucher, accepted as consideration or part of consideration by the supplier, is subject to VAT in accordance with the provisions of Article 5, and the transfer of the voucher is not subject to VAT.
(ii) Where a multi-purpose voucher is transferred by a taxable person other than the taxable person carrying out the transaction subject to VAT under sub-paragraph (ii), any supply of services that can be specified, such as distribution services or promotional services, is subject to VAT.
3. Without prejudice to Article 14, the taxable value of the supply of goods or services made against a multi-purpose voucher is equal to the consideration paid for the voucher by the last purchaser or, in the absence of evidence of such consideration, to the monetary value stated on the multi-purpose voucher or related documentation, reduced by the amount of VAT attributable to goods delivered or services provided.
4. Paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 apply only to vouchers issued after 1h January 2019 and from there.
FIFTEENTH APPENDIX(Article 18B)
1. Delivery of books, newspapers and magazines provided either in physical form or electronically or in both formats (including brochures, prospectuses and similar printed material, children's illustrated books and tracing and coloring books, printed or handwritten musical scores, hydrographic maps or the like), excluding publications intended entirely or mainly for advertising purposes and publications consisting entirely or mainly of video content or audio music; production of publications of non-profit organizations and services related to the production of such.
The items in this paragraph are classified in Codes CN 49.01 to CN 49.05.
2. Talking books for people with disabilities of CN Code ex. 85.23.
3. Delivery of the following goods:
(a) Special lifting devices (stairs, lifts, hoists and the like), used to serve disabled persons of CN Code ex. 84.28.
(b) Armchair type carriages and other vehicles for disabled persons, whether or not with a motor or other propulsion mechanism of CN Code 87.13.
(c)(i) Orthopedic articles and appliances, including medical surgical belts, bandages and crutches.
(ii) Splints, braces and other articles and devices for fractures.
(iii) Prosthetic items and devices.
(iv) Hearing aids for the deaf and other devices that are hand-held, carried by persons, or inserted into the human body for the purpose of filling a deficiency or treating a disability. Parts and accessories of the above goods are excluded.
The items of this subparagraph are classified in CN Code ex. 90.21.
1. Street cleaning, waste collection and waste treatment services, excluding services provided by state authorities, local government authorities and organizations under public law.
2. Wastewater disposal and treatment and emptying of septic and industrial tanks.
3. Right of entry from the first performance of theatrical, musical and dance works or classical works.
